iṉit tāṉ yāṉ aḻaṉ puḻaṉ eṉṉum ṉakara īṟṟiṉum, ēḻeṉṉum ḻakara īṟṟiṉum oḻintaṉa, poṉṉiṉai poṉṉai, tāḻiṉai tāḻai eṉṟāṟpōla varuvaṉa piṟavumām. iṉi, īkāra īṟṟuḷ oḻintaṉa tīyiṉai tīyai, īyiṉai īyai, vīyiṉai vīyai eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa piṟavumām. aikāra īṟṟuḷ oḻintaṉa, tiṉaiyiṉai tiṉaiyai, kaḻaiyiṉai kaḻaiyai eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa piṟavumām. ēṉai īṟukaḷilum varuvaṉa uṇarntu koḷka.
mēlē peyarīṟṟuc ceykaiyellān tattam īṟṟiṉkaṇ muṭippārātaliṉ avai īṇṭuk kūṟal vēṇṭā.
iṉit ‘tēruṅkālai’ eṉṟataṉāṉē, urupukaḷ nilaimoḻiyāka niṉṟu tamporuḷoṭu puṇarumvaḻi vēṟupaṭum urupīṟṟuc ceykai yellām īṇṭu muṭittuk koḷka.
e-ṭu:	nampiyaik koṇarntāṉ, maṇṇiṉaik koṇarntāṉ, koṟṟaṉaik koṇarntāṉ eṉa mūvakaip poruḷoṭuṅ kūṭi niṉṟa urupiṟku oṟṟukkoṭukka. malaiyoṭu poru tatu, mattikaiyāṟ puṭaittāṉ, cāttaṟkuk koṭuttāṉ, ūrkkuc ceṉṟāṉ, kākkaiyiṟ karitu, kākkaiyatu pali, maṭiyuṭ paḻukkāy, taṭāviṉuṭ koṇṭāṉ eṉṉun toṭakkattaṉa urupu kāraṇamākap poruḷoṭu puṇarum vaḻi iyalpāyum īṟutirintum oṟṟumikkum vantaṉa koḷka.
iṉik, kaṇ kāl puṟam mutaliyaṉa peyarāyum urupāyum niṟku mātaliṉ, avai urupākak koḷḷumvaḻi vēṟupaṭuñ ceykaika ḷellām ivvilēcāṉ muṭikka.
iḵtu urupiyalātaliṉ ‘urupoṭu civaṇi’ eṉa vēṇṭā, ammikaiyāṉē urupupuṇarccikkaṭ ceṉṟa cāriyaikaḷellām īṟṟup potu muṭipu uḷvaḻip poruṭpuṇarccikkuṅ koḷka. viḷaviṉkōṭu, kiḷiyiṉkāl eṉa ellā īṟṟiṉuṅ koḷka. nampiyai, koṟṟaṉai eṉa uyirīṟum puḷḷiyīṟuñ cāriyai peṟātu iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum īṇṭē koḷka.	(30)
urupiyal muṟṟiṟṟu.

7
uyirmayaṅkiyal
akara īṟṟuppeyar vaṉkaṇam varuvaḻi
alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu
203.	akara iṟutip peyarnilai muṉṉar
	vēṟṟumai yalvaḻik kacatapat tōṉṟiṉ
	tattam otta oṟṟiṭai mikumē
eṉpatu cūttiram.
uyirīṟu niṉṟu vaṉkaṇattoṭuñ ciṟupāṉmai ēṉaik kaṇaṅkaḷoṭum mayaṅkip puṇarum iyalpu uṇarttiṉamaiyiṉ, ivvōttu ‘uyirmayaṅkiyal’ eṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. mēṟ peyarōṭu urupu puṇarumāṟu kūṟip peyarvaruvaḻi urupu tokku niṉṟa poruṭ puṇarcci kūṟukiṉṟamaiyiṉ urupiyalōṭu iyaipuṭait tāyiṟṟu.
iccūttiram, akara īṟṟuppeyar alvaḻikkaṇ vaṉkaṇattoṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) akara iṟutip peyarnilai muṉṉar - akaramākiya iṟutiyai yuṭaiya peyarccoṉ muṉṉar, vēṟṟumai yalvaḻik kacatapat tōṉṟiṉ - vēṟṟumaiyallāta viṭattuk kacatapa mutaṉmoḻikaḷ varumoḻiyāyt tōṉṟumāyiṉ, tattam otta oṟṟu iṭai mikum - tattamakkup poruntiya ak kacatapakkaḷākiya oṟṟu iṭaikkaṇ mikum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	viḷakkuṟitu, nuṇakkuṟitu, atakkuṟitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa oṭṭuka.
ivai aḵṟiṇai iyaṟpeyarākiya eḻuvāy viṉaikkuṟippup paṇpākiya payaṉilaiyoṭu muṭintaṉa.
‘otta’ eṉṟamaiyātu ‘tattam otta’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, akara īṟṟu uriccol valleḻuttu mikkum, melleḻuttu mikkum muṭiyum muṭipum, akarantaṉṉai uṇara niṉṟavaḻi vaṉkaṇattoṭu mikku muṭiyum muṭipum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	taṭakkai tavakkoṇṭāṉ ‘vayakkaḷiṟu’ (puṟam. 100) ‘vayappuli’ (akam. 22) kuḻakkaṉṟu eṉavum, taṭañcevi ‘kamañcūl’ (muruku. 7)  eṉavum, akkuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavum varum.
iṉi, iṭaiccol valloṟṟup peṟṟu varuvaṉa uḷavēl avaṟṟaiyum ivvilēciṉāṉ muṭittuk koḷka.	(1)
akara īṟṟuḷ valleḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉa
204.	viṉaiyeñcu kiḷaviyum uvamak kiḷaviyum
	eṉaveṉ eccamum cuṭṭiṉ iṟutiyum
	āṅka eṉṉum uraiyacaik kiḷaviyum
	ñāṅkark kiḷanta valleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu akara īṟṟu viṉaiccollum iṭaiccollum puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) viṉaiyeñcu kiḷaviyum - viṉaiyai oḻipākavuṭaiya akara īṟṟu viṉaiccollum, uvamak kiḷaviyum - uvamavurupāy niṉṟa akara īṟṟu iṭaiccollum, eṉaveṉ eccamum - eṉaveṉṉum vāypāṭ ṭāṉ niṉṟa akara īṟṟu iṭaiccollum, cuṭṭiṉ iṟutiyum - cuṭṭākiya akara īṟṟu iṭaiccollum, āṅka eṉṉum uraiyacaik kiḷaviyum - āṅka veṉṉum akara īṟṟu uraiyacai yiṭaiccollum, ñāṅkark kiḷanta valleḻuttu mikum - muṉṉark kūṟiya valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uṇa tāva cāva eṉa niṟuttik, koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa valleḻuttuk koṭuttu muṭikka.
ivviṉaiyeccam oḻintaṉa ellām iṭaiccol leṉṟu uṇarka. pulipōlak koṉṟāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tāviṉāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum, koḷḷeṉak koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum, akkoṟṟaṉ cāttaṉ tēvaṉ pūtaṉ eṉavum, āṅkak koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ, ‘āṅkak kuyilu mayiluṅ kāṭṭik, kēḷvaṉai viṭuttup pōki yōḷē’ eṉavum varum.
‘uvamam’ viṉaiyecca viṉaikkuṟippēṉum, oṉṟaṉoṭu poruvappaṭutal nōkki uvamaviyaliṉkaṇ āciriyar vēṟupaṭuttik kūṟiṉār.
‘eṉaveṉṉum eccamum’, irucollaiyum iyaivikkiṉṟa nilaimaiyāṉ iṭaiccollōttiṉuḷ vēṟōtiṉār.
‘āṅka’ eṉpatu, ēḻaṉurupiṉ poruḷpaṭa vantatallāmai ‘āṅka veṉṉu muraiyacai’ eṉṟataṉāṉum ‘āṅka vuraiyacai’ (co. 279) eṉṉum iṭaiyiyaṟ cūttirattāṉum uṇarka.
ivai iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ muṭiyum muṭipu ‘ñanamayava’ (eḻut. 144) eṉpuḻik kūṟiyatēyām. avai, tāva pulipōla koḷḷeṉa āṅka eṉa niṟutti, ñanamayava mutaliya moḻi ēṟpaṉa koṇarntu puṇarttu iyalpāmāṟu oṭṭik koḷka. cuṭṭu mēṟkūṟupa.	(2)
akaraccuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar meṉkaṇam muṭiyumāṟu
205.	cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar ñanamat tōṉṟiṉ
	oṭṭiya oṟṟiṭai mikutal vēṇṭum.
itu ‘ñanamayava’ (eḻut. 144) eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṉ meṉkaṇam iyalpākum eṉa muṟkūṟiyataṉai vilakki mikku muṭika eṉṟaliṉ,  eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakukkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar ñanamat tōṉṟiṉ - akaraccuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar ñanamakkaḷ mutalākiya moḻivariṉ, oṭṭiya oṟṟu iṭai mikutal vēṇṭum - tattamakkup poruntiṉa oṟṟu iṭaimikutalai virumpum āciriyaṉ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aññāṇ, annūl, ammaṇi eṉa varum.
‘oṭṭiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ aññeḷintatu, annaṉṟu, ammāṇṭatu eṉa akaran taṉṉai yuṇara niṉṟavaḻiyum mikutal koḷka.	(3)
ataṉ muṉṉar iṭaikkaṇam muṭiyumāṟu
206.	yavamuṉ variṉē vakaram oṟṟum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) yava muṉ variṉ - yakara vakara mutaṉmoḻi akarac cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉē variṉ, vakaram oṟṟum - iṭaikkaṇ vakaram oṟṟām e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	avyāḻ, avvaḷai eṉa varum.
varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, akaran taṉṉai yuṇara niṉṟa vaḻiyum vakaram mikutal koḷka. avvaḷaintatu eṉa varum.	(4)
uyirkkaṇam muṭiyumāṟu
207.	uyirmuṉ variṉum āyiyal tiriyātu.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) uyir muṉ variṉum āyiyal tiriyātu - uyirkaḷ akarac cuṭṭiṉ muṉ variṉum muṟkūṟiya vakaram mikku varum iyalpiṉ tiriyātu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	a eṉa niṉṟa cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar aṭai mutaliyaṉa varuvittu vakaram oṟṟit taṉṉuru iraṭṭi uyirēṟṟi, avvaṭai avvāṭai avvilai avvīyam avvural avvūrti avveḻu avvēṇi avvaiyam avvoḻukkam avvōṭai avvauviyam eṉa oṭṭuka.
‘neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar’ (eḻut. 160) eṉpataṉuḷ ‘neṟiyiyal’ eṉṟataṉāṉ iraṭṭutal kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ, atu nilaimoḻit toḻileṉpatu peṟappaṭṭatu.
varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, akaran taṉṉai uṇara niṉṟa vaḻiyum vakaram mikutal koḷka. avvaḻakitu eṉa varum. ‘tiriyātu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, mēṟ cuṭṭu nīṇṭavaḻi vakarakkēṭu koḷka.	(5)
ceyyuṭkaṇ akaraccuṭṭu nīṭal
208.	nīṭa varutal ceyyuḷuḷ urittē.
iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkic ceyyuṭku āvatoru viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) nīṭa varutal ceyyuḷuḷ urittu - akaraccuṭṭu nīṭavarutal ceyyuḷiṭattu urittu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	‘āyiru tiṇaiyi ṉicaikkumaṉa collē’ (col. 1), ‘āyirupāṟcol (col. 3) eṉa varum.
itu, varumoḻi varaiyātu kūṟaliṉ vaṉkaṇam oḻinta kaṇam ellāvaṟṟoṭuñ ceṉṟatu. avaṟṟiṟku utāraṇam vantavaḻik kāṇka. innīṭci irumoḻip puṇarccikkaṇ varutaliṉ ‘nīṭṭumvaḻi nīṭṭal’ (col. 403) ākāmai uṇarka.	(6)
‘cāva’, vaṉkaṇam varuvaḻi muṭiyumāṟu
209.	cāva eṉṉuñ ceyaveṉ eccattu
	iṟuti vakaraṅ keṭutalum urittē.
itumēl ‘viṉaiyeñcu kiḷavi’ eṉṟa eccattiṟku eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) cāva eṉṉuñ ceyaveṉ eccattu iṟuti vakaram - cāva veṉṟu collappaṭuñ ceyaveṉeccattu iṟutikkaṇ niṉṟa akaramum ataṉāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭa vakaramum, keṭutalum urittu - keṭṭu niṟṟalum keṭātu niṟṟalum uriya e-ṟu.
e -ṭu:	kōṭṭiṭaic cākkuttiṉāṉ eṉa varum. cīṟiṉāṉ takarttāṉ puṭaittāṉ eṉa oṭṭuka. keṭātatu muṉṉar muṭittām.
itaṉai ‘viṉaiyeñcu kiḷavi’ (eḻut. 204) eṉṟataṉpiṉ vaiyātata ṉāṉ, iyalpukaṇattum innilaimoḻikkēṭu koḷka. cāñāṉṟāṉ nīṇṭāṉ māṇṭāṉ yāttāṉ vīḻntāṉ aṭaintāṉ eṉa oṭṭuka.(7)
akara īṟṟuḷ iyalpāy muṭivaṉa
210.	aṉṉa veṉṉum uvamak kiḷaviyum
	aṇmai cuṭṭiya viḷinilaik kiḷaviyum
	ceymmaṉa veṉṉun toḻiliṟu collum
	ēval kaṇṇiya viyaṅkōṭ kiḷaviyum
	ceyta eṉṉum peyareñcu kiḷaviyum
	ceyyiya eṉṉum viṉaiyeñcu kiḷaviyum
	amma eṉṉum uraipporuṭ kiḷaviyum
	palavaṟ ṟiṟutip peyarkkoṭai uḷappaṭa
	aṉṟi aṉaittum iyalpeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu akara īṟṟuḷ orucār peyarkkum viṉaikkum iṭaikkum muṉṉeytiyatu vilakkiyum, eytātatu eytuvittum ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) aṉṉa eṉṉum uvamak kiḷaviyum - aṉṉa eṉṟu collap paṭum uvamavurupākiya akara īṟṟu iṭaiccollum, aṇmai cuṭṭiya viḷinilaik kiḷaviyum - aṇiyāraik karutiṉa viḷiyākiya nilaimai yiṉaiyuṭaiya akara īṟṟu uyartiṇaip peyarccollum, ceymmaṉa eṉṉun toḻiliṟu collum - ceymmaṉa eṉṟu collappaṭun toḻiṟcol poruḷ taruṅkāl um īṟṟāṉ iṟuñ collum, ēval kaṇṇiya viyaṅkōṭ kiḷaviyum - oruvarāṉ ēvaṟṟoḻiṉmai karutik kūṟappaṭṭa ēvaṟ poruṇmaiyai muṟṟa muṭittalai uṇarttum akara īṟṟu viṉaiccollum, ceyta eṉṉum peyareñcu kiḷaviyum - ceyta eṉṟu collappaṭum peyareccamākiya akara īṟṟu viṉaiccollum, ceyyiya eṉṉum viṉaiyeñcu kiḷaviyum - ceyyiya eṉṟu collap paṭum viṉaiyeccamākiya akara īṟṟu viṉaiccollum, amma eṉṉum uraipporuṭ kiḷaviyum - amma eṉṟu collappaṭum etirmukamākkiya akara īṟṟu iṭaiccollum, palavaṟṟu iṟutip peyarkkoṭai uḷappaṭa - paṉmaip poruḷai uṇarttum akara īṟṟup peyarkaḷ aintaṉaiyum muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟoṭu kūṭṭik koṭuttal uḷḷiṭṭu, aṉṟi aṉaittum iyalpeṉa moḻipa - av veṭṭuc collum iyalpāy muṭiyumeṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	poṉṉaṉṉa kutirai, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi eṉa, itu ‘viṉaiyeñcu kiḷaviyum uvamak kiḷaviyum’ (eḻut. 204) eṉpataṉāṉ mikku muṭitalai vilakkiṟṟu.
ūra kēḷ, cel, tā, pō eṉa ‘uyirīṟākiya vuyartiṇai’ (eḻut. 153) eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṉ iyalpāy muṭivatu īṇṭu ṉakaraṅ keṭṭu akara īṟāy viḷiyēṟṟu muṭintamaiyiṉ eytātatu eytuvittatu.
uṇmaṉa kutirai cennāy takar paṉṟi eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟku uṇṇu meṉa virittum, yāṉuṇmaṉa nīyuṇmaṉa avaṉuṇmaṉa eṉa niṟuttik, kūḻ cōṟu tēṉ pāl eṉa varuvittum muṭikka. ivaṟṟiṟkum avvāṟē virittuk koḷka. iṅṅaṉañ ceyyumeṉpataṉ eccap poruṭṭākiya maṉave ṉiṟuticcol akkālam vaḻaṅkiyatātaliṉ āciriyar ataṉaiyum vēṟāka eṭuttōtiṉār.
yāṉum niṉṉōṭuṭaṉ varuka, avaṉ celka, avaḷ celka, avar celka eṉa niṟuttik, kāṭṭiṉkaṇ ceruviṉkaṇ tāṉaikkaṇ pōriṉkaṇ eṉa varuvittu muṭikka. ivai ēvaṟ poruṇmaiyai muṟṟa muṭittaṉa.
‘ēval kaṇṇiya’ eṉavē ēval kaṇṇātaṉavum uḷavāyiṉa; avai nī celka atu celka avai celka eṉa niṟutti, muṟkūṟiya kāṭu mutaliya vaṟṟai varuvittu muṭikka. ivai ēvaṟ poruṇmaiyai muṟṟa muṭiyātaṉa. aḵṟiṇai ēvaṟ poruṇmaiyai muṟṟa muṭiyāmai, viṉaiyiyaluḷ (col. 228) viyaṅkōṭkaṇṇē poruḷiyaluñ ceyyuḷiyalum paṟṟik kūṟutum.
‘maṉavum’ ‘viyaṅkōḷum’ eytātatu eytuvittatu.
uṇṭa kutirai cennāy takar paṉṟi - ituvum atu. itaṟku uriya uṇṇāta kutirai yeṉṉum etirmaṟaiyum, nalla kutirai yeṉṉum kuṟippuṅ koḷka.
uṇṇiya koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ; itu muṉṉar viṉaiyeccam valleḻuttup peṟuka eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu.
amma koṟṟā cāttā tēvā pūtā eṉpatu iṭaiccollāta liṉ eytātatu eytuvittatu. itu kēḷāy koṟṟaṉē eṉa etirmuka mākkiyavāṟu kāṇka.
pallakutirai, palakutirai, cillakutirai, cilakutirai, uḷḷakutirai, illa kutirai, cennāy takar paṉṟi eṉa oṭṭuka. ikkālattup palla cilla eṉpaṉa vaḻaṅkā. ituvum viḷakkuṟitu eṉṟāṟ pōlap palakkutiraiyeṉa valleḻuttu eytiyataṉai vilakkiṟṟu.
viḷinilaik kiḷaviyākiya peyar muṟkūṟātataṉāṉē, ceyyumeṉ pataṉ maṟaiyākiya ceyyāta veṉpataṟkum ivviyalpu muṭipu koḷka. atu vārāta koṟṟaṉ eṉa varum. ivviyalpu muṭipiṟkuc ceymmaṉa ciṟattaliṉ, viyaṅkōṭku muṉ vaittār.
‘ēval kaṇṇiya’ eṉpataṉāṉ ēval kaṇṇātatum uḷateṉṟu kūṟi ‘maṉṉiya peruma nī’ (puṟam. 91) eṉa utāraṇaṅ kāṭṭukaveṉiṉ, atu poruntātu; kūṟukiṉṟāṉ avaṉ nilaipeṟṟiruttal vēṇṭumeṉṟē karutik kūṟutaliṉ atuvum ēval kaṇṇiṟṟēyām.
ellāvaṟṟiṉuñ ciṟanta ‘palavaṟṟiṟuti’ muṟkūṟukaveṉiṉ, atu vaḻakkiṟkuñ ceyyuṭkum vēṟu vēṟu muṭipuṭait teṉṟaṟkuñ, ceyyuṇ muṭipu ivviyalpupōṟ ciṟappiṉṟeṉṟaṟkum, akara īṟṟuḷ muṭipu kūṟātu niṉṟa muṟṟuviṉaiyum viṉaikkuṟippum ivviyalpu muṭipu peṟumeṉṟaṟkum piṉ vaittār.
uṇṭaṉa kutirai itu muṟṟuviṉai. kariyaṉa kutirai itu muṟṟu viṉaikkuṟippu.
iḵtu iyalpukaṇattu muṭipu. ‘ñanamayava’ (eḻut. 144) eṉpuḻip poruntuvaṉa vellāṅ koḷka.	(8)
‘vāḻiya’ iṟuti yakaram keṭutalum uṇmai
211.	vāḻiya eṉṉuñ ceyaveṉ kiḷavi
	iṟuti yakaraṅ keṭutalum urittē.
iḵtu eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) vāḻiya eṉṉuñ ceya eṉ kiḷavi - vāḻuṅkālam neṭuṅ kālamākuka eṉṉum poruḷait tarum vāḻiya veṉṟu collappaṭuñ ceyaveṉeccakkiḷavi, iṟuti yakaraṅ keṭutalum urittu - iṟutikkaṇ akaramum ataṉāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭa yakaravoṟṟuṅ keṭṭu muṭitalum urittu e-ṟu.
‘keṭutalum’ eṉavē, keṭātu muṭitalē perumpāṉmai eṉṟavāṟu.
e-ṭu:	vāḻi koṟṟā, cāttā, tēvā, pūtā eṉa varum.
vāḻiya eṉpatē perumpāṉmai. vāḻiya yāṉ nī avaṉ avaḷ avar atu avai eṉa itu mūṉṟiṭattuñ cēṟaliṉ ‘uyirīṟākiya muṉṉilaik kiḷaviyum’ (eḻut. 151) eṉpuḻi muṉṉilai yiyalpām eṉṟa taṉkaṇ aṭaṅkātāyiṟṟu. itu kuṟippu viyaṅkōḷ.
‘oṉṟeṉa muṭittalāṉ’ iḵtu iyalpukaṇattuṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	vāḻi ñeḷḷā eṉavarum.
itu vāḻttappaṭum poruḷ vāḻavēṇṭumeṉṉuṅ karuttiṉa ṉākak kūṟutaliṉ ēval kaṇṇiṟṟēyām.
allākkāl ‘vāḻttiyal vakaiyē nāṟpākku murittē’ (cey. 109) eṉpataṟkum, vāḻttiyalākac cāṉṟōr kūṟiya ceyyuḷkaḷukkum payaṉiṉṟāmeṉṟu uṇarka.	(9)
‘amma’ iṟuti akaram nīṭalum uṇmai
212.	uraipporuṭ kiḷavi nīṭṭamum varaiyār.
iḵtu amma veṉpataṟku eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) uraipporuṭ kiḷavi - etirmukamākkum poruḷai yuṭaiya ammaveṉṉuñ col, nīṭṭamum varaiyār - akaramāki niṟṟalēyaṉṟi ākāramāy nīṇṭu muṭitalaiyum nīkkār e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ammā koṟṟā, cāttā, tēvā, pūtā eṉa varum.
ummaiyāṉ, nīḷāmaiyē perumpāṉmaiyām. varaiyātu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ in nīṭci iyalpu kaṇattuṅ koḷka. ammāñeḷḷā, nākā, māṭā, vaṭukā, ātā eṉa oṭṭuka.	(10)
‘palāañ cilāam’ nilaivarumoḻikaḷ
213.	palavaṟ ṟiṟuti nīṭumoḻi uḷavē
	ceyyuḷ kaṇṇiya toṭarmoḻi yāṉa.
itu muṟkūṟiya palavaṟṟiṟutikkaṇ cilavaṟṟiṟkuc ceyyuṇ muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) palavaṟṟu iṟuti nīṭum moḻi uḷa - palavaṟṟai uṇarttum aivakaic colliṉ iṟuti akaram nīṇṭu muṭiyum moḻikaḷuñ cila uḷa; ceyyuḷ kaṇṇiya toṭarmoḻi āṉa - yāṇṭuḷaveṉiṟ ceyyu ḷātalaik karutiya oṉṟōṭoṉṟu toṭarccippaṭuñ ceyyuṇmuṭi puṭaiya moḻikaḷiṉkaṇ e-ṟu.
‘uṭaittu’ eṉṉātu ‘uḷa’ eṉṟa paṉmaiyāṉ, varumoḻikkaṇ cila eṉpatu vantu nīṭu meṉṟu koḷka.
‘ceyyuḷāṉa’ eṉṉātu ‘ceyyuḷ kaṇṇiya toṭarmoḻiyāṉa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, pala eṉpataṉ iṟuti akaram nīṇṭuḻi nilaimoḻi akarap pēṟum varumoḻi ñakaramākiya melleḻuttup pēṟum, varumoḻi yiṟuti nīṇṭavaḻi akarappēṟum makaramākiya melleḻuttup pēṟuṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	‘palāañ cilāa meṉmaṉār pulavar’.  itaṉ coṉ ṉilai pala cila eṉṉuñ cevveṇ.	(11)
pala, cila - tammuṉ tām vantu muṭiyumāṟu
214.	toṭaral iṟuti tammuṉ tāmvariṉ
	lakaram ṟakaravoṟ ṟākalu murittē.
itu pala cila eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟku iyalpēyaṉṟit tiripum uṇṭeṉa, eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) toṭar al iṟuti - toṭarmoḻi yallāta īreḻut torumoḻiyākiya pala cila eṉṉum akara īṟṟuc col, tammuṉ tāmvariṉ - tammuṉṉē tām varumāyiṉ, lakaram ṟakara voṟṟu ākalum urittu - tam īṟṟiṉ niṉṟa lakara voṟṟu ṟakaravoṟṟākat tirintu muṭitalum urittu e-ṟu.
ummaiyāṉ tiriyāmaiyum uritteṉṟār.
e-ṭu:	paṟpala koṇṭār, ciṟcila vitti eṉa varum.
akara īṟṟuc cuṭṭallāta kuṟṟeḻuttu ōreḻuttoru moḻiyātal iṉmaiyiṉ ‘toṭaraliṟuti’ yeṉavē īreḻuttoru moḻiyē uṇarttiṟṟu. ‘taṉmuṉ’ eṉṉātu ‘tammuṉ’ eṉṟa paṉmaiyāṟ pala cila eṉa niṉṟa iraṇṭun taḻuvappaṭṭaṉa.
‘tammuṉvariṉ’ eṉṉātu ‘tām’ eṉṟataṉāṟ palaviṉmuṉ pala varutaluñ cilaviṉ muṉ cila varutaluṅ koḷka. lakaram ṟakara voṟṟā meṉa oṟṟiṟkut tiripu kūṟi akaraṅ keṭutal kūṟiṟṟilareṉiṉ, atu vārātataṉāṉ vantatu muṭittaleṉṉum uttipeṟa vaittateṉṟu uṇarka. itaṉai ñāpakameṉpārum uḷar.
aruttāpattiyāṉ ‘tammuṉ tāmvariṉ’ lakaram ṟakara oṟṟām. eṉavē, tammuṉ piṟavariṉ lakaram ṟakaravoṟṟākātu akaram keṭumeṉṟu koḷḷappaṭum.
e-ṭu:	palkaṭal cēṉai tāṉai paṟai eṉavum, ‘palyāṉai’       (puṟam. 63) palvēḷvi eṉavum, cilkāṭu cēṉai tāṉai paṟai eṉavum, cilyāṉai cilvēḷvi eṉavum varum.
‘urittu’ eṉṟatu akara īṟṟorumai paṟṟi.	(12)
āṇṭu valleḻuttu uṟaḻntu tōṉṟal
215.	valleḻut tiyaṟkai uṟaḻat tōṉṟum.
itu muṟkūṟiya iraṇṭaṟkum uḷḷatoru muṭipu vēṟṟumai kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) valleḻuttu iyaṟkai - muṟkūṟiya pala cila veṉṉum iraṇṭaṟkum akara īṟṟup potuvitiyiṟ kūṟiya valleḻuttumikum iyalpu, uṟaḻattōṉṟum - mikutalum mikāmaiyumāy uṟaḻntuvarat tōṉṟum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	palappala palapala, cilaccila cilacila eṉa varum.
īṇṭun ‘tammuṉ tām’ varutal koḷka.
‘iyaṟkai’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, akaraṅ keṭa lakaram tirintun tiriyātum uṟaḻntu muṭitaluṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	paṟpala palpala, ciṟcila cilcila eṉavarum.
‘tōṉṟum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, akaraṅ keṭa lakaram melleḻuttum āytamumākat tirintu muṭitaluṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	‘paṉmīṉ vēṭṭattu’ (kuṟun. 123), paṉmalar, paḵṟāli, paḵṟāḻicai, ciṉṉūl, ciḵṟāḻicai eṉa varum.
ivai muṉṉart ‘tōṉṟum’ eṉṟu eṭuttōtiya mikaiyāṉ akaraṅ keṭa niṉṟa lakaravoṟṟiṉ muṭipākaliṉ ‘takaram varuvaḻi āytam’ (eḻut. 369) ‘melleḻuttu iyaiyiṉ’ (eḻu. 367) eṉpavaṟṟāṉ muṭiyā.		(13)
akara īṟu, vaṉkaṇam varuvaḻi vēṟṟumaikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu
216.	vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum ataṉō raṟṟē.
iḵtu akara īṟṟiṟku alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟi, vaṉkaṇattoṭu vēṟṟumai tokku niṉṟa muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum ataṉō  raṟṟu - akara īṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumaipporuṭ puṇarccikkaṇṇum muṟkūṟiya alvaḻiyōṭu oru taṉmaittāyk, kacatapa mutaṉ moḻi vantuḻit tattam otta voṟṟu iṭaimikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	iruviḷakkoṟṟaṉ, cāttaṉ, tēvaṉ, pūtaṉ eṉa varum. iruviḷakkuṟumai ciṟumai tīmai perumai eṉak kuṇa vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇuṅ koḷka.
‘iruviḷa’ eṉpatu ōlai; vēṇāṭṭakattu ōrūr; karuvūriṉakattu oru cēriyu meṉpa. iruviḷaviṟ koṟṟaṉ eṉa virikka.	(14)
ivvīṟṟu marappeyar melleḻuttu mikutal
217.	marappeyark kiḷavi melleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu akara īṟṟu marap peyarkku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) marap peyarkkiḷavi melleḻuttu mikum - akara īṟṟu marap peyarāya col vēṟṟumaip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ataṅkōṭu viḷaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.
itu ‘kacatapa mutaliya’ (eḻut. 143) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭiyum.	(15)
‘maka’ iṉcāriyai peṟutal
218.	makappeyark kiḷavikku iṉṉē cāriyai.
iḵtu akara īṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) makappeyark kiḷavikku iṉṉē cāriyai - akara īṟṟu maka eṉṉum peyarccolliṟku vēṟṟumaip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ varuñcāriyai iṉ cāriyai e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	makaviṉkai, cevi, talai, puṟam eṉa varum.
cāriyaippēṟu varaiyātu kūṟiyavaḻi nāṉku kaṇattukkaṇṇuñ cellumeṉpatu āciriyarkkuk karuttākaliṉ, makaviṉ ñāṇ, nūl, maṇi, yāḻ, vaṭṭu, aṭai eṉa oṭṭuka.
mēl ‘avaṇ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, iṉcāriyai peṟṟuḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka.	(16)
aḵtu attup peṟutalum uḷatātal
219.	attavaṇ variṉum varainilai iṉṟē.
iḵtu īṟṟu valleḻuttōṭu attu vakuttaliṉ, eytiyataṉ mēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṇ - muṟkūṟiya makaviṭattu, attu variṉum varainilai iṉṟu  - iṉṉēyaṉṟi attuc cāriyaiyum īṟṟu valleḻuttum vantu muṭiyiṉum nīkkum nilaimaiyiṉṟu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	makattuk kai cevi talai puṟam eṉa varum.
‘avaṇ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, makappālyāṭu eṉa valleḻuttuppēṟum, makaviṉkai eṉa mēl iṉcāriyai peṟṟuḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻvum, viḷaviṉkōṭu eṉa urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṭ ceṉṟuḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻvuṅ koḷka. ‘nilai’ yeṉṟataṉāṉ, makampālyāṭu eṉa melleḻuttup pēṟuṅ koḷka.	(17)
palavaṟṟiṟuti vaṟṟup peṟutal
220.	palavaṟ ṟiṟuti urupiyal nilaiyum.
iḵtu atikārattāṉ iyaipu valleḻuttoṭu vaṟṟum vakuttaliṉ, eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) palavaṟṟiṟuti - palla pala cila uḷḷa illa eṉṉum palavaṟṟai yuṇarttum akara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷiṉ iṟuti, urupiyal nilaiyum - urupiyaṟkaṇ vaṟṟup peṟṟup puṇarntāṟ pōla urupiṉatu poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇṇum vaṟṟup peṟṟup puṇarum e-ṟu.
iyaipu valleḻuttu atikārattāṟ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	pallavaṟṟukkōṭu palavaṟṟukkōṭu cilavaṟṟukkōṭu uḷḷavaṟṟukkōṭu illavaṟṟukkōṭu, cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa oṭṭuka.
urupu virintuḻi niṟkumāṟu pōlaṉṟi, avvurupu tokku ataṉ poruḷ niṉṟu puṇaruṅkāl vēṟupāṭu uṭaimaiyiṉ, avvēṟu pāṭukaḷ īṇṭu ōtiṉār ittuṇaiyumeṉṟu uṇarka.	(18)
ākāra īṟṟup peyar alvaḻikkaṇ (mikku) muṭiyumāṟu
221.	ākāra iṟuti akara iyaṟṟē.
iḵtu ākāra īṟṟup peyar alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ākāra iṟuti akara iyaṟṟu - ākāra īṟṟup peyar al vaḻikkaṇ akara īṟṟu alvaḻiyatu iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantuḻit tattam otta oṟṟu iṭaimikum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	mūṅkākkaṭitu, tārākkaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa oṭṭuka.	(19)
‘ceyyā’ vāypāṭṭu viṉaiyeccamum aṟṟātal
222.	ceyyā eṉṉum viṉaiyeñcu kiḷaviyum
	avviyal tiriyā teṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu ākāra īṟṟu viṉaiccoṉ muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ceyyā eṉṉum viṉaiyeñcu kiḷaviyum - ceyyā veṉṉum viṉaiyeccamākiya collum ummaiyāṟ peyarecca maṟaiyākiya collum, avviyal tiriyātu eṉmaṉār pulavar - valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum avviyalpu tiriyāteṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uṇṇākkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum, uṇṇākkoṟṟaṉ cāttaṉ tēvaṉ pūtaṉ eṉavum varum.	(20)
ummaittokaikkaṇ nilaimoḻi ākāra īṟu muṭiyumāṟu
223.	ummai eñciya irupeyart tokaimoḻi
	meymmai yāka akaram mikumē.
iḵtu ākāra īṟṟu alvaḻikkaṇ ummaittokai muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ummai eñciya irupeyart tokaimoḻi - ummai tokku niṉṟa irupeyarākiya tokaic coṟkaḷ; meymmaiyāka akaram mikum - meyyāka nilaimoḻi yīṟṟakaram mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uvāappatiṉāṉku, irāappakal eṉa varum.
‘meymmaiyāka’ eṉpataṉāṉ, valleḻuttuk koṭukka. iḵtu eḻuvāyum payaṉilaiyumaṉṟi ummait tokaiyātaliṉ māṭṭēṟṟāṉ valleḻuttup peṟātāyiṟṟu.
‘ummai tokka’ eṉṉātu ‘eñciya’ eṉṟa vāypāṭṭu vēṟṟumai yāṉ, arāappāmpu eṉap paṇputtokaikkum, irāakkoṭitu eṉa eḻuvāy muṭipiṟkum, irāakkākkai eṉap peyarecca maṟaikkum akarappēṟu koḷka.
varumoḻi varaiyātu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ, iyalpukaṇattukkaṇṇum akarappēṟu koḷka. iṟāavaḻutuṇaṅkāy eṉavarum. iḵtu ummait tokai. arāakkuṭṭi eṉpatu paṇputtokaiyum vēṟṟumait tokaiyumām. uvāap paṭṭiṉi eṉpatu vēṟṟumait tokai.	(21)
ākāra īṟṟuḷ iyalpāy muṭivaṉa
224.	āvum māvum viḷippeyark kiḷaviyum
	yāveṉ viṉāvum palavaṟ ṟiṟutiyum
	ēval kuṟitta uraiyacai miyāvum
	taṉtoḻil uraikkum viṉāviṉ kiḷaviyō(ṭu)
	aṉṟi aṉaittum iyalpeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkalum eytātatu eytuvittalum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) āvum - āveṉṉum peyarum, māvum - māveṉṉum peyarum, viḷippeyark kiḷaviyum - viḷittalaiyuṭaiya peyarākiya uyartiṇaic collum, yāveṉ viṉāvum - yāveṉṉum viṉāp peyarum, palavaṟṟu iṟutiyum - paṉmaip poruḷai uṇarttum ākāra īṟṟup peyarecca maṟaiyākiya muṟṟuviṉaiccollum, ēval kuṟitta uraiyacai miyāvum - muṉṉilai yēval viṉaiyaik karutivarum etirmukamākkuñ col liṉaic cērnta miyāveṉṉum ākāra īṟṟu iṭaiccollum, taṉ toḻil uraikkum viṉāviṉ kiḷaviyōṭu - taṉatu toḻiliṉaic collum ākāra īṟṟut taṉmaiyākiya viṉāc colloṭu kūṭa, aṉṟi yaṉaittum - avveḻuvakaiyākiya collum, iyalpeṉa moḻipa - iyalpāy muṭiyu meṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ākuṟitu mākuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu; kuṟiya ciṟiya tīya periya eṉa oṭṭuka.
ivai ākāra īṟṟup peyarākaliṉ, mikku muṭivaṉa mikāveṉa eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu.
ūrākēḷ cel tā pō eṉa iḵtu iyalpāmeṉṟa uyartiṇaip paṭarkkaip peyar tirintu muṉṉilaiyāy viḷiyēṟṟaliṉ eytātatu eytuvittatu.
yā kuṟiya ciṟiya tīya periya eṉa ituvum peyarākaliṉ, eytiya iyaipu valleḻuttu vilakkiyatām.
uṇṇā kutiraikaḷ cennāykaḷ takarkaḷ paṉṟikaḷ eṉa iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu; ‘ceyyā’ eṉṉuñ cūttirattup (eḻut. 222) peṟṟa valleḻuttiṉai vilakkaliṉ.
kēṇmiyā koṟṟā cāttā tēvā pūtā eṉavum, uṇkā koṟṟā cāttā tēvā pūtā eṉavum, ivviṭaic coṟkaḷ muṭiyāmaiyiṉ eytātatu eytuvittatu.
uṇkā eṉpatu, yāṉuṇpēṉō eṉṉum poruṭṭu. iyalpu kaṇattukkaṇṇāyiṉ ‘ña na ma ya va’ (eḻut. 144) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭipey tum.	(22)
ākāra īṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumaikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu
225.	vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum ataṉō raṟṟē.
iḵtu ākāra īṟu vēṟṟumaip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum - ākāra īṟṟup peyar al vaḻikkaṇṇē yaṉṟi vēṟṟumaipporuṭ puṇarccik kaṇṇum, ataṉōraṟṟu - akara īṟṟu alvaḻiyōṭu orutaṉmaittāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi avvalleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	tārā mūṅkā vaṅkā eṉa niṟuttik, kāl cevi talai puṟam eṉa varuvittu valleḻuttuk koṭuttu oṭṭuka.	(23)
ivvīṟṟuḷ akara eḻuttuppēṟu eyti muṭivaṉa
226.	kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉarum ōreḻuttu moḻikkum
	aṟiyat tōṉṟum akarak kiḷavi.
iḵtu avvīṟṟiṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu; akaramum valleḻuttoṭu peṟutaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉarum - kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ muṉṉiṉṟa ākāra īṟṟiṟkum, ōreḻuttu moḻikkum - ōreḻuttorumoḻiyākiya ākāra īṟṟiṟkum, akarakkiḷavi aṟiyat tōṉṟum - nilaimoḻikkaṇ akaramākiya eḻuttu viḷaṅkat tōṉṟum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	palāakkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉavum kāakkuṟai ceykai talai puṟam eṉavum varum.
‘ōreḻuttorumoḻi’ akarampeṟutal ciṟupāṉmaiyeṉṟaṟku ataṉaip piṟkūṟiṉār. itu nilaimoḻic ceykaiyātaliṟ palāa vilai palāanār eṉa iyalpukaṇattuṅ koḷka.
‘aṟiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ avvakaram īriṭattum poruntiṉavaḻik koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, aṇṇāattēri tiṭṭāattukkuḷam eṉa attuk koṭuttum, uvāattuñāṉṟu koṇṭāṉ eṉa attum ñāṉṟuṅ koṭuttum, uvāattāṟ koṇṭāṉ eṉa attum āṉuṅ koṭuttum, iṭāviṉuṭ (iṟaikūṭai) koṇṭāṉ eṉa iṉṉum ēḻuṉurupuṅ koṭuttuñ ceykai ceytu muṭikka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, mūṅkāviṉkāl mūṅkāviṉṟalai eṉa urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṭ ceṉṟuḻi iyaipuvalleḻuttuk kēṭuṅ koḷka.	(24)
‘irā’ vēṟṟumaikkaṇ akaram peṟāmai
227.	irāveṉ kiḷavikku akaram illai.
iḵtu ākāra īṟṟup peyarkku oruvaḻi eytiyatu vilakkukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) irāveṉ kiḷavikku  - irāveṉṉum ākāra īṟṟuc col liṟku, akaram illai - muṟkūṟiya akaram peṟutaliṉṟi valleḻuttup peṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	irākkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
irāakkākkai irāakkūttu eṉap peyarecca maṟaipporuḷ tārātu, irāviṭattukkākkai irāviṭattukkūttu eṉa vēṟṟumai karutiyavaḻi, irākkākkai irākkūttu eṉa akaram peṟāteṉṟu uṇarka.	(25)
‘nilā’ attuppeṟṟu muṭital
228.	nilāveṉ kiḷavi attoṭu civaṇum.
iḵtu akaram vilakki atikāra valleḻuttiṉōṭu attu vakuttaliṉ eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) nilāveṉ kiḷavi attoṭu civaṇum - nilāveṉṉuñ col attuccāriyaiyoṭu porunti muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nilāattukkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
nilaimoḻittoḻil nilaimoḻittoḻilai vilakkumātaliṉ, attu vakuppa akaram vīḻntatu. itaṟku ēḻaṉurupu virikka. nilāakkatir eṉpatu ‘vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum’ (eḻut. 225) eṉpataṉāṉ iyaipu valleḻuttup peṟṟatu. nilāamuṟṟam eṉpatu ‘oṭṭutaṟ koḻukiya vaḻakku’ aṉmaiyiṉ attup peṟātāyiṟṟu. īṇṭu varumoḻi varaiyātu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ, nilāattu ñāṉṟāṉ eṉa iyalpu kaṇattuk kaṇṇum ēṟpaṉa koḷka.	(26)
yā, piṭā, taḷā - ivai mūṉṟum muṭiyumāṟu
229.	yāmarak kiḷaviyum piṭāvun taḷāvum
	āmup peyarum melleḻuttu mikumē.
itu varumoḻi valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vakuttaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakukkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) yāmarak kiḷaviyum - yāveṉṉum marattai uṇara niṉṟa collum, piṭāvum- piṭāveṉṉuñ collum, taḷāvum - taḷāveṉ ṉuñ collum, ām muppeyarum melleḻuttu mikum - ākiya mūṉṟu peyarum valleḻuttu mikātu melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	yāaṅkōṭu, piṭāaṅkōṭu, taḷāaṅkōṭu, cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.
varumoḻit toḻilākiya melleḻuttu vakuppavē, valleḻuttu vilakkiṟṟām. itaṟku vilakkāmaiyiṉ akaram peṟṟatu.(27)
ivai valleḻuttuppēṟum eytutal
230.	valleḻuttu mikiṉum māṉa millai.
iḵtu eytiyatu ikantupaṭāmaṟ kāttatu, akarattoṭu melleḻuttēyaṉṟi valleḻuttum peṟumeṉṟaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) valleḻuttu mikiṉum māṉam illai - muṟkūṟiya mūṉṟu peyarkkum melleḻuttēyaṉṟi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyiṉuṅ kuṟṟamillai e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	yāakkōṭu, piṭāakkōṭu, taḷāakkōṭu, cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.
‘māṉamillai’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, immūṉṟaṟkum urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṭ ceṉṟuḻi, iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. yāviṉ kōṭu, piṭāviṉkōṭu, taḷāviṉkōṭu eṉa varum. cāriyai peṟavē akaram vīḻntatu. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, yāattukkōṭu, piṭāattuk kōṭu, taḷāattukkōṭu eṉa attup peṟutaluṅ koḷka. akaramum valleḻuttum peṟutaliṉ, ‘yāmarak kiḷavi’ (eḻut. 229) eṉpataṉaik ‘kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉarum’ (eḻut. 226) eṉpataṉpiṉ vaiyātavataṉāṉ, irāviṟkoṇṭāṉ nilāviṟ koṇṭāṉ eṉa urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṭ ceṉṟuḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka.	(28)
māmarak kiḷaviyum āvum māvum muṭiyumāṟu
231.	māmarak kiḷaviyum āvum māvum
	āmup peyarum avaṟṟō raṉṉa
	akaram valleḻut tavaiyavaṇ nilaiyā
	ṉakaram oṟṟum āvum māvum.
iḵtu eytiyatu vilakki eytātatu eytuvittatu. im mūṉṟum valleḻuttup peṟā eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu. māmarattukku akaramum ṅañanama oṟṟum, ēṉaiyavaṟṟiṟku ṉakara oṟṟum eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) māmarak kiḷaviyum āvummāvum ām muppeyarum avaṟṟōraṉṉa - māmaramākiya collum āveṉṉuñ collum māveṉṉuñ collumākiya mūṉṟu peyarum yāmaram mutaliya mūṉṟōṭum oru taṉmaiyavāy melleḻuttup peṟṟu muṭiyum, āvum māvum akaram avaṇ nilaiyā ṉakaram oṟṟum - avaṟṟuḷ āvum māvum puṇarcciyiṭattu akaram nilaipeṟāvāy ṉakara oṟṟup peṟṟu muṭiyum, eṉavē aruttāpattiyāṉ māmarattiṟku akaram nilaipeṟṟu ṅañanama oṟṟum peṟumāyiṟṟu, avai valleḻuttu avaṇ nilaiyā - ammūṉṟu peyarum muṟkūṟiya valleḻuttup puṇarcciyiṭattu nilaipeṟāvāy varum e-ṟu.
‘avaṇ nilaiyā’ eṉṟataṉai iraṇṭiṭattuṅ kūṭṭuka.
e-ṭu:	māaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū, āṉkōṭu māṉkōṭu cevi talai puṟam eṉa varum.
‘māmarak kiḷaviyum āvum māvum avaṟṟōraṉṉa’ eṉṟu ñāpakamākak kūṟiyavataṉāṉ, māṅkōṭeṉa akaramiṉṟiyum varum.
iṉi, ‘avaṇ’ eṉṟataṉāṟ kāyāṅkōṭu nuṇāṅkōṭu āṇaṅ kōṭu eṉṟāṟ pōlap piṟavum melleḻuttup peṟutalum, aṅkāk koṇṭāṉ iṅkākkoṇṭāṉ uṅkākkoṇṭāṉ eṅkāk koṇṭāṉ eṉa ivaṟṟuḷ ēḻām vēṟṟumai iṭapporuḷ uṇara niṉṟu iṭaiccoṟkaḷ valleḻuttup peṟutalum, āviṉkōṭu māviṉkōṭu eṉac ciṟupāṉmai iṉ peṟutalum, peṟṟuḻi valleḻuttu vīḻvuṅ koḷka.
māṭṭēṟṟāṉ mūṉṟu peyarum valleḻuttup peṟātu melleḻuttup peṟṟavāṟum, māmaram akaram peṟṟavāṟum ic cūttirattiṉ kaṇṇaḻivāṉ uṇarka.	(29)
mēlai vitiyāṉ muṭinta ‘āṉ’ akaramum peṟutal
232.	āṉoṟ ṟakaramoṭum nilaiyiṭaṉ uṭaittē.
iḵtu avaṟṟuḷ āṉ eṉṟataṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) āṉoṟṟu - āveṉṉuñ coṉ muṉṉarp peṟṟuniṉṟa ṉakaravoṟṟu, akaramoṭum nilaiyiṭaṉ uṭaittu - akarattoṭu kūṭi niṟkum iṭaṉum uṭaittu e-ṟu.
‘iṭaṉuṭaittu’ eṉṟavataṉāṉ, vaṉkaṇa moḻinta kaṇattatu immuṭipeṉak koḷka.
e-ṭu:	‘āṉaney teḷittu nāṉa nīvi’, ‘āṉamaṇi kaṟaṅkum kāṉat tāṅkaṇ’ eṉa varum.
‘akaramoṭum’ eṉṟa ummaiyāṉ, akaramiṉṟi varutalē perumpāṉmai. āṉey teḷittu, āṉmaṇi, āṉvāl eṉa varum.(30)
‘āṉ’ muṉ pakara īkāram muṭiyumāṟu
233.	āṉmuṉ varūum īkāra pakaran
	tāṉmikat tōṉṟik kuṟukalum urittē.
iḵtu āṉ eṉpataṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) āṉmuṉ varūum īkāra pakaram - āṉeṉṉuñ coṉ muṉṉar varumoḻiyāy varukiṉṟa īkārattoṭu kūṭiya pakaramākiya moḻi, tāṉ mikat tōṉṟi - ap pakaramākiya tāṉ mikku niṟpa nilaimoḻi ṉakarattiṟkuk kēṭu tōṉṟi, kuṟukalum urittu - īkāram ikaramākak kuṟuki niṟṟalum urittu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	āppi eṉa varum.
ummai etirmaṟai yākalāṉ, āṉpī eṉṟumām.	(31)
ivvīṟṟuṭ cila ceyyuṭkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu
234.	kuṟiyataṉ iṟutic ciṉaikeṭa ukaram
	aṟiya varutal ceyyuḷuḷ urittē.
iḵtu ākāra īṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkuc ceyyuṇ muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟiyataṉ iṟutic ciṉaikeṭa  -  kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ iṟutikkaṇ niṉṟa ākārattiṉatu iraṇṭu māttiraiyiṉ oru māttirai keṭṭu aḵtu akaramāy niṟpa, ukaram aṟiya varutal ceyyuḷuḷ urittu - āṇṭu ukaram pulappaṭa varutal ceyyuḷiṭattu urittu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	‘iṟavuppuṟat taṉṉa piṇarppaṭu taṭavumutaṟ
	 cuṟavukkōṭ ṭaṉṉa muḷḷilait tāḻai’	(naṟ. 19)
	‘puṟavuppuṟat taṉṉa puṉkā yukāy’	(kuṟun. 264).
eṉa varum. ‘ukaram’ vakuppavē nilaimoḻi akaraṅ keṭṭatu. atikāra valleḻuttu vilakkāmaiyiṉ niṉṟu muṭintatu.
iṉi, nilaimoḻittoḻil varaiyātu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ, iyalpu kaṇattiṟkum ivviti eytiṟṟākaliṉ, āṇṭuvarum ukaram pulappaṭa vārāmaiyum uṇarka. cuṟavuyarkoṭi, ‘aravuyarkoṭi’, muḻavuṟaḻtōḷ eṉa ivai kuṟiyataṉiṟutic ciṉaikeṭṭu, varumoḻi uyir mutaṉ moḻiyāy varutaliṉ vakara uṭampaṭumey peṟṟu, ukaram peṟātu muṭintaṉa. ivaṟṟiṟku iraṇṭā murupu virikka; mūṉṟāvatumām.	(32)
ikara īṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumaikkaṇ vali mikal
235.	ikara iṟutip peyarnilai muṉṉar
	vēṟṟumai yāyiṉ valleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu ikara īṟṟup peyarkku alvaḻi muṭipu tokaimarapiṟ kūṟi īṇṭu vēṟṟumai muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ikara iṟutip peyarnilai muṉṉar - ikara īṟṟup peyarccoṉ muṉṉar atikārattāṟ kacatapa mutaṉ moḻi vantuḻi, vēṟṟumaiyāyiṉ valleḻuttu mikum - vēṟṟumaip poruṭpuṇarcci yāyiṉ tamakkup poruntiṉa valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kiḷikkāl ciṟaku talai puṟam eṉa varum. puli nari eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉavum avai.
iṉi, kiḷikuṟumai kiḷikkuṟumai eṉak kuṇampaṟṟi vanta uṟaḻcci muṭipu mēl ‘valleḻuttu mikiṉum’ (eḻut. 246) eṉṉum cūttirattu ‘olvaḻi yaṟital’ eṉpataṉāṟ koḷka.	(33)
ivvīṟṟu iṭaiccoṟkaḷum, viṉaiyeccamum aṉṉavātal
236.	iṉiaṇi eṉṉuṅ kālaiyum iṭaṉum
	viṉaiyeñcu kiḷaviyum cuṭṭu maṉṉa.
iḵtu eytātatu eytuvittatu, ivvīṟṟu iṭaiccoṟkum viṉaiccoṟkum muṭipu kūṟutaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) iṉiaṇi eṉṉuṅ kālaiyum iṭaṉum - iṉiyeṉṟum aṇiyeṉṟuñ collappaṭukiṉṟa kālattaiyum iṭattaiyum uṇaraniṉṟa iṭaiccollum, viṉaiyeñcu kiḷaviyum - ivvīṟṟu viṉaiyeccamākiya collum, cuṭṭum  -  ivvīṟṟuc cuṭṭākiya iṭaic collum, aṉṉa - muṟkūṟiyavāṟē valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	iṉikkoṇṭāṉ aṇikkoṇṭāṉ tēṭik koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum, ik koṟṟaṉ cāttaṉ tēvaṉ pūtaṉ eṉavum varum.
ivviṭaic col mūṉṟum ippoḻutu koṇṭāṉ, aṇiya iṭattē koṇṭāṉ, ivviṭattuk koṟṟaṉ eṉa urupiṉ poruḷpaṭa vanta vēṟṟumaiyātaliṉ vēṟōti muṭittār.	(34)
‘iṉṟi’yatu ikaram ukaramāyt tiriyum ceyyuḷmuṭipu
237.	iṉṟi eṉṉum viṉaiyeñ ciṟuti
	niṉṟa ikaram ukara mātal
	toṉṟiyal maruṅkiṉ ceyyuḷuḷ urittē.
iḵtu ivvīṟṟu viṉaiyeccattuḷ oṉṟaṟkuc ceyyuṇmuṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) iṉṟi eṉṉum viṉaiyeñcu iṟuti niṉṟa ikaram ukaram ātal - iṉṟiyeṉṟu collappaṭum viṉaiyeccak kuṟippiṉ iṟutikkaṇ niṉṟa ikaram ukaramākat tirintu muṭital, toṉṟiyal maruṅkiṉ ceyyuḷuḷ urittu - paḻaka naṭanta kūṟṟaiyuṭaiya ceyyuḷuḷ urittu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	‘uppiṉṟu puṟkai yuṇkamā koṟkaiyōṉē’ eṉa varum.
‘niṉṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, viṉaiyeccattiṟku muṉ eytiya valleḻuttu vīḻkka. ‘toṉṟiyaṉ maruṅkiṉ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, aṉṟi eṉpatūuñ ceyyuḷil immuṭipu eytutal koḷka. ‘iṭaṉaṉṟu tuṟattal valli yōrē’, ‘vāḷaṉṟu piṭiyā vaṉkaṇāṭavar’, ‘nāḷaṉṟupōki’ (puṟam. 124) eṉa varum. muṟṟiyalikaran tirintu kuṟṟiyalukaramāy niṉṟatu.	(35)
ikaraccuṭṭiṉ iyalpu akaraccuṭṭu pōṟal
238.	cuṭṭi ṉiyaṟkai muṟkiḷan taṟṟē.
iḵtu ikara īṟṟuc cuṭṭup peyar iyalpu kaṇattoṭu muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutaliṉ eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭiṉ iyaṟkai - ikara īṟṟuc cuṭṭiṉ iyalpu, muṉ kiḷantaṟṟu - muṉ akara īṟṟuc cuṭṭiṟkuk kūṟiya taṉmaittām e-ṟu.
eṉṟatu, ‘cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar ñanamat tōṉṟiṉ’ (eḻut. 205 - 208) eṉpatu mutaliya nāṉku cūttirattāṉuṅ kūṟiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷai; avai meṉkaṇattu melleḻuttu mikutalum, iṭaikkaṇattum uyirkkaṇat tum vakaram peṟutaluñ, ceyyuṭ kaṇ vakaraṅ keṭṭuc cuṭṭu nīṭalumām.
e-ṭu:	iññāṇ nūl maṇi eṉavum, ivyāḻ ivvaṭṭu eṉavum, ivvaṭai ivvāṭai ivvilai ivvīyam ivvural ivvūrti ivveḻu ivvēṇi ivvaiyam ivvoṭu ivvōkkam ivvauviyam eṉavum, ‘īvayiṉāṉa’ eṉavum varum.
‘ītukāṇ ṭōṉṟumeñ ciṟunal lūrē’ eṉṟatum ‘kaḷvaṉō vallaṉ kaṇavaṉeṉ kāṟcilampu, koḷḷum vilaipporuṭṭāṟ koṉṟārē yītoṉṟu’ (cilap. ūrcūḻvari) eṉṟatum ituveṉṉuñ cuṭṭumutal ukara īṟātaliṉ, atu ceyyuḷakattatu; puṟaṉaṭaiyāṉ muṭiyumeṉa uṇarka.	(36)
‘tūṇi’ patakkoṭu muṭital
239.	patakkumuṉ variṉē tūṇik kiḷavi
	mutaṟkiḷan teṭutta vēṟṟumai yiyaṟṟē.
iḵtu ivvīṟṟu alvaḻikaḷuḷ aḷavup peyaruḷ oṉṟaṟkut tokaimarapiṉuḷ eytiya ēyeṉ cāriyai vilakki vēṟu muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) tūṇik kiḷavi muṉ patakku variṉ - tūṇiyākiya aḷavup peyariṉ muṉṉarp patakku eṉṉum aḷavuppeyar varumāyiṉ, mutaṟ kiḷantu eṭutta vēṟṟumai iyaṟṟu - muṉpu vitanteṭutta vēṟṟumai muṭipiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	tūṇippatakku eṉa varum. iḵtu ummait tokai.
varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ aṭaiyoṭu vantuḻiyum ivviti koḷka. irutūṇip patakku muttūṇip patakku eṉa oṭṭuka.
‘kiḷanteṭutta’ eṉṟataṉāṉ tūṇikkoḷ cāmai tōrai pāḷitam eṉap poruṭpeyar muṉ vantuḻiyum, irutūṇik koḷ eṉa atutāṉ aṭaiyaṭuttuḻiyun, tūṇittūṇi toṭittoṭi kāṇikkāṇi pūṇip pūṇi eṉat taṉ muṉṉart tāṉ vantuḻiyum ivviti koḷka.  iṉṉum itaṉāṉē taṉ muṉṉart tāṉ vantuḻiyum atutāṉ aṭai yaṭuttu vantuḻiyum ikkuc cāriyai peṟutaluṅkoḷka. tūṇikkut tūṇi irutūṇikkut tūṇi eṉavarum. ivaṟṟuṭ paṇput tokaiyum uḷa.(37)
‘nāḻi’ muṉ ‘uri’ vantu muṭiyumāṟu
240.	urivaru kālai nāḻik kiḷavi
	iṟuti ikaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭumē
	ṭakara moṟṟum āvayi ṉāṉa.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) urivarukālai - nāḻi muṉṉar uri varumoḻiyāy varuṅ kālattu, nāḻik kiḷavi - annāḻi eṉṉuñ col, iṟuti ikaram mey yoṭuṅ keṭum - taṉ iṟutiyiṉiṉṟa ikaran tāṉēṟiya meyyoṭuṅ keṭum, āvayiṉāṉ ṭakaram oṟṟum - avviṭattu ṭakaram oṟṟāy varum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nāṭuri eṉa varum. itaṉāṉ ēkāram vilakkuṇṭatu.
varumoḻi muṟkūṟiya vataṉāṉ, irunāṭuri munnāṭuri eṉavum oṭṭuka.
‘iṟuti yikaram’ eṉa muṉṉum ōr ikaram uḷḷatu pōlak kūṟiyavata ṉāṉ, īṇṭai nilaimoḻiyum varumoḻiyum nilai moḻikaḷāy niṉṟu piṟaporuṭpeyaroṭu valleḻuttu mikku muṭitaluṅ koḷka. nāḻikkāyam urikkāyam, cukku tōrai pāḷitam eṉavarum.	(38)
‘paṉi’ attum iṉṉum peṟṟumuṭital
241.	paṉiyeṉa varūuṅ kāla vēṟṟumaik(ku)
	attum iṉṉum cāriyai yākum.
iḵtu ikara īṟṟu vēṟṟumaiyuḷ oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttiṉoṭu cāriyai peṟumeṉa eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) paṉiyeṉa varūuṅ kāla vēṟṟumaikku - paṉiyeṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa nōyaṉṟik kālattai uṇara niṉṟa vēṟṟumai muṭipuṭaiya peyarkku, attum iṉṉum cāriyai ākum - attum iṉṉum cāriyaiyāka varum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	paṉiyattuk koṇṭāṉ, paṉiyiṟ koṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
‘vēṟṟumai’ yeṉṟataṉāṉ, iṉpeṟṟuḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka.	(39)
‘vaḷi’ eṉum peyarum aṟṟātal
242.	vaḷiyeṉa varūum pūtak kiḷaviyum
	avviyal nilaiyal cevvi teṉpa.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) vaḷiyeṉa varūum pūtak kiḷaviyum - vaḷiyeṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa iṭakkarallāta aimperum pūtaṅkaḷiṉ oṉṟai uṇara niṉṟa collum, avviyal nilaiyal cevvi teṉpa - muṉṉark kūṟiya attum iṉṉum peṟum avviyalpiṉkaṇ niṟṟal cevviteṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	vaḷiyattukkoṇṭāṉ, vaḷiyiṟ koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
‘cevvitu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, iṉ peṟṟuḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka.	(40)
‘uti’ marappeyar meli mikal
243.	utimarak kiḷavi melleḻuttu mikumē.
itu marappeyariṉ oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vitikkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) utimarak kiḷavi - utitta leṉṉun toḻilaṉṟi uti eṉṉum marattiṉai uṇara niṉṟa col, melleḻuttu mikum - valleḻuttu mikātu melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	utiṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.
ammuccāriyai vitikkiṉṟa puḷimarattiṉai itaṉpiṉ vaittamaiyāṉ, utiyaṅkōṭu eṉa itaṟkum ammuppeṟutal koḷka. iḵtu ikkālattu otiyeṉa maruviṟṟu.	(41)
‘puḷi’ marappeyar ammuccāriyai peṟutal
244.	puḷimarak kiḷavikku ammē cāriyai.
itu valleḻuttu vilakki ammu vakuttaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) puḷimarak kiḷavikku ammē cāriyai - cuvaiyaṉṟip puḷiyeṉṉum marattiṉai uṇara niṉṟa colliṟku ammeṉṉuñ cāriyai varum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	puḷiyaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.
cāriyaip pēṟṟiṭai muṉṉarc cūttirattu eḻuttuppēṟu kūṟiyavataṉāṉ, ammuppeṟṟuḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka.	(42)
cuvaippuḷi melleḻuttu mikku muṭital
245.	ēṉaip puḷippeyar melleḻuttu mikumē.
itu valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vitittaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) ēṉaip puḷippeyar melleḻuttu mikum - am marap peyaraṉṟic cuvaippuḷi uṇara niṉṟa peyar valleḻuttu mikātu melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	puḷiṅkūḻ cāṟu tayir pāḷitam eṉa varum.
pāḷitam - pāṟcōṟu. ivaṟṟiṟku iraṇṭāmurupu virikka.	(43)
appeyar valimikkum muṭital
246.	valleḻuttu mikiṉum māṉa millai
	olvaḻi yaṟital vaḻakkat tāṉa.
iḵtu eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) valleḻuttu mikiṉum māṉamillai - cuvaippuḷi melleḻuttē yaṉṟi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyiṉum kuṟṟamillai, olvaḻi aṟital vaḻakkattu āṉa - poruntum iṭam aṟika vaḻakkiṭattu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	puḷikkūḻ cāṟu tayir pāḷitam eṉa varum.
‘olvaḻi’ eṉṟataṉāṟ, puḷiccāṟu pōla ēṉaiya vaḻakkup payiṟci ilaveṉṟu koḷka.
‘vaḻakkattāṉa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ivvīṟṟukkaṇ eṭuttōttum ilēcumiṉṟi varuvaṉa ellāvaṟṟiṟkum ēṟkumāṟu ceykaiyaṟintu muṭittukkoḷka. avai, iṉṉiṉik koṇṭāṉ aṇṇaṇik koṇṭāṉ eṉpaṉa aṭaiyaṭuttaliṉ ‘iṉiyaṇi’ (eḻut. 236) eṉṟavaḻi muṭiyāvāy valleḻuttup peṟṟaṉa.
kappitantai ceṉṉitantai eṉpaṉa, ‘aḵṟiṇai viravup peyar’ (eḻut. 155) eṉpataṉāṉ iyalpeytātu, īṇṭu varumoḻit takara akaraṅ keṭṭuk kappintai ceṉṉintai eṉa muṭintaṉa.
kūtāḷi kaṇaviri eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟku ammuk koṭuttu ikaraṅ keṭuttuk kūtāḷaṅkōṭu kaṇaviraṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa muṭikka. ‘kūtaḷa naṟumpū’ eṉak kuṟaintum varum.
iṉi, ivai makara īṟāyum vaḻaṅkum. atu ‘veṇkū tāḷattut taṇpūṅ kōtaiyar’ (paṭṭi. 85) eṉa attup peṟṟu makaraṅ keṭṭum, ‘kaṇavira mālai yiṭūuk kaḻintaṉṉa’ (akam. 31) eṉa makaraṅ keṭṭum, kaṇaviraṅkōṭu eṉa melleḻuttup peṟṟum niṟkum.
kaṭṭi eṉa niṟutti, iṭi akal eṉat tantu ṭakarattiṉ ikaraṅ keṭuttuk kaṭṭiṭi kaṭṭakal eṉa muṭikka. paruttikkuc ceṉṟāṉeṉa iyaipu valleḻuttum ikkuṅ koṭuttu muṭikka. tuḷiyattuk koṇṭāṉ tuḷiyiṟ koṇṭāṉ eṉa attum iṉṉum koṭuttu muṭikka. ‘puḷiṅkāy vēṭkait taṉṟu’ (aiṅkuṟu. 51) eṉavum, puḷimpaḻam eṉavum ammuppeṟātu melleḻuttup peṟṟu muṭitaluṅ koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṭ ceṉṟuḻi iyaipu valleḻuttuk keṭuttuk kiḷiyiṉ kāl, puḷiyiṉ kōṭu, utiyiṉ kōṭu eṉa muṭikka.	(44)
ivvīṟṟu nāṭpeyar āṉ cāriyai peṟutal
247.	nāḷmuṉ tōṉṟun toḻilnilaik kiḷavikku
	āṉiṭai varutal aiya miṉṟē.
iḵtu iyaipu valleḻuttu vilakki, āṉ cāriyai vitikkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) nāḷ muṉ tōṉṟun toḻil nilaik kiḷavikku  - ikara īṟṟu nāṭpeyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟun toḻiṟcoṟku, āṉ iṭai varutal aiyamiṉṟu - āṉ cāriyai iṭai vantu muṭital aiyamiṉṟu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	paraṇiyāṟ koṇṭāṉ, cōtiyāṟkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
‘aiyamiṉṟu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. itaṟkuk kaṇṇeṉ urupu virikka.	(45)
tiṅkaṭpeyar ikkuc cāriyai peṟutal
248.	tiṅkaḷ muṉvariṉ ikkē cāriyai.
iḵtu iyaipu valleḻuttiṉōṭu ikku vakuttaliṉ, eytiyataṉ mēṟ ciṟappu viti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) tiṅkaḷ muṉvariṉ ikkē cāriyai - tiṅkaḷai uṇara niṉṟa ikara īṟṟup peyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉart toḻiṉilaik kiḷavi variṉ varuñ cāriyai ikkuc cāriyaiyām e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	āṭikkukkoṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ  tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa iyaipu valleḻuttuk koṭuttu muṭikka. itaṟkum kaṇṇeṉ urupu virikka.	(46)
īkāra īṟṟup peyar alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu
249.	īkāra iṟuti ākāra iyaṟṟē.
iḵtu īkāra īṟṟup peyar alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) īkāra iṟuti ākāra iyaṟṟu - īkāra īṟṟup peyar al vaḻikkaṇ ākāra īṟṟu alvaḻiyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi avvalleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	īkkaṭitu, tīkkaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum.	(47)
ivvīṟṟuppeyaruḷ iyalpāy muṭivaṉa
250.	nīeṉ peyarum iṭakkarp peyarum
	mīeṉa marīiya iṭamvarai kiḷaviyum
	āvayiṉ valleḻut tiyaṟkai yākum.
iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkalum, eytātatu eytuvittaluṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) nī eṉ peyarum iṭakkarppeyarum - nī yeṉṉum peyarum iṭakkarp peyarākiya īkāra īṟṟup peyarum, mī eṉa marī iya iṭamvarai kiḷaviyum - mī eṉṟu colla maruvāy vaḻaṅkiṉa ōriṭattai varaintu uṇarttuñ collum, āvayiṉ valleḻuttu iyaṟkaiyākum - puṇaru miṭattu muṟkūṟiya valleḻuttup peṟātu iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nī kuṟiyai ciṟiyai tīyai periyai eṉavum, pīkuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavum ivaiyiṟṟukkup potuvāṉ eytiya valleḻuttu vilakkuṇṭaṉa. mīkaṇ cevi talai puṟam; iḵtu ilakkaṇattoṭu poruntiya maruvātaliṉ eytātatu eytuvittatu.
nī eṉpatu ‘aḵṟiṇai viravuppeyaruḷ’ (eḻut. 155) aṭaṅkātō veṉiṉ, mēl niṉkai yeṉat tirintu muṭitaliṉ aṭaṅkātāyiṟṟu. mīkaṇ eṉpatu mēliṭattuk kaṇṇeṉa vēṟṟumai eṉiṉum, iyalpu paṟṟi uṭaṉ kūṟiṉār.	(48)
avaṟṟuḷ, ‘mī’ valleḻuttu mikutalum peṟutal
251.	iṭamvarai kiḷavimuṉ valleḻuttu mikūum
	uṭaṉilai moḻiyum uḷaveṉa moḻipa.
itu valleḻuttu mikuka eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu ikantu paṭāmaṟ kāttatu.
(i-ḷ.) iṭam varai kiḷavimuṉ valleḻuttu mikūum - iṭattai varaintu uṇarttum mīyeṉṉuñ colliṉ muṉṉar iyalpāy muṭitalē yaṉṟi valleḻuttu mikkum muṭiyum, uṭaṉilai moḻiyum uḷa eṉa moḻipa - tammil ōcaiyiyaintu niṟṟalaiyuṭaiya moḻikaḷum uḷaveṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	mīkkōḷ, mīppal eṉa varum.
‘uṭaṉilai’ eṉṟataṉāṉ mīṅkuḻi mīntōl eṉa melleḻuttup peṟṟu muṭivaṉavuṅ koḷka.	(49)
ivvīṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumaikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu
252.	vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum ataṉō raṟṟē.
iḵtu īkāra īṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumaikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum ataṉōraṟṟu - īkāra īṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumaipporuṭ puṇarccik kaṇṇum ākāra īṟṟu alvaḻipōla valleḻuttu vantuḻi avvalleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	īkkāl ciṟaku talai puṟam, tīkkaṭumai ciṟumai tīmai perumai eṉa varum.	(50)
‘nī’ niṉ ātal
253.	nīeṉ orupeyar urupiyal nilaiyum
	āvayiṉ valleḻut tiyaṟkai yākum.
iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu; valleḻuttu vilakki ṉakaravoṟṟē peṟuka eṉṟaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) nī eṉ oru peyar urupiyal nilaiyum - nīyeṉṉum ōreḻuttorumoḻi urupu puṇarccikkaṇ neṭumutal kuṟuki ṉakaram oṟṟi niṉṟāṟpōla īṇṭup poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum muṭiyum, āvayiṉ valleḻuttu iyaṟkaiyākum - avvāṟu muṭipuḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu mikātu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	niṉkai cevi talai puṟam eṉa varum.
iḵtu īkāra īṟu ikara īṟāy, ikara īṟu ṉakara īṟāy niṉṟuḻiyum ‘nīyeṉorupeyar’ eṉṟaliṉ tirintataṉ tiripatuvēyāyiṟṟu. ‘iyaṟkaiyākum’ eṉavē nilaimoḻittoḻil atikāra valleḻuttai vilakkātāyiṟṟu.	(51)
ukara īṟṟup peyar alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu
254.	ukara iṟuti akara iyaṟṟē.
iḵtu ukara īṟṟuppeyar alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ukara iṟuti akara iyaṟṟu - ukara īṟṟup peyar al vaḻikkaṇ akara īṟṟu iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi avval leḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kaṭukkuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum.	(52)
ukaraccuṭṭum vaṉkaṇam varuvaḻi valimikutal
255.	cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉarum attoḻiṟ ṟākum.
iḵtu ukara īṟṟuc cuṭṭu vaṉkaṇattoṭu muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉarum attoḻiṟṟu ākum - ukara īṟṟuc cuṭ ṭiṉ muṉṉum valleḻuttu vantuḻi avvalleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ukkoṟṟaṉ cāttaṉ tēvaṉ pūtaṉ eṉa varum.	(53)
piṟakaṇam variṉ, akaraccuṭṭiṉ iyalpiṟṟātal
256.	ēṉavai variṉē mēlnilai iyala.
iḵtu ukara īṟṟuc cuṭṭu oḻinta kaṇaṅkaḷoṭu muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ēṉavai variṉ - ukara īṟṟuc cuṭṭiṉmuṉ valleḻut tallāta meṉkaṇam mutaliya mūṉṟum variṉ, mēlnilai iyala - akara īṟṟuc cuṭṭu muṭintāṟpōla ñanamat tōṉṟiṉ oṟṟumikkum yavavariṉum uyirvariṉum vakaram oṟṟiyuñ ceyyuḷil nīṇṭum muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uññāṇ nūl maṇi eṉavum, uvyāḻ uvvaṭṭu eṉavum, uvvaṭai uvvāṭai eṉavum, ūvayiṉāṉa eṉavum varum.	(54)
cuṭṭumutal ukaram iyalpāka muṭital
257.	cuṭṭumutal iṟuti iyalpā kummē.
iḵtu ivvīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku valleḻuttu vilakki iyalpu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu mutal iṟuti - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉai mutalākavuṭaiya ukara īṟṟup peyar, iyalpākum - muṟkūṟiya valleḻuttu mikātu iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	atu kuṟitu, itu kuṟitu, utu kuṟitu, ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavarum.
muṟkūṟiyavai cuṭṭumāttirai, ivai cuṭṭuppeyarāka uṇarka.(55)
ataṉmuṉṉar ‘aṉṟu’ varuvaḻiyum,
‘ai’ varuvaḻiyum ceyyuḷmuṭipu
258.	aṉṟuvaru kālai āvā kutalum
	aivaru kālai meyvaraintu keṭutalum
	ceyyuḷ maruṅkiṉ uritteṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu ivvīṟṟuc cuṭṭumutaṟpeyarkku oru ceyyuṇ muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) aṉṟu varukālai ā ākutalum - atikārattāṉ niṉṟa cuṭṭumutal ukara īṟṟiṉ muṉṉar aṉṟeṉṉum viṉaikkuṟippuc col varuṅkālattu attakaravoṟṟiṉmēl ēṟi niṉṟa ukaram ākāramāyt tirintu muṭitalum, aivarukālai meyvaraintu keṭutalum - ataṉ muṉṉar aiyeṉṉuñ cāriyai varuṅkālattu attakaravoṟṟu niṟka ataṉ mēl ēṟiya ukaraṅ keṭutalum, ceyyuṇ maruṅkiṉ uritteṉamoḻipa - ceyyuṭkaṇ uritteṉṟu colluvar āciriyar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	atāaṉṟamma, itāaṉṟamma, utāaṉṟamma, ‘atā aṉṟeṉpa veṇpā yāppē’ (cey. 82) eṉavum, atai maṟṟamma, itaimaṟṟamma, utaimaṟṟamma eṉavum varum.
‘moḻinta poruḷōṭoṉṟa avvayiṉ moḻiyātataṉai muṭṭiṉṟi muṭittal’ eṉpataṉāṉ, ataṉṟu itaṉṟu utaṉṟu eṉa ukaraṅ keṭṭut takaravoṟṟu niṟṟal koḷka.	(56)
ukara īṟṟup peyaratu vēṟṟumaimuṭipu
259.	vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum ataṉō raṟṟē.
iḵtu ivvīṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumaikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum - ukara īṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumaip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇṇum, ataṉōraṟṟu - akara īṟṟu alvaḻiyōṭu orutaṉmaittāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi avvalleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kaṭukkāy cetiḷ tōl pū eṉavum, kaṭukkaṭumai eṉavum varum.	(57)
eru, ceru - eṉpavaṟṟiṉ muṭipu
260.	eruvum ceruvum ammoṭu civaṇit
	tiripiṭa ṉuṭaiya teriyuṅ kālai
	ammiṉ makaram ceruvayiṉ keṭumē
	tammoṟṟu mikūum valleḻut tiyaṟkai.
iḵtu avvīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttu vilakkic cāriyai vitiyum, oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttiṉoṭu cāriyai vitiyum, cāriyai peṟātavaḻi valleḻuttu melleḻuttup pēṟum kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) eruvum ceruvum ammoṭu civaṇi - eruveṉṉuñ collum ceruveṉṉuñ collum ammuccāriyaiyoṭu porunti, tiripu iṭaṉuṭaiya teriyuṅ kālai - atikāra valleḻuttup peṟāmal tiriyum iṭaṉuṭaiya ārāyuṅkālattu; ammiṉ makarañ ceruvayiṟ keṭum - āṇṭu ammuccāriyaiyiṉatu īṟṟiṉ makarañ ceruveṉṉuñ colliṭattuk keṭṭu muṭiyum; valleḻuttu mikūum - āṇṭu ceruviṉkaṇ valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum; iyaṟkait tam oṟṟu mikūum - ammup peṟātavaḻi iraṇṭaṟkun tamakku iyaṟkaiyākiya valloṟṟum melloṟṟum mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	eruveṉa niṟuttik, kuḻi cēṟu tātu pūḻi eṉat tantu, ammukkoṭuttu ‘ammiṉiṟuti kacatakkālai’ (eḻut. 129) eṉpataṉāṉ, eruvaṅkuḻi cēṟu tātu pūḻi eṉa muṭikka. ceruveṉa niṟuttik kaḷam cēṉai tāṉai paṟai eṉat tantu iṭai ammukkoṭuttu makaraṅ keṭuttu valleḻuttuk koṭuttuc ceruvakkaḷam cēṉaitāṉai paṟaiyeṉa muṭikka.
iṉi ammup peṟātavaḻi erukkuḻi eruṅkuḻi eṉa valleḻuttum melleḻuttuṅ koṭuttu muṭikka. iṉic ceruviṟku ēṟpuḻik kōṭal eṉpataṉāṉcerukkaḷameṉa valleḻuttē koṭuttu muṭikka.
‘teriyuṅ kālai’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, eruviṉkuṟumai ceruviṉ kaṭumai eṉa urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṭ ceṉṟuḻi valleḻuttu vīḻtalum, eruvañāṟci ceruvañāṟci eṉa iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ ammup peṟutalum koḷka. makaram, ‘meṉmaiyu miṭaimaiyum’ (eḻut. 130) eṉpataṉāṟ keṭukka.
‘tammoṟṟu mikūum’ eṉa uṭampoṭu puṇarttuc cūttirañ ceytaliṉ, ukaram nīṭavarutaluṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	varūum, tarūum, paṭūum eṉa varum.	(58)
ḻakara ukara īṟṟuccol muṭiyumāṟu
261.	ḻakara ukaram nīṭiṭaṉ uṭaittē
	ukaram varutal āvayi ṉāṉa.
iḵtu eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti vakuttatu; valleḻuttiṉōṭu ukaram peṟutaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) ḻakara ukaram nīṭiṭaṉ uṭaittu - ukara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷuḷ ḻakarattoṭu kūṭiya ukara īṟṟuccol nīṇṭu muṭiyum iṭaṉuṭaittu; āvayiṉāṉ ukaram varutal - avviṭattu ukaram vantu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	eḻūukkatavu ciṟai tāṉai paṭai eṉavarum.
‘nīṭiṭaṉuṭaittu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, nīḷātum ukaram peṟātum varumāyiṟṟu.
e-ṭu:	kuḻuttōṟṟam eṉa varum.
iṉṉum itaṉāṟ paḻukkāy eṉa alvaḻikkaṇṇum iv vitiyiṉṟi varutal koḷka.
‘āvayiṉāṉa’ eṉṟataṉāṟ, perumpāṉmai ceyyuṭkaṇ nīṇṭu ukaram peṟṟu,
‘eḻūuttāṅkiya katavumalaittavar
kuḻūukkaḷiṟṟuk kuṟumpuṭaittaliṉ’	(puṟam. 97)
eṉavum,
‘paḻūuppal laṉṉa paruvukirp pāvaṭi’	(kuṟun. 180)
eṉavum varutal koḷka.	(59)
‘oṭu’ marappeyar melleḻuttu mikutal
262.	oṭumarak kiḷavi utimara iyaṟṟē.
iḵtu avvīṟṟu marappeyaruḷ oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vitittatu.
(i-ḷ.) oṭu marak kiḷavi - oṭuveṉṉum marattiṉai uṇara niṉṟa col, utimara iyaṟṟu - utiyeṉṉum marattiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy mel leḻuttup peṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	oṭuṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.
‘maram’ eṉṟār, oṭu veṉṉum nōyai nīkkutaṟku. muṉṉar utimarattiṉ piṉṉar ammup peṟukiṉṟa puḷimaram vaitta iyaipāṉ, itaṟkum ammup pēṟu koḷka.
e-ṭu:	oṭuvaṅkōṭu eṉavarum.	(60)
cuṭṭumutal ukaram (vēṟṟumaikkaṇ) muṭiyumāṟu
263.	cuṭṭumutal iṟuti urupiyal nilaiyum
	oṟṟiṭai mikāa valleḻut tiyaṟkai.
itu cuṭṭup peyarkku valleḻuttu vilakkic cāriyai vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu mutal iṟuti urupiyal nilaiyum - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉai mutalākavuṭaiya ukara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷ poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇṇum urupu puṇarcciyiṟ kūṟiya iyalpilē niṉṟu aṉcāriyai peṟṟu ukaraṅkeṭṭu muṭiyum, valleḻuttu iyaṟkai oṟṟu iṭai mikāa-valleḻuttu iyaṟkaiyākiya oṟṟu iṭaikkaṇ mikā e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ataṉkōṭu, itaṉkōṭu, utaṉkōṭu, cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.
‘oṟṟiṭai mikā’ eṉavē, cāriyai vakuppa valleḻuttu vīḻā veṉpatu peṟṟām. ‘valleḻut tiyaṟkai’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ukara īṟṟuḷ eruvuñceruvum oḻittu ēṉaiyavaṟṟiṟkum urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṭ ceṉṟuḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. kaṭuviṉkuṟai, oṭuviṉkuṟai, eḻuviṉpuṟam, koḻuviṉ kūrmai eṉa varum. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, ‘utukkāṇ’ (akam. 4) eṉṟa vaḻi valleḻuttu mikutalum koḷka.	(61)
ūkāra īṟṟuppeyaratu alvaḻimuṭipu
264.	ūkāra iṟuti ākāra iyaṟṟē.
itu niṟutta muṟaiyāṉē ūkāra īṟu alvaḻikkaṇ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ūkāra iṟuti ākāra iyaṟṟu - ūkāra īṟṟup peyar alvaḻikkaṇ ākāra īṟṟu alvaḻiyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi avvalleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kaḻūukkaṭitu, koṇmūkkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavarum.	(62)
ivvīṟṟu viṉaiyeccamum ēvalorumaiyum aṉṉavātal
265.	viṉaiyeñcu kiḷavikkum muṉṉilai moḻikkum
	niṉaiyuṅ kālai avvakai varaiyār.
iḵtu ivvīṟṟu viṉaiyeccattiṟku mikku muṭiyum eṉṟaliṉ, eytātatu eytuvittatūum, muṉṉilai viṉaikku iyalpum uṟaḻpum (151) māṟṟutaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkiyatūum nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) viṉaiyeñcu kiḷavikkum - ūkāra īṟṟu viṉaiyecca mākiya coṟkum, muṉṉilai moḻikkum - muṉṉilai viṉaiccoṟkum, niṉaiyuṅkālai avvakai varaiyār - ārāyuṅ kālattu avval leḻuttu mikku muṭiyuṅ kūṟṟiṉai nīkkār e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uṇṇūk koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum, kaitūkkoṟṟā cāttā tēvā pūtā eṉavum varum.
‘niṉaiyuṅkālai’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ivvīṟṟu uyartiṇaip peyarkkum alvaḻikkaṇ valleḻuttuk koṭuttu muṭikka. āṭūuk kuṟiyaṉ makaṭūuk kuṟiyaḷ eṉa varum. uyartiṇaippeyar eṭuttōtiyē muṭippārātaliṉ ammuṭipu peṟāmaiyiṉ, īṟṟup potuvitiyāṉ muṭiyātu ilēcāṉ muṭittām.	(63)
ivvīṟṟu vēṟṟumaimuṭipu
266.	vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum ataṉō raṟṟē.
iḵtu ūkāra īṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum ataṉōraṟṟu - ūkāra īṟu vēṟṟumaip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum ākāra īṟṟu alvaḻi pōla valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kaḻūuk kaṭumai ciṟumai tīmai perumai, koṇmūk kuḻām celavu tōṟṟam paṟaivu eṉa varum.	(64)
ukara eḻuttuppēṟu tōṉṟumiṭaṉ
267.	kuṟṟeḻut timparum ōreḻuttu moḻikkum
	niṟṟal vēṇṭum ukarak kiḷavi.
iḵtu iyaipu valleḻuttiṉōṭu ukaram vakuttaliṉ, eytiyataṉ mēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟṟeḻuttu imparum - kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ piṉ vanta ūkāra īṟṟu moḻikkum, ōreḻuttu moḻikkum - ōreḻuttorumoḻiyākiya ūkāra īṟṟu moḻikkum, ukarakkiḷavi niṟṟal vēṇṭum - ukaramākiya eḻuttu niṟṟalai virumpum āciriyaṉ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uṭūukkuṟai ceykai talai puṟam eṉavum, tūuk kuṟai ceykai talai puṟam eṉavum varum.
‘niṟṟal’ eṉpataṉāṉ, uyartiṇaip peyarkkum valleḻuttum ukaramum koṭukka. āṭūukkai makaṭūukkai cevi talai puṟam eṉa varum. ivai tokaimarapiṉuḷ iyalpātal eytiyavaṟṟai (153) īṇṭu iruvaḻikkaṇṇum muṭittār; īṟṟup potu oppumai kaṇṭu.	(65)
‘pū’ mēlaiviti peṟāmai
268.	pūeṉ orupeyar āyiyal piṉṟē
	āvayiṉ valleḻuttu mikutalum urittē.
iḵtu ūkāra īṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku ukaramum iyaipu valleḻuttum vilakkip, perumpāṉmai melleḻuttuñ ciṟupāṉmai valleḻut tum peṟumeṉa (eytiyatu vilakki)p piṟituviti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) pūveṉ oru peyar a iyalpu iṉṟu - pūveṉṉum ūkāra īṟṟaiyuṭaiya oru peyar mēṟkūṟiya ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟu muṭiyum avviyal iṉmaiyai uṭaittu, āvayiṉ valleḻuttu mikutalum urittu - avviṭattu melleḻuttu mikku muṭitalē yaṉṟi valleḻuttu mikku muṭitalum urittu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	pūṅkoṭi cōlai tāmam pantu eṉavum, pūkkoṭi ceykai tāmam pantu eṉavum varum.
‘pū’ veṉpatu, poliveṉṉum viṉaikkuṟippai uṇarttātu niṟṟaṟku ‘oru peyar’ eṉṟār.	(66)
‘ū’ eṉum peyar ṉakara oṟṟup peṟṟu muṭital
269.	ūeṉ orupeyar āvoṭu civaṇum.
iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatu, ukaramum valleḻuttum vilakki ṉakaram vitittaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) ūveṉ oru peyar - ūveṉat tacaiyai uṇartti niṉṟa ōreḻuttorumoḻi, āvoṭu civaṇum - ākāra īṟṟil āveṉṉuñ col valleḻuttup peṟātu ṉakara oṟṟup peṟṟu muṭintāṟpōla ṉakara oṟṟup peṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ūveṉa niṟutti ṉakara oṟṟak koṭuttu, ūṉ kuṟai ceykai talai puṟam eṉa muṭikka.
ū eṉpatu tacaiyai uṇartti niṉṟa vaḻakku, āciriyar nūl ceyta kālattu vaḻakku. aṉṟit tēya vaḻakkēṉum uṇarka.	(67)
appeyar ṉakara oṟṟōṭu akkum peṟutal
270.	akkeṉ cāriyai peṟutalum urittē
	takkavaḻi aṟital vaḻakkat tāṉa.
iḵtu eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) akkeṉ cāriyai peṟutalum urittu - atikārattāṉ niṉṟa ū veṉṉum peyar muṟkūṟiya ṉakarattōṭu akkeṉṉuñ cāriyai peṟṟu muṭitalum urittu, vaḻakkattāṉa takkavaḻi aṟital - ammuṭipu vaḻakkiṭattut takka iṭam aṟika e-ṟu.
‘takkavaḻi aṟital’ eṉṟataṉāṟ, cāriyai peṟṟuḻi ṉakaram vilakkuṇṇātu niṟṟalum, muṉ māṭṭēṟṟāl vilakkuṇṭa valleḻuttuk keṭātu niṟṟaluṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	ūṉakkuṟai ceykai talaipuṟam eṉa varum.
‘vaḻakkattāṉa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ūkāra īṟṟuc colliṟku urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṭ ceṉṟuḻi iyaipu valleḻuttuk keṭukka.
e-ṭu:	koṇmūviṉ kuḻām, uṭūuviṉṟalai, ūviṉkuṟai eṉa varum.	(68)
āṭūu makaṭūup peyarkaḷ iṉ cāriyai peṟalumām eṉal
271.	āṭūu makaṭūu āyiru peyarkkum
	iṉiṭai variṉum māṉa millai.
itu ‘kuṟṟeḻuttimparum’ (eḻut. ) eṉpataṉuḷ ‘niṟṟal’ eṉṟa ilēcāṉ eytiya valleḻuttēyaṉṟic cāriyaiyum vakuttaliṉ eytiyataṉmēl ciṟappu viti uṇarttiyatu.
(i-ḷ.) āṭūu makaṭūu āyiru peyarkkum - āṭūu makaṭūuvākiya uyar tiṇaippeyar iraṇṭaṟkum, iṉiṭai variṉum māṉam illai - muṉṉeytiya valleḻuttē yaṉṟi iṉ cāriyai iṭaiyē variṉum kuṟṟamillai e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	āṭūuviṉkai, makaṭūuviṉkai, cevi talai puṟam eṉa varum.
‘māṉamillai’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, iṉpeṟṟuḻi mēl eytiya valleḻuttu vīḻkka.	(69)
ekaramum okaramum īṟāka varumiṭaṉ
272.	ekara okaram peyarkkī ṟākā
	muṉṉilai moḻiya eṉmaṉār pulavar
	tēṟṟamum ciṟappum alvaḻi yāṉa.
iḵtu ekara okaram īṟām iṭam uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) tēṟṟamum ciṟappum alvaḻi āṉa - teḷivup poruḷuñ ciṟappup poruḷum allāta vēṟṟumaip poruṇmaiyiṭattu aḷapeṭuttuk kūṟutaliṉ uḷavākiya, ekara okaram peyarkku īṟākā - ekara okaraṅkaḷ peyarkku īṟāy vārā, viṉaikku īṟāy varum, muṉṉilai moḻipa eṉmaṉār pulavar - avaitān taṉmaiyiṉum paṭarkkaiyiṉum vārā, muṉṉilaic colliṭattaṉavāmeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar e-ṟu.
eṉavē, teḷivup poruḷiṉuñ ciṟappup poruḷiṉum muṟaiyē vantu peyarkku īṟām iṭaiccollākiya ekara okaram mūṉṟiṭattiṟkum uriyavāmeṉṟu poruḷāyiṟṟu. eṉa, iṅṅaṉam aruttāpattiyāṟ koṇṭataṟku ilakkaṇam mēlaic cūttirattāṟ kūṟupa.
e-ṭu:	ēekkoṟṟā, ōokkoṟṟā, cāttā tēvā pūtā eṉa varum.
ivai, eṉakku oru karumampaṇi eṉavum, iṅṅaṉañ ceykiṉṟa taṉai oḻi eṉavum muṉṉilaiyēvaṟ poruḷavāy vantaṉa. itaṟku valleḻuttup peṟumāṟu mēlē kūṟupa.	(70)
tēṟṟa ekaramum ciṟappiṉ okaramum vali mikappeṟāmai
273.	tēṟṟa ekaramum ciṟappiṉ ovvum
	mēṟkū ṟiyaṟkai valleḻuttu mikā.
iḵtu eytiyatu ikantupaṭāmaṟ kāttu eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) tēṟṟa ekaramuñ ciṟappiṉ ovvum mēṟkūṟu iyaṟkai - muṉṉar aruttāpattiyāṟ peyarkkaṇ varumeṉṟa tēṟṟap poruṇmaiyiṉ ekaramuñ ciṟappupporuṇmaiyiṉ okaramum mūṉṟiṭattum varumeṉṟa ilakkaṇattaṉavām; valleḻuttu mikā - avai valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyā; eṉavē muṉṉilaikkaṇ varumeṉṟa ekara okaraṅkaḷ valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	yāṉēekoṇṭēṉ, nīyēekoṇṭāy, avaṉēe koṇṭāṉ eṉavum; yāṉōokoṭiyēṉ, nīyōo koṭiyai, avaṉōokoṭiyaṉ eṉavum peyarkkaṇ īṟāy iyalpāy vantavāṟu kāṇka.
itu muṉṉar eytiya ilakkaṇam ikavāmaṟkāttār. muṉniṉṟa cūttirattiṉ muṉṉilaikkum valleḻuttu mikuttu eytātatu eytuvit tār. iccūttirattiṟku aḷapeṭuttal ‘teḷiviṉēyum’ (col. 261) eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṟ koḷka. eṉavē, muṭivu peṟṟuḻi iṅṅaṉam iṭaiccollum eṭuttōtip puṇarppa reṉpatūum peṟṟām.	(71)
ēkāra īṟṟuppeyaratu alvaḻimuṭipu
274.	ēkāra iṟuti ūkāra iyaṟṟē.
itu niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē ēkāra īṟu alvaḻikkaṇ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ēkāra iṟuti - ēkāra īṟṟuppeyar alvaḻik kaṇ, ūkāra iyaṟṟu - ūkāra īṟṟu alvaḻiyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi avvalleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ēkkaṭitu, cēkkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum.	(72)
iv(vīṟṟu) iṭaiccoṟkaḷ iyalpāy muṭital
275.	māṟukōḷ eccamum viṉāvum eṇṇum
	kūṟiya valleḻuttu iyaṟkai yākum.
iḵtu iṭaiccoṟkaḷ iyalpāyp puṇarkaveṉa eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) māṟukōḷ eccamum - māṟukōṭalai yuṭaiya eccap poruṇmaikkaṇ varum ēkāra īṟṟu iṭaiccollum, viṉāvum - viṉāp poruṇmaikkaṇ varum ēkāra īṟṟu iṭaiccollum, eṇṇum - eṇṇup poruṇmaikkaṇ varum ēkāra vīṟṟu iṭaic collum, kūṟiya valleḻuttu iyaṟkaiyākum - muṟkūṟiya valleḻut tup peṟātu iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	yāṉēkoṇṭēṉ ceṉṟēṉ tantēṉ pōyiṉēṉ eṉpuḻi, yāṉkoṇṭilēṉeṉa māṟukoṇṭa oḻivupaṭa niṉṟatu. nīyē koṇṭāy ceṉṟāy tantāy pōyiṉāy eṉavum, nilaṉē nīrē tīyē vaḷiyē, koṟṟaṉē cāttaṉē eṉavum varum.
‘kūṟiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, pirinilai ēkāramum īṟṟacai ēkāramum iyalpāy muṭital koḷka. avaruḷ ivaṉē koṇṭāṉ eṉavum, ‘kaḻiyē, ciṟukura ṉeytaloṭu pāṭō vātē; kaṭalē, pāṭeḻun tolikkum’ (akam. 150) eṉavum varum.	(73)
ivvīṟṟup peyaratu vēṟṟumai muṭipu
276.	vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum ataṉō raṟṟē.
iḵtu ivvīṟṟu vēṟṟumai muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum - ēkāra īṟṟu vēṟṟumaip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum, ataṉōraṟṟu - ūkāra īṟṟu alvaḻi pōla valleḻuttu vantuḻi avvalleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ēkkaṭumai ciṟumai tīmai perumai eṉavum, vēkkuṭam cāṭi tūtai pāṉai eṉavum varum.
vēkkuṭam - vētalaiyuṭaiya kuṭameṉa viriyum.	(74)
ē eṉṉum iṟuti ekaram peṟumāṟu
277.	ēyeṉ iṟutikku ekaram varumē.
itu valleḻuttiṉōṭu ekaram vitittaliṉ, eytiyataṉ mēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ēyeṉ iṟutikku ekaram varum - avvēṟṟumaikkaṇ ēyeṉṉum iṟutikku ekaram varum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ēekkoṭṭil cālai tuḷai puḻai eṉa varum.
varumoḻi varaiyātu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ, iyalpukaṇattukkaṇṇum varumeṉak koḷka.
e-ṭu:	ēeñekiḻcci nērmai eṉa varum.
‘uraiyiṟ kōṭalāṉ’ ekaram ēṟpuḻik koḷka.	(75)
cē eṉum marappeyar meli mikutal
278.	cēeṉ marappeyar oṭumara iyaṟṟē.
iḵtu avvīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vitittatu.
(i-ḷ.) cē eṉ marappeyar - peṟṟamaṉṟic cēveṉṉum marattiṉai uṇaraniṉṟa peyar, oṭumara iyaṟṟu - oṭumaram pōla melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	cēṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.	(76)
appeyar peṟṟamāyiṉ iṉcāriyai peṟutal
279.	peṟṟam āyiṉ muṟṟaiṉ vēṇṭum.
iḵtu iyaipu valleḻuttu vilakki iṉ vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) peṟṟam āyiṉ - muṟkūṟiya cēveṉpatu peṟṟattiṉai uṇarttiya poḻutāyiṉ, muṟṟa iṉ vēṇṭum - muṭiya iṉ cāriyai peṟṟu muṭiyavēṇṭum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	cēviṉkōṭu cevi talai puṟam eṉa varum.
‘muṟṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉē, muṟkūṟiya cēveṉṉum marappeyarkkum ēveṉpataṟkum urupiṟku eytiya cāriyai poruṭkaṭ ceṉṟuḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻtal koḷka.
e-ṭu:	cēviṉkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉavum, ēviṉ kaṭumai ciṟumai tīmai perumai eṉavum varum.
cāriyaippēṟu varumoḻi varaiyātu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ, iyalpu kaṇattum iṉpeṟutal koḷka.
e-ṭu:	cēviṉalam maṇi vāl, cēviṉimil, cēviṉaṭai, cēviṉāṭṭam eṉa varum.
iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ iṉ peṟātu varutaluṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	ceyyuṭkaṇ ‘teṉṟaṟku vīṇaikkuc cēmaṇikkuk kōkilattiṟku, aṉṟiṟku’ eṉa varum.	(77)
aikāra īṟṟup peyaratu vēṟṟumai muṭipu
280.	aikāra iṟutip peyarnilai muṉṉar
	vēṟṟumai yāyiṉ valleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu aikāra īṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu. tokaimarapiṉuḷ ‘vēṟṟumaiyalvaḻi iai yeṉṉum’ (eḻut. 158) eṉpataṉkaṇ alvaḻi muṭittār.
(i-ḷ.) aikāra iṟutip peyarnilai muṉṉar - aikāra īṟṟup peyarccoṉ muṉṉar atikārattāṟ kacatapa mutaṉmoḻi vantuḻi, vēṟṟumaiyāyiṉ valleḻuttu mikum - vēṟṟumaip poruṭpuṇarcci yāyiṉ tamakkup poruntiṉa valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	yāṉaikkōṭu cevi talai puṟam eṉa varum.
‘vēṟṟumaiyāyiṉ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, urupupuṇarccikkaṇṇum yāṉaiyaik koṇarntāṉeṉa valleḻuttu mikutal koḷka.	(78)
cuṭṭu mutal ‘ai’ īṟu vaṟṟuppeṟṟu muṭital
281.	cuṭṭumutal iṟuti urupiyal nilaiyum.
itu valleḻuttiṉoṭu vaṟṟu vakuttaliṉ, eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu mutal iṟuti - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉai mutalākavuṭaiya aikāra īṟṟuppeyar, urupiyal nilaiyum - urupu puṇarcciyiṟ kūṟiya iyalpu pōlap poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ vaṟṟuppeṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	avaiyaṟṟukkōṭu, ivaiyaṟṟukkōṭu, uvaiyaṟṟuk kōṭu cevi talai puṟam eṉa varum.
itaṉai ‘vaḵkāṉ meykeṭa’ (eḻut. 122) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭikka.(79)
vicai, ñemai, namai - immarappeyarkaḷ meli mikutal
282.	vicaimarak kiḷaviyum ñemaiyum namaiyum
	āmup peyarum cēmara iyala.
itu valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vitittaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) vicaimarak kiḷaviyum - vicaittaṟṟoḻilaṉṟi vicai yeṉṉum marattai uṇaraniṉṟa collum, ñemaiyum - ñemai yeṉṉum marattiṉai uṇaraniṉṟa collum, namaiyum - namai eṉṉum marattiṉai uṇara niṉṟa collum āmup peyarum - ākiya ammūṉṟu peyarum, cēmara iyala - valleḻuttu mikātu cēmaram pōla mel leḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	vicaiṅkōṭu, ñemaiṅkōṭu, namaiṅkōṭu, cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.
ivai, ‘kacatapa mutaliya moḻimēl tōṉṟum melleḻuttu’ (eḻut. 143) eṉṟu uṇarka.	(80)
paṉai, arai, āvirai - ippeyarkaḷ muṭiyumāṟu
283.	paṉaiyum araiyum āviraik kiḷaviyum
	niṉaiyuṅ kālai ammoṭu civaṇum
	aiyeṉ iṟuti araivaraintu keṭumē
	meyavaṇ oḻiya eṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu iyaipu valleḻuttu vilakki ammu vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) paṉaiyum araiyum āviraik kiḷaviyum - paṉaiyeṉṉum peyarum araiyeṉṉum peyarum āviraiyeṉṉum peyarum, niṉaiyuṅkālai ammoṭu civaṇum - ārāyuṅ kālattu valleḻuttu mikātu ammuc cāriyaiyoṭu porunti muṭiyum; aiyeṉ iṟuti arai varaintu keṭum - avviṭattu ai yeṉṉum īṟu araiyeṉṉuñ collai nīkki ēṉai iraṇṭaṟkuṅkeṭum; mey avaṇ oḻiya eṉmaṉār pulavar - taṉṉāṉ ūrappaṭṭa meykeṭātu accol liṭattē niṟka eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	paṉai āvirai eṉa niṟutti, ammu varuvittu aikāraṅ keṭuttu oṟṟiṉmēlē ammiṉ akaramēṟṟip, paṉaṅkāy āviraṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉavarum. araiyeṉa niṟutti ammuk koṭuttu aikāraṅ keṭātu, araiyaṅ kōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa muṭikka.
valleḻuttuk kēṭu mēlē ‘kaṭinilaiyiṉṟu’ (eḻut. 285) eṉṟata ṉāṟ kūṟutum. ‘niṉaiyuṅkālai’ yeṉṟataṉāṉ, tūtuṇai vaḻutuṇai tillai ōlai tāḻai eṉa niṟutti, ammukkoṭuttu aikāraṅkeṭuttut tūtuṇaṅkāy vaḻutuṇaṅkāy tillaṅkāy  ōlampōḻ tāḻaṅkāy eṉa muṭikka.	(81)
paṉaiyiṉ muṉṉar ‘aṭṭu’p puṇarumāṟu
284.	paṉaiyiṉ muṉṉar aṭṭuvaru kālai
	nilaiyiṉ ṟākum aiyeṉ uyirē
	ākāram varutal āvayi ṉāṉa.
itu nilaimoḻic ceykai nōkki eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) paṉaiyiṉ muṉṉar aṭṭuvaru kālai - muṟkūṟiya vāṟaṉṟip paṉaiyeṉṉum coṉmuṉṉar aṭṭeṉṉuñ col varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, nilaiyiṉṟu ākum aiyeṉ uyir - niṟṟalillaiyākum aiyeṉṉum uyir; āvayiṉāṉ ākāram varutal - avviṭattu ākāram vantu ammeymmēlēṟi muṭika e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	paṉāaṭṭu eṉa varum. itaṟku mūṉṟāvatum āṟāvatum viriyum.
‘āvayiṉāṉa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ōrānayam viccāvāti eṉṉum vēṟṟumai muṭipum, kēṭṭāmūlam pāṟāṅkal eṉṉum alvaḻi muṭipuṅ koḷka. ivaṟṟuḷ vaṭamoḻikaḷai maṟuttalum oṉṟu.	(82)
‘koṭi’ vantu puṇarumāṟu
285.	koṭimuṉ variṉē aiyavaṇ niṟpak
	kaṭinilai yiṉṟē valleḻuttu mikuti.
itumēl aikāraṅ keṭuttu ammuppeṟuka eṉṟār; īṇṭu atu keṭātu niṟka valleḻuttup peṟuka eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu ikantu paṭāmaṟ kāttatu. ammu vilakki valleḻuttu vitittaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatumām.
(i-ḷ.) paṉaimuṉ koṭi variṉ - paṉaiyeṉṉuñ coṉ muṉṉark koṭiyeṉṉuñ col variṉ, ai avaṇ niṟpa - kēṭu ōtiya aikāram āṇṭuk keṭātu niṟpa, valleḻuttu mikuti kaṭinilaiyiṉṟu - valleḻuttu mikku muṭital nīkku nilaimaiyiṉṟu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	paṉaikkoṭi eṉa varum. itaṟku iraṇṭāvatum mūṉṟāvatum viriyum.
‘kaṭinilaiyiṉṟu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, aikāra īṟṟuppeyarkaḷellām eṭuttōttāṉum ilēcāṉum ammuccāriyaiyum piṟacāriyaiyum peṟṟuḻi atikāra valleḻuttuk keṭuttuk koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, urupiṟkucceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṭ ceṉṟuḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. paṉaiyiṉ kuṟai araiyiṉ kōṭu āviraiyiṉ kōṭu vicaiyiṉ kōṭu ñemaiyiṉ kōṭu namaiyiṉ kōṭu eṉavum, tūtuṇaiyiṉ kāy vaḻutuṇaiyiṉ kāy uḻaiyiṉ kōṭu vaḻaiyiṉ kōṭu eṉavum varum.
paṉaitiraḷ, paṉaittiraḷ, eṉṉum uṟaḻccimuṭipu tokai marapiṉuṭ puṟaṉaṭaiyāṟ koḷka; alvaḻiyu mātaliṉ. aṉṟi, īṇṭu ‘avaṇ’ eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷvārum uḷar.	(83)
ivvīṟṟut tiṅkaṭpeyarum nāṭpeyarum muṭiyumāṟu
286.	tiṅkaḷum nāḷum muntukiḷan taṉṉa.
iḵtu iyaipu valleḻuttiṉōṭu ikkuc cāriyaiyum, valleḻuttu vilakki āṉ cāriyaiyum vakuttaliṉ, eytiyataṉ mēṟ ciṟappuvitiyum, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituvitiyuṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) tiṅkaḷum nāḷum - aikāra īṟṟut tiṅkaḷai uṇara niṉṟa peyarum nāḷai uṇaraniṉṟa peyarum, muntu kiḷantaṉṉa - ikara īṟṟut tiṅkaḷum nāḷumpōla ikkum āṉum peṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	cittiraikkuk koṇṭāṉ, kēṭṭaiyāṟ koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
cittirai nāḷāyiṉ āṉ cāriyai koṭukka. valleḻuttukkēṭu muṉṉark ‘kaṭinilaiyiṉṟu’ (eḻut. 285) eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷka.
tiṅkaḷ muṟkūṟiya muṟaiyaṉṟik kūṟṟiṉāṉ, uḻaiṅkōṭu amaiṅkōṭu uṭaiṅkōṭu eṉa melleḻuttuk koṭuttum, kalaiṅ kōṭu kalaikkōṭu eṉa uṟaḻcci eytuvittum, kariyavaṟṟukkōṭu kuṟiyavaṟṟukkōṭu neṭiyavaṟṟukkōṭu eṉa aikāra īṟṟup paṇpukoḷ peyarkku vaṟṟukkoṭuttu aikāraṅ keṭuttu vaṟṟumicai yoṟṟeṉṟu oṟṟuk keṭuttum, avaiyattuk koṇṭāṉ avaiyiṟ koṇṭāṉ eṉa attum iṉṉum koṭuttum, paṉaiyiṉmāṇpu kēṭṭaiyi ṉāṭṭiṉāṉeṉa iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ iṉ cāriyai koṭuttum muṭikka. aikāra īṟu iṉcāriyai peṟutal tokai marapiṉuṭ kūṟāmaiyiṉ īṇṭuk koṇṭām.	(84)
‘maḻai’ attum iṉṉum peṟṟumuṭital
287.	maḻaiyeṉ kiḷavi vaḷiyiyal nilaiyum.
itu valleḻuttiṉōṭu attu vakuttaliṉ eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappu vitiyum, iyaipu valleḻuttu vilakki iṉ vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituvitiyuṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) maḻaiyeṉ kiḷavi - maḻaiyeṉṉum aikāra īṟṟuc col, vaḷiyiyal nilaiyum - vaḷiyeṉṉuñ col attum iṉṉum peṟṟu muṭinta iyalpiṉkaṇṇē niṉṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	maḻaiyattukkoṇṭāṉ, maḻaiyiṟkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
īṇṭu iṉpeṟṟuḻi valleḻuttukkēṭu ‘kaṭinilaiyiṉṟu’   (eḻut. 285) eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷka. cāriyaippēṟu varumoḻi varaiyātu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ iyalpukaṇattukkaṇṇuṅ koḷka. maḻaiyattu ñāṉṟāṉ, maḻaiyiṉ ñāṉṟāṉ niṟuttiṉāṉ māṭṭiṉāṉ vantāṉ aṭaintāṉ eṉa oṭṭuka.	(85)
‘vēṭkai’ muṉṉar ‘avā’p puṇarum ceyyuḷmuṭipu
288.	ceyyuḷ maruṅkiṉ vēṭkai eṉṉum
	aiyeṉ iṟuti avāmuṉ variṉē
	meyyoṭuṅ keṭutal eṉmaṉār pulavar
	ṭakāram ṇakāra mātal vēṇṭum.
itu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ ceyyuṇ muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ceyyuḷ maruṅkiṉ vēṭkai eṉṉum aiyeṉ iṟuti - ceyyuḷiṭattu vēṭkaiyeṉṉum aikāra īṟṟuc col, avā muṉvariṉ - avā veṉṉuñ coṟku muṉṉar variṉ, meyyoṭuṅ keṭutal eṉmaṉār pulavar - avvaikāram tāṉ ūrnta meyyoṭuṅkūṭak keṭumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar, ṭakāram ṇakāram ātal vēṇṭum - avviṭattu niṉṟa ṭakāra oṟṟu ṇakāra oṟṟāyt tirital vēṇṭum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	‘vēṇavā naliya veyya vuyirā’ (naṟ. 61) eṉa varum.
vēṭkaiyāvatu poruḷkaḷmēl tōṉṟum paṟṟuḷḷam. avāvāvatu, apporuḷkaḷaip peṟavēṇṭumeṉṟu mēṉmēl nikaḻum ācai, eṉavē, vēṭkaiyā luṇṭākiya avāveṉa mūṉṟaṉurupu virintatu. itaṉai vēṭkaiyum avāvumeṉa alvaḻi yeṉpārum uḷar. iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟuvār pāṟaṅkal eṉpataṉai ammuk koṭuttu īṇṭu muṭippar.	(86)
ōkāra īṟṟup peyaratu alvaḻi muṭipu
289.	ōkāra iṟuti ēkāra iyaṟṟē.
iḵtu ōkāra īṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ōkāra iṟuti ēkāra iyaṟṟu - ōkāra īṟṟup peyarccol ēkāra īṟṟu alvaḻiyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi avvalleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ōkkaṭitu, cōkkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum.	(87)
ivvīṟṟu iṭaiccol iyalpāy muṭital
290.	māṟukōḷ eccamum viṉāvum aiyamum
	kūṟiya valleḻut tiyaṟkai yākum.
iḵtu iṭaiccol muṭipu kūṟaliṉ, eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) māṟukōḷ eccamum - māṟukōṭalai uṭaiya eccap poruṇmaiyai oḻipākavuṭaiya ōkāramum, viṉāvum - viṉāp poruṇmaiyaiyuṭaiya ōkāramum, aiyamum - aiyap poruṇmaiyai yuṭaiya ōkāramum, kūṟiya valleḻuttu iyaṟkaiyākum - muṟkūṟiya valleḻuttiṉṟi iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	yāṉō koṇṭēṉ eṉavum, nīyō koṇṭāy eṉavum, pattōpatiṉoṉṟō, puṟṟōputalō eṉavum varum.
‘kūṟiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ‘yāṉō tēṟē ṉavarpoy vaḻaṅkalarē’ (kuṟun. 21) eṉap pirinilaiyum, naṉṟō tītō kaṇṭatu eṉat terinilai yum, ōokoṇṭāṉ eṉac ciṟappum, ‘kuṉṟuṟaḻnta kaḷiṟeṉkō koyyuḷaiya māveṉkō’ (puṟam. 387) eṉa eṇṇunilaiyum val leḻuttu mikātu iyalpāy muṭital koḷka. itaṉāṉē īṟṟacai varumēṉum uṇarka.	(88)
291.	oḻintataṉ nilaiyum moḻintavaṟ ṟiyaṟṟē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) oḻintataṉ nilaiyum moḻintavaṟṟu iyaṟṟu - oḻiyicai ōkārattiṉatu nilaiyum muṟkūṟiya ōkāraṅkaḷiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	koḷalō koṇṭāṉ, celalō ceṉṟāṉ, taralō tantāṉ, pōtalō pōyiṉāṉ eṉa ōcai vēṟṟumaiyāṉ orucol tōṉṟap poruḷ tantu niṟkum.	(89)
ōkāra īṟṟu vēṟṟumai muṭipu
292.	vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum ataṉō raṟṟē
	okaram varutal āvayi ṉāṉa.
iḵtu ōkāra īṟṟu vēṟṟumai muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum ataṉōraṟṟu - ōkāra īṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum avvēkāra īṟṟu alvaḻiyōṭu ottu valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum; āvayiṉāṉ okaram varutal - avviṭattu okaram varuka e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ōokkaṭumai, kōokkaṭumai ciṟumai tīmai perumai eṉa varum.	(90)
‘kō’ ‘il’loṭu puṇarumvaḻi iyalpātal
293.	illoṭu kiḷappiṉ iyaṟkai yākum.
iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu; eṉṉai? muṉṉar vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi okaram peṟuka eṉa varaintu kūṟātum, nilaimoḻit toḻil varaiyātuṅ kūṟaliṉ nāṉku kaṇattukkaṇṇuñ cēṟaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) illoṭu kiḷappiṉ iyaṟkaiyākum - ōkāra īṟṟuk kōveṉṉum moḻiyiṉai illeṉṉum varumoḻiyoṭu colliṉ okaram mikātu iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kōvil eṉa varum.
kōveṉṟatu uyartiṇaip peyaraṉṟō veṉiṉ, kōvanta teṉṟu aḵṟiṇaiyāy muṭitaliṉ aḵṟiṇaippāṟpaṭṭateṉka.	(91)
ivvīṟṟuppeyar oṉcāriyai peṟṟum muṭital
294.	urupiyal nilaiyum moḻiyumā ruḷavē
	āvayiṉ valleḻut tiyaṟkai yākum.
itu valleḻuttu vilakkic cāriyai vakuttaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) urupiyal nilaiyum moḻiyumāruḷa - ōkāra īṟṟuc cila poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ urupu puṇarcciyatu iyalpilē niṉṟu oṉ cāriyai peṟṟu muṭiyum moḻikaḷum uḷa, āvayiṉ valleḻuttu iyaṟkaiyākum - avviṭattu valleḻuttiṉṟi iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kōoṉkai cevi talai puṟam eṉa varum.
cāriyaippēṟu varumoḻi valleḻuttai vilakkāmai itaṉāṉum peṟṟām.	(92)
aukāra īṟu iruvaḻiyum muṭiyumāṟu
295.	aukāra iṟutip peyarnilai muṉṉar
	alvaḻi yāṉum vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum
	valleḻuttu mikutal varainilai yiṉṟē
	avviru vīṟṟum ukaram varutal
	cevvi teṉpa ciṟantici ṉōrē.
iḵtu aukāra īṟu iruvaḻiyum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) aukāra iṟutip peyarnilai muṉṉar - aukāram iṟutiyākiya peyarccoṉmuṉṉar valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy variṉ, alvaḻiyāṉum vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum - al vaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum, valleḻuttu mikutal varainilai yiṉṟu - valleḻuttu mikkumuṭital nīkku nilaimaiyiṉṟu, avviru vīṟṟum ukaram varutal cevvi teṉpa ciṟanticiṉōr - avviru kūṟṟu muṭipiṉkaṇṇum nilaimoḻikkaṇ ukaram vantu muṭital cevvi teṉṟu colluvar ciṟantōr e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kauvukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavum, kaṭumai ciṟumai tīmai perumai eṉavum varum.
‘cevvitu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, meṉkaṇattum iṭaikkaṇattum ukaram peṟutal koḷka. kauvuñemirntatu ñemircci eṉavum, vauvuvalitu valimai eṉavum varum.
‘nilai’ yeṉṟataṉāṟ, kauviṉ kaṭumai eṉa urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṭceṉṟuḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻvuṅ koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, aikāramum ikaramum vēṟṟumaikkaṇ urupu tokaiyāyuḻi iyalpātaluṅ koḷka.	(93)
uyir mayaṅkiyal muṟṟiṟṟu.

8
puḷḷimayaṅkiyal
ñakāra īṟu iruvaḻiyum vaṉkaṇattoṭu puṇartal
296.	ñakārai oṟṟiya toḻiṟpeyar muṉṉar
	allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum
	valleḻut tiyaiyiṉ avveḻuttu mikumē
	ukaram varutal āvayi ṉāṉa
eṉpatu cūttiram.
niṟutta muṟaiyāṉē uyirīṟu puṇarttup puḷḷiyīṟu vaṉkaṇat toṭuñ ciṟupāṉmai ēṉaik kaṇattoṭum puṇarumāṟu kūṟaliṉ ivvōttup ‘puḷḷimayaṅkiyal’ eṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu.
iccūttiram ñakāra īṟu vaṉkaṇattōṭu iruvaḻiyum puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ñakārai oṟṟiya toḻiṟpeyar muṉṉar - ñakāram īṟṟiṉkaṇ oṉṟāka niṉṟa toḻiṟpeyariṉ muṉṉar, allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum - alvaḻiyaic collumiṭattum vēṟṟumaiyaic collumiṭattum, valleḻuttu iyaiyiṉ avveḻuttumikum-valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy variṉ avvalleḻuttu varumoḻikkaṇ mikku muṭiyum, āvayiṉāṉ ukaram varutal - avviṭattu ukaram varuka e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uriñukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavum, uriñukkaṭumai ciṟumai tīmai perumai eṉavum varum.	(1)
ña na ma va varuvaḻiyum ivvīṟu ukaram peṟutal
297.	ñanamava iyaiyiṉum ukaram nilaiyum.
iḵtu avvīṟu meṉkaṇattoṭum iṭaikkaṇattu vakarat toṭum muṭiyumeṉa eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) ñanamava iyaiyiṉum ukaram nilaiyum - aññakara īṟu vaṉkaṇamaṉṟi ñanamava mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy variṉum nilaimoḻikkaṇ ukaram nilaipeṟṟu muṭiyum. e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uriñuñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci valimai eṉavum varum.
yakarattōṭum uyiroṭum puṇarumāṟu tokaimarapiṉuḷ ‘ukara moṭu puṇarum’ (eḻut. 163) eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟiṉār.	(2)
nakāra īṟum ukaram peṟṟu muṭital
298.	nakara iṟutiyum ataṉō raṟṟē.
itu nakara īṟu muṟkūṟiya kaṇaṅkaḷōṭu alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟi eytātatu eytuvikkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) nakara iṟutiyum - nakara īṟṟuppeyarum muṟkūṟiya kaṇaṅka ḷoṭu puṇarumvaḻi, ataṉōraṟṟu - aññakara īṟṟut toḻiṟ peyar iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi avveḻuttu mikku ukaram peṟṟum ñanamava vantuḻi ukaram peṟṟum muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	porunukkaṭitu verinukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavum, ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum varum.
muṭipu oppumai nōkki nakaraīṟu īṇṭup puṇarttār. īṇṭu vēṟṟumai yoḻittu māṭṭēṟu ceṉṟateṉṟu uṇarka.	(3)
ivvīṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ akaram peṟṟumuṭital
299.	vēṟṟumaikku ukkeṭa akaram nilaiyum.
itu nilaimoḻi ukaram vilakki akaram vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakukkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumaikku - annakara īṟu vēṟṟumaip poruṭ puṇarccikku, ukkeṭa akaram nilaiyum - mēleytiya ukaraṅ keṭa akarattoṭu nilaipeṟṟup puṇarum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	porunakkaṭumai verinakkaṭumai ciṟumai tīmai perumai ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci vaṉmai eṉa varum.
‘akaranilaiyum’ eṉṉātu ‘ukaraṅkeṭa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṭ ceṉṟuḻi iyaipuvalleḻuttu vīḻvuñ ciṟupāṉmai ukarappēṟuṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	veriniṉkuṟai poruniṉkuṟai uriñiṉkuṟai eṉavum, ‘uyaval yāṉai verinuc ceṉṟaṉṉa’ (akam. 168) eṉavum varum. yāṉaiyiṉatu mutukiṉmēṟ ceṉṟaṉṉa eṉa virikka.
porun eṉpatu oru cātippeyarum, porunutal eṉṉum viṉaippeyarumām.	(4)
‘verin’ nakaram keṭṭu melimikku muṭital
300.	verineṉ iṟuti muḻutuṅ keṭuvaḻi
	varumiṭaṉ uṭaittē melleḻut tiyaṟkai.
iḵtu annakara īṟṟuḷ orumoḻikku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) verin eṉ iṟuti - verin eṉṟu collappaṭum nakara īṟṟumoḻi, muḻutuṅ keṭuvaḻi - taṉīṟṟu nakaram muṉpeṟṟa ukarattōṭu eñcāmaik keṭṭa iṭattu, melleḻuttu iyaṟkai varum iṭaṉ uṭaittu - melleḻuttup peṟum iyalpu vantu muṭiyum iṭaṉuṭaittu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	veriṅkuṟai ceykai talai puṟam eṉa varum.
melleḻuttu varumoḻi nōkki vantatu. ‘veyilveri niṟutta payilitaḻp pacuṅkuṭai’ (akam. 37) eṉpatil nakara ikaramē iṭṭeḻutupa.	(5)
accol valimikku muṭitalum uṭaimai
301.	āvayiṉ valleḻuttu mikutalum urittē.
iḵtu ataṟku eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) āvayiṉ - avverin eṉṉuñ col avvāṟu īṟu keṭṭu niṉṟa iṭattu, valleḻuttu mikutalum urittu - mel leḻuttēyaṉṟi valleḻuttu mikku muṭitalum urittu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	verikkuṟai ceykai talai puṟam eṉa varum.	(6)
ṇakāra īṟṟup peyaratu vēṟṟumai muṭipu
302.	ṇakāra iṟuti valleḻut tiyaipiṉ
	ṭakāra mākum vēṟṟumaip poruṭkē.
itu niṟutta muṟaiyāṉē ṇakāra īṟu vēṟṟumaip poruṭkaṇ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ṇakāra iṟuti - ṇakāra īṟṟuppeyar, valleḻuttu iyaiyiṉ - valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi vantu iyaiyiṉ, ṭakāramākum vēṟṟumaip poruṭku - ṭakāramākat tirintu muṭiyum vēṟṟumaip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ e-ṟu. 
e-ṭu:	maṭkuṭam cāṭi tūtai pāṉai eṉa varum. maṇkai puṇkai eṉpaṉa iraṇṭāvataṉ tiripiṉ muṭintaṉa.
kavaṇkāl paraṇkāl eṉpaṉa mēl muṭittum.	(7)
‘āṇ’, ‘peṇ’ iyalpāy muṭiyumāṟu
303.	āṇum peṇṇum aḵṟiṇai iyaṟkai.
iḵtu ivvīṟṟu viravuppeyaruḷ cilavaṟṟiṟku, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) āṇum peṇṇum - āṇeṉṉum viravuppeyarum peṇṇeṉṉum viravuppeyarum, aḵṟiṇai iyaṟkai - tokai marapiṉuḷ ‘moḻi mutalākum’ (eḻut. 147) eṉpataṉkaṇ aḵṟiṇaip peyar muṭinta iyalpupōlat tāmum vēṟṟumaikkaṇ iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	āṇkai peṇkai cevi talai puṟam eṉa varum.
itu tokaimarapiṉuḷ ‘aḵṟiṇai viravuppeyar’ (eḻut. 155) eṉpataṉuḷ muṭinta iyalpaṉṟōveṉiṉ, ivai āṇṭu muṭintaṉa pōlat tattam marapiṉ viṉaiyāṟ pālaṟiyap paṭuvaṉa aṉṟi iru tiṇaikkaṇṇum aḵṟiṇaiyāy muṭitaliṉ, aḵṟiṇaip peyaratu iyalpōṭu māṭṭeṟintu muṭittāreṉka. āṇkaṭitu peṇkaṭitu eṉṉum alvaḻi muṭipu ‘moḻi mutalākum’ (eḻut. 147) eṉpataṉkaṇ varumoḻi muṟkūṟiya vataṉāṉ muṭikka.	(8)
‘āṇ’ marappeyar ammuccāriyai peṟutal
304.	āṇmarak kiḷavi araimara iyaṟṟē.
itu tiripu vilakkic cāriyai vakuttaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) āṇmarak kiḷavi - āṇpālai uṇarttātu āṇeṉṉum marattiṉai uṇaraniṉṟa col, arai mara iyaṟṟu - araiyeṉṉum maram ammuppeṟṟa iyalpiṟṟāyt tāṉum ammup peṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	āṇaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.
‘oṉṟeṉa muṭittalāṉ’ iyalpukaṇattuṅ koḷka. āṇa nār ilai eṉa varum.
viravuppeyaraṉṟeṉṟaṟku ‘maram’ eṉṟār.	(9)
‘viṇ’ eṉum pūtakkiḷaviyatu ceyyuḷ muṭipu
305.	viṇṇeṉa varūuṅ kāyap peyarvayiṉ
	uṇmaiyum urittē atteṉ cāriyai
	ceyyuḷ maruṅkiṉ toḻilvaru kālai.
itu ceyyuḷuḷ tiripu vilakkic cāriyai vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) viṇ eṉa varūuṅ kāyap peyarvayiṉ - viṇṇeṉṟu collavarukiṟa ākāyattai uṇara niṉṟa peyarkkaṇ, attu eṉ cāriyai uṇmaiyum urittu - atteṉṉuñ cāriyai uṇṭātalum urittu illaiyātalum urittu; ceyyuḷ maruṅkiṉ toḻil varukālai - ceyyuḷiṭattut toḻiṟcol varuṅkālattu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	‘viṇṇattuk koṭkum vaṇṇat tamarar’ ‘viṇṇattuk koṭkum viraicela lūrtiyōy’ eṉavum, ‘viṇkuttu nīḷ varai veṟpa kaḷaipavō’ (nālaṭi. 226) eṉavum varum.
viṇṇeṉṉum kuṟippiṉai nīkkutaṟkuk ‘kāyam’ eṉṟār.
viṇvattuk koṭkum eṉa uṭampaṭumey puṇarntu niṟṟaluṅ koḷka. atikāra valleḻuttiṉmaiyiṟ cāriyai valleḻuttuk koṭukka.	(10)
ivvīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar ukaram peṟṟu muṭital
306.	toḻiṟpeya rellān toḻiṟpeya riyala.
iḵtu ivvīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkut tiripu vilakki ukaramum valleḻuttum vitikkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) toḻiṟpeyar ellām toḻiṟ peyar iyala - ṇakāra īṟṟu mutaṉilait toḻiṟpeya rellām alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum ñakāra īṟṟut toḻiṟ peyaratu iyalpiṉavāy vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi valleḻuttum ukaramum peṟṟum, meṉkaṇattum iṭaikkaṇattu vakarattum ukaram peṟṟum muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	maṇṇukkaṭitu paṇṇukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, maṇṇukkaṭumai ciṟumai tīmai perumai ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci vaṉmai eṉavum varum.
‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāl toḻiṟpeyarallaṉavum ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟum iṉcāriyai peṟṟum puṇarvaṉa koḷka.
e-ṭu:	veṇṇukkarai, ‘tāḻpeyal kaṉaikural kaṭuppap paṇṇup peyarttu’ (maturaik. 560) eṇṇuppāṟu, veṇṇiṉkarai eṉa varum.	(11)
ivvīṟṟuk kiḷaippeyar iyalpāy muṭital
307.	kiḷaippeya rellāṅ koḷattiri pilavē.
iḵtu ivvīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkut tiripu vilakki iyalpu kūṟutaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) kiḷaip peyarellām - ṇakāra īṟṟuḷ ōriṉattai uṇaraniṉṟa peyarellām, koḷat tiripu ila - tiripuṭaiya veṉṟu karutumpaṭiyākat tiritalilavāy iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	umaṇ eṉa niṟuttik kuṭi cēri tōṭṭam pāṭi eṉat tantu muṭikka.
iṉi, ‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, piṟa cāriyai peṟṟu muṭivaṉavum, iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum koḷka. maṇṇappattam eṇṇanōlai eṉavum, kavaṇkāl paraṇkāl eṉavuṅ koḷka.
‘koḷa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ēḻām vēṟṟumai iṭap poruṇmai uṇara niṉṟa iṭaiccoṟkaḷ tirintu muṭivaṉavum koḷka.
aṅkaṭ koṇṭāṉ iṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ uṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ eṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ eṉavum, āṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ īṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ ūṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ yāṅkaṭ koṇṭāṉ eṉavum, avaṭkoṇṭāṉ ivaṭkoṇṭāṉ uvaṭkoṇṭāṉ evaṭkoṇṭāṉ eṉavum oṭṭuka.	(12)
‘eṇ’ eṉum uṇavuppeyaratu alvaḻimuṭipu
308.	vēṟṟumai yalvaḻi eṇṇeṉ uṇavuppeyar
	vēṟṟumai yiyaṟkai nilaiyalu murittē.
iḵtu avvīṟṟuḷ oṉṟu alvaḻiyuḷ vēṟṟumai muṭipu pōlat tirintu muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumai alvaḻi - vēṟṟumaiyallāta iṭattu, eṇṇeṉ uṇavuppeyar - varaiyaṟaip poruṇmai uṇarttātu eṇṇeṉṟu collappaṭum uṇaviṉai uṇarttum peyar, vēṟṟumai iyaṟkai nilaiyalum urittu - vēṟṟumaiyatu tirintu muṭiyum iyalpiṉ niṟṟalum urittu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	eṭkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum.
ummaiyāṉ, tokaimarapiṉuḷ ‘moḻimutalākum’ (eḻut. 147) eṉṟataṉāṟ kūṟiya iyalpu perumpāṉmaiyāyiṟṟu. aḵtu eṇkaṭitu eṉa varum.	(13)
‘muraṇ’ eṉum toḻiṟpeyar iyalpāy muṭital
309.	muraṇeṉ toḻiṟpeyar mutaliyal nilaiyum.
iḵtu, ivvīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyaruḷ oṉṟaṟkut toḻiṟ peyarkku eytiya ukaramum valleḻuttum vilakki ivvīṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipum vēṟṟumaimuṭipum eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) muraṇ eṉ toḻiṟpeyar - māṟupāṭu uṇarttum muraṇeṉṉun toḻiṟpeyar, mutaliyal nilaiyum - tokai marapiṟ kūṟiya alvaḻikkaṇ tiriyātu muṭinta iyalpiṉkaṇṇum (147) īṇṭu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ tirintu muṭinta iyalpiṉkaṇṇum nilaipeṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	muraṇkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñekiḻntatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, muraṭkaṭumai cēṉai tāṉai paṟai eṉavum, muraṇñekiḻcci nīṭci māṭci valimai eṉavum varum.
itaṉait ‘toḻiṟpeyarellām’ (eḻut. 306) eṉṟataṉ piṉ vaiyāta muṟaiyaṉṟik kūṟṟiṉāṉ, muraṇkaṭumai muraṭkaṭumai, araṇkaṭumai araṭkaṭumai eṉṉum uṟaḻcciyuṅ koḷka.	(14)
makara īṟṟuppeyaratu vēṟṟumaimuṭipu
310.	makara iṟuti vēṟṟumai yāyiṉ
	tuvarak keṭṭu valleḻuttu mikumē.
itu muṟaiyāṉē makara īṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumaikkaṭ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) makara iṟuti vēṟṟumai āyiṉ - makara īṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumaip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇāyiṉ, tuvarak keṭṭu valleḻuttu mikum - annilaimoḻi makaram muṟṟak keṭṭu varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	marakkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum. ‘muṇṭakakkōtai’ eṉavum varum.
