‘ammarapoḻukum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, aracakkaṉṉi, muracakkaṭippu eṉa akkum valleḻuttuṅ koṭuttum; aracavāḻkkai, muraca vāḻkkai eṉa akkuk koṭuttum muṭikka. iṉṉum ataṉāṉē iruṭṭattuk koṇṭāṉ, viḷakkattuk koṇṭāṉ eṉa attum valleḻuttuṅ koṭuttum; mayilāppiṟ koṟṟaṉ, paṟampiṟpāri eṉa iṉ koṭuttum; kariyataṉkōṭu, neṭiyataṉ kōṭu eṉa aṉkoṭuttum muṭikka.	(12)
akkuc cāriyai peṟum kuṟṟukara īṟu
418.	oṟṟunilai tiriyātu akkoṭu varūum
	akkiḷai moḻiyu muḷaveṉa moḻipa.
itu meṉṟoṭarmoḻiyuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakukkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) oṟṟu nilai tiriyātu akkoṭum varūum - oṟṟu muṉṉiṉṟa nilaitiriyātu akkuccāriyaiyōṭum piṟa cāriyaiyoṭum varum, akkiḷai moḻi uḷa eṉa moḻipa - akkiḷaiyāṉa coṟkaḷ uḷa eṉṟu colluvar āciriyar e-ṟu.
itaṟku ummaiyai akkoṭum eṉa muṉṉar māṟuka.
e-ṭu:	kuṉṟakkūkai, maṉṟappeṇṇai eṉavarum. ummaiyāṟ koṅkattuḻavu, vaṅkattuvāṇikam eṉa attum peṟṟaṉa.
‘nilai’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, oṟṟu nilaitiriyā atikārattukkaṇ varuñ cāriyaikku iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. ‘akkiḷai’ yeṉṟār, iraṇṭu cāriyai toṭarntu muṭivaṉavum uḷaveṉṟaṟku. pārppaṉakkuḻavi, cēri, tōṭṭam, piḷḷai eṉa aṉṉum akkum vantaṉa. ivaṟṟiṟku uṭaimai virikka. pārppiṉuṭkuḻavi eṉṟumām. pārppāṉākiyakuḻavi eṉṟāl īṇṭu muṭiyāteṉṟu uṇarka. pārppaṉa makaṉ, pārppaṉa vaṉitai eṉpaṉavum pārppāṉ cāti uṇarttiṉa.	(13)
kuṟṟukara īṟṟu eṇṇup peyar aṉcāriyai peṟumāṟu
419.	eṇṇuppeyark kiḷavi urupiyal nilaiyum.
itu kuṟṟukara īṟṟu eṇṇuppeyaroṭu poruṭpeyar muṭikkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) eṇṇup peyark kiḷavi - eṇṇuppeyarākiya coṟkaḷ poruṭpeyaroṭu puṇarumiṭattu, urupiyal nilaiyum - urupu puṇarcciyiṉ iyalpiṉkaṇṇē niṉṟu aṉpeṟṟup puṇarum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	oṉṟaṉkāyam, iraṇṭaṉkāyam, cukku, tōrai payaṟu eṉa oṭṭuka. oṉṟaṉāṟkoṇṭa kāyameṉa viriyum.
varumoḻi varaiyātu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ, iyalpu kaṇattukkaṇṇuṅ koḷka. oṉṟaṉñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu aṭai eṉa varum. mēlaic cūttirattu ‘nilai’ (eḻut. 418) eṉṟataṉāṉ, iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka.	(14)
‘vaṇṭu’, ‘peṇṭu’ ivaṟṟiṉ cāriyaippēṟu
420.	vaṇṭum peṇṭum iṉṉoṭu civaṇum.
itu meṉṟoṭar moḻiyuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkup piṟa muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) vaṇṭum peṇṭum iṉṉoṭu civaṇum - vaṇṭeṉṉuñ collum peṇṭeṉṉuñ collum iṉcāriyaiyoṭu porunti muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	vaṇṭiṉkāl peṇṭiṉkāl eṉa varum. itaṟku muṟkūṟiya nilai (418) eṉṟa ilēciṉāl valleḻuttu vīḻkka.	(15)
‘peṇṭu’ aṉṉum peṟutal
421.	peṇṭeṉ kiḷavik(ku) aṉṉum varaiyār.
itu mēṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) peṇṭu eṉ kiḷavikku aṉṉum varaiyār - peṇṭeṉ ṉuñ coṟku iṉṉēyaṉṟi aṉcāriyai varutalaiyum nīkkār āciriyar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	peṇṭaṉkai, cevi talai puṟam eṉa varum.	(16)
‘yātu’, cuṭṭumutal āytattoṭark kuṟṟukara īṟukaḷiṉ muṭipu
422.	yāteṉ iṟutiyuñ cuṭṭumuta lākiya
	āyta iṟutiyum urupiyal nilaiyum.
iḵtu īreḻuttorumoḻik kuṟṟiyalukarattuḷ oṉṟaṟkum, cuṭṭu mutalākiya āytat toṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟkum vēṟumuṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) yātu eṉ iṟutiyuñ cuṭṭu mutalākiya āyta iṟutiyum - yāteṉṉum īṟuñ cuṭṭeḻuttu mutalākiya āytat toṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukara īṟum, urupiyal nilaiyum - urupupuṇarcciyiṉ iyal piṉkaṇṇē niṉṟu aṉ peṟṟuc cuṭṭu mutaliṟuti āytaṅ keṭṭu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	yātaṉkōṭu, ataṉkōṭu, itaṉkōṭu, utaṉkōṭu; cevi, talai, puṟam eṉa varum.
āytaṅkeṭāmuṉṉē aṉṉiṉ akarattaik kuṟṟukarattiṉ mēl ēṟṟuka; āytaṅkeṭṭāl atu muṟṟukaramāy niṟṟaliṉ.	(17)
uyirvaruvaḻi, cuṭṭumutal āytattoṭark
kuṟṟukarattiṉ alvaḻimuṭipu
423.	muṉuyir varumiṭat tāytap puḷḷi
	maṉṉal vēṇṭum alvaḻi yāṉa.
itu muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuṭ cuṭṭu mutalākiya āyta iṟutikku oruvaḻi alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) muṉuyir varumiṭattu - cuṭṭu mutalākiya āytat toṭar moḻik kuṟṟukara īṟṟiṉmuṉṉē uyirmutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu, āytap puḷḷi maṉṉal vēṇṭum - āytavoṟṟu muṉpōlak keṭātu nilai peṟṟu muṭitalai virumpum āciriyaṉ, alvaḻiyāṉa - alvaḻikkaṇ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aḵtu, iḵtu, uḵtu eṉa niṟutti; aṭai āṭai ilai īyam ural ūrti eḻu ēṇi aiyam oṭukkam ōkkam auviyam eṉa oṭṭuka.
‘muṉ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum ivviti koḷka. aḵtaṭaivu, aḵtoṭṭam eṉa oṭṭuka. ivaṟṟiṟku iraṇṭāmurupu virikka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, ēṉai ilakkaṇam muṭiyumāṟu aṟintu muṭikka.	(18)
ēṉaikkaṇam varuvaḻi ataṉ muṭipu
424.	ēṉaimuṉ variṉē tāṉilai iṉṟē.
itu mēlavaṟṟiṟkup piṟakaṇattōṭu alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ēṉai muṉvariṉ - muṟkūṟiya īṟukaḷiṉ muṉṉar uyirkkaṇa mallaṉa varumāyiṉ, tāṉ nilaiyiṉṟu - avvāytaṅkeṭṭu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	atukaṭitu itukaṭitu utukaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu yātu valitu eṉa oṭṭuka.	(19)
kuṟṟukara īṟukaḷ alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu
425.	allatu kiḷappiṉ ellā moḻiyuñ
	colliya paṇpiṉ iyaṟkai yākum.
iḵtu āṟu īṟṟukkuṟṟukarattiṟkum alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) allatu kiḷappiṉ - alvaḻiyaic collumiṭattu, ellā moḻiyum - āṟu īṟṟuk kuṟṟukaramum, colliya paṇpiṉ iyaṟkai yākum - mēl āciriyaṉ kūṟiya kuṇattaiyuṭaiya iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nākukaṭitu, varakukaṭitu, teḷkukaṭitu, eḵkukaṭitu, kuraṅku kaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum. ēṉaikkaṇattuk kaṇ ‘niṉṟa coṉmu ṉiyal pākum’ (eḻut. 144) eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷka.
‘ellāmoḻiyum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, viṉaiccollum viṉaik kuṟippuc collum iyalpāy muṭital koḷka.
e-ṭu:	kiṭantatu kutirai, karitu kutirai eṉa varum.
‘colliya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, irupeyaroṭṭup paṇputtokai vaṉkaṇattukkaṇ iṉavoṟṟumikku valleḻuttup peṟṟu muṭitalum, iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ iṉavoṟṟu mikku muṭitalum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	karaṭṭukkāṉam, kuruṭṭukkōḻi, tiruṭṭuppulaiyaṉ, kaḷiṟṟuppaṉṟi, veḷiṟṟuppaṉai, eyiṟṟuppal eṉavum; vaṟaṭṭāṭu, kuruṭṭerutu eṉavum varum.
‘paṇpiṉ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, melloṟṟu valloṟṟāy aikāram peṟṟu muṭivaṉavum, melloṟṟu valloṟṟāy aikāramum valleḻuttum peṟṟu muṭivaṉavum, melloṟṟu valloṟṟākātu aikāramum valleḻuttum peṟṟu muṭivaṉavum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	ōr yāṭṭai yāṉai, aiyāṭṭai yerutu eṉavum; aṟṟaik kūttar, iṟṟaikkūttar eṉavum; maṉṟaittūtai maṉṟaip pāṉai, paṇṭaic cāṉṟār eṉavum varum.	(20)
avaṟṟuḷ vaṉtoṭar īṟu vali mikutal
426.	valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi valleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu av vāṟu īṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) valloṟṟut toṭar moḻi valleḻuttu mikum - valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻik kuṟṟukara īṟu valleḻuttu varuvaḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kokkukkaṭitu, pākkukkaṭitu, paṭṭukkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum.	(21)
valleḻuttu mikappeṟum meṉtoṭark kuṟṟukara moḻikaḷ
427.	cuṭṭuccuṉai nīṭiya meṉṟoṭar moḻiyum
	yāviṉā mutaliya meṉṟoṭar moḻiyum
	āyiyal tiriyā valleḻut tiyaṟkai.
iḵtu av vāṟu īṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṉkaṇ ēḻām vēṟṟumai iṭap poruḷ uṇara niṉṟa iṭaiccoṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭuc ciṉai nīṭiya meṉṟoṭar moḻiyum - cuṭṭākiya ciṉaiyeḻuttu nīṇṭa meṉṟoṭark kuṟṟukara īṟum, yāviṉā mutaliya meṉṟoṭar moḻiyum - yāveṉṉum viṉā mutalākiya meṉṟoṭar moḻik kuṟṟukara īṟum, valleḻuttiyaṟkai ā iyal tiriyā - valleḻuttup peṟṟu muṭiyun taṉmaiyākiya avviyalpiṉ tiriyātu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	āṅkukkoṇṭāṉ, īṅkukkoṇṭāṉ, ūṅkukkoṇ ṭāṉ, yāṅkukkoṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyi ṉāṉ eṉa varum.
‘iyaṟkai’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, meṉṟoṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu viṉaiyeccam iyalpāyum, vaṉṟoṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu viṉaiyeccam mikkum muṭivaṉa koḷka.
e-ṭu:	iruntukoṇṭāṉ, āṇṭuceṉṟāṉ, tantutīrntāṉ, vantupōyiṉāṉ eṉavum; cettukkiṭantāṉ, ceṟṟuc ceytāṉ, uyttukkoṇṭāṉ, naṭṭuppōṉāṉ eṉavum varum.	(22)
iyalpumuṭipu peṟum meṉtoṭar īṟu
428.	yāviṉā moḻiyē iyalpu mākum.
itu mēlaṉavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu, valleḻuttup peṟutalē aṉṟi iyalpā meṉṟaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) yāviṉā moḻiyē iyalpumākum  - avaṟṟuḷ yāveṉṉum viṉāvaiyuṭaiya col muṟkūṟiyavāṟaṉṟi iyalpāyum muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	yāṅkukoṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
iḵtu eppaṭiyeṉṉum viṉāpporuḷai uṇarttiṟṟu. ummaiyāṉ mikkumuṭitalē valiyuṭaittu. ēkāram pirinilai.	(23)
melloṟṟu valiyāk kuṟṟukara īṟṟu moḻikaḷ
429.	annāṉ moḻiyun tannilai tiriyā.
itu mēlaṉavaṟṟiṟku nilaimoḻic ceykai kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) annāṉmoḻiyum - cuṭṭumutaṉ mūṉṟum yāmutaṉ moḻiyu mākiya annāṉku moḻiyum, tam nilai tiriyā - tam melloṟṟāya taṉmai tirintu valloṟṟākātu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	muṟkāṭṭiyavē.
‘tannilai’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, melloṟṟut tiriyātu valleḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉa piṟavuṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu: aṅkukkoṇṭāṉ, iṅkukkoṇṭāṉ, uṅkukkoṇ ṭāṉ, eṅkukkoṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyi ṉāṉ eṉa varum.
iṉi muṉṉar yāmoḻi eṉṉātu viṉā eṉṟataṉāṉ, ēḻāvataṉ kālap poruṭṭākiya piṟavum iyalpāy muṭivaṉa koḷka.
e-ṭu:	muntu koṇṭāṉ, paṇṭu koṇṭāṉ, iṉṟu koṇ ṭāṉ, aṉṟu koṇṭāṉ, eṉṟu koṇṭāṉ eṉa varum.	(24)
uṇṭu eṉum viṉaikkuṟippu muṭiyumāṟu
430.	uṇṭeṉ kiḷavi uṇmai ceppiṉ
	muntai iṟuti meyyoṭuṅ keṭutalum
	mēṉilai yoṟṟē ḷakāra mātalum
	āmuṟai yiraṇṭum urimaiyu muṭaittē
	valleḻuttu varūuṅ kālai yāṉa.
itu meṉṟoṭar moḻiyuḷ viṉaikkuṟippāy niṉṟatoru coṟpaṇpai uṇarttuṅkāl vēṟumuṭipu peṟutal kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) uṇṭeṉ kiḷavi uṇmai ceppiṉ - uṇṭeṉṉuñ col viṉaikkuṟippai yuṇarttātu oru poruḷ tōṉṟuṅkāl tōṉṟi atu keṭuntuṇaiyum uṇṭāy niṟkiṉṟa taṉmaiyākiya paṇpai uṇartti niṟkumāyiṉ, muntai iṟuti meyyoṭuṅ keṭutalum - muṉṉar niṉṟa kuṟṟukaram tāṉ ēṟi niṉṟa meyyoṭuṅ keṭutalum, mēṉilai oṟṟē ḷakāra mātalum - ataṟku mēl niṉṟa ṇakāra oṟṟu ḷakāra oṟṟātalumākiya, ā muṟai iraṇṭum urimaiyum uṭaittu-ammuṟai yiṉaiyuṭaiya iraṇṭu nilaiyum urittu; aḵtu urittaṉṟi muṉṉar niṉṟa nilaiyilē kēṭun tiripum iṉṟi niṟṟalum uṭaittu, valleḻuttu varūuṅ kālaiyāṉa - valleḻuttu mutaṉ moḻiyāy varuṅkālattu e-ṟu.
valleḻuttu atikārattāṉ vārā niṟpa ‘valleḻuttu varūuṅ kālai’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, avvirumuṭipum uḷavām paṇpai yuṇarttum pakaram varumoḻikkaṇṇē variṉ eṉpatūum, ēṉaik kacatakkaḷilum iyalpukaṇattilum uṇṭeṉa niṉṟu viṉaikkuṟippāyuñ ciṟupāṉmai paṇpāyum niṟkumeṉpatūuṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	uḷporuḷ, uṇṭuporuḷ eṉa varum. itu poruḷaic cuṭṭātu uṇmait taṉmaip paṇpai īṇṭu uṇarttiṟṟu. iṉi, uṇṭukāṇam, uṇṭucākkāṭu, uṇṭu tāmarai, uṇṭuñāṉam; nūl, maṇi, yāḻ, vaṭṭu, aṭai, āṭai eṉa varuvaṉavellāṅ kēṭun tiripumiṉṟi viṉaikkuṟippāyuñ ciṟupāṉmai paṇpāyum niṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu viṉaiyiyaluḷ viṉaikkuṟippu ōtumvaḻi uṇarka.
uḷporuḷeṉpatu paṇputtokai muṭipaṉṟōveṉiṉ, aḵtu ōcai oṟṟumaipaṭac collum vaḻiyatu pōlum. iḵtu ōcai iṭaiyaṟavupaṭac collum vaḻiyateṉka.	(25)
iruperunticai alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu
431.	iruticai puṇariṉ ēyiṭai varumē.
itu kuṟṟukara īṟṟut ticaippeyarkku alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) iru ticai puṇariṉ - iraṇṭu perunticaiyun tammiṟ puṇariṉ, ē iṭai varum - ēyeṉṉuñ cāriyai iṭai niṉṟu puṇarum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	teṟkēvaṭakku, vaṭakkēteṟku ivai ummait tokai.	(26)
perunticaikaḷoṭu kōṇatticai puṇartal
432.	tiripuvēṟu kiḷappiṉ oṟṟum ukaramuṅ
	keṭutal vēṇṭum eṉmaṉār pulavar
	oṟṟumey tirintu ṉakāra mākum
	teṟkoṭu puṇaruṅ kālai yāṉa.
itu perunticaikaḷoṭu kōṇatticaikaḷaip puṇarttaliṉ, eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) tiripu vēṟu kiḷappiṉ - apperun ticaikaḷoṭu kōṇat ticaikaḷai vēṟākap puṇarkku miṭattu, oṟṟum ukaramuṅ keṭutal vēṇṭum eṉmaṉār pulavar - avvukaram ēṟi niṉṟa oṟṟum avvīṟṟu ukaramuṅ keṭṭumuṭital vēṇṭumeṉṟu colluvar pulavar, teṟkoṭu puṇaruṅkālai - avai teṟkeṉṉun ticaiyoṭu puṇaruṅkālattu, āṉa oṟṟu mey tirintu ṉakāramākum - atticaikkup porunti niṉṟa ṟakara oṟṟut taṉ vaṭivutirintu ṉakara oṟṟāy niṟkum e-ṟu.
‘tirintu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, vaṭakku eṉpataṉkaṇ niṉṟa kakara oṟṟuk keṭuttu muṭittuk koḷka.
e-ṭu:	vaṭakiḻakku, vaṭamēṟku, teṉkiḻakku, teṉmēṟku eṉa varum.
‘vēṟu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ticaippeyaroṭu poruṭpeyar variṉum immuṭipu koḷka.
e-ṭu:	vaṭakaṭal, vaṭacuram, ‘vaṭavēṅkaṭam, teṉkumari’ (pāyiram), teṉcuram, teṉṉilaṅkai eṉa varum.
‘mey’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, uyir keṭṭun tirintum mey keṭṭum muṭivaṉavum uḷa, ticaippeyar muṉṉarp poruṭpeyar vantuḻi yeṉṟu uṇarka. kiḻakku eṉpatu, karai kūrai eṉpavaṟṟoṭu puṇarumiṭattuk kīḻkarai kīḻkūrai eṉa nilaimoḻiyiṟuti ukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭṭu ataṉmēl niṉṟa kakara oṟṟum ḻakarattiṉ akaramuṅkeṭṭu mutaleḻuttu nīṇṭu muṭintaṉa. mēṟkukarai, kūrai, mīkarai mīkūrai eṉa nilaimoḻi īṟṟu ukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭṭu ataṉmēl niṉṟa ṟakara oṟṟuṅ keṭṭu ēkāram īkāramāki muṭintaṉa. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē. mēṉmāṭu mēlpāl mēlaiccēri eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉavuñ ceykaiyaṟintu muṭikka.	(27)
pattu, mutal eṭṭu eṇkaḷoṭu puṇarumāṟu
433.	oṉṟumuta lāka eṭṭa ṉiṟuti
	ellā veṇṇum pattaṉ muṉvariṟ
	kuṟṟiya lukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭumē
	muṟṟaiṉ varūum iraṇṭalaṅ kaṭaiyē.
niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē āṟu īṟṟuk kuṟṟukaramum poruṭ peyarkkaṇ puṇarumāṟu uṇartti, iṉi avvīṟṟu eṇṇuppeyar muṭikkiṉṟār. iḵtu avaṟṟuṭ patteṉṉum eṇṇup peyarōṭu eṇṇuppeyar vantu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) oṉṟu mutalāka eṭṭaṉ iṟuti ellā eṇṇum - oṉṟeṉṉum eṇ mutalāka eṭṭeṉṉum eṇṇīṟāyuḷḷa ellā eṇṇup peyarkaḷum, pattaṉmuṉ variṉ - patteṉṉum eṇṇup peyariṉ muṉvariṉ, kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭum - appat teṉṉuñ colliṟ kuṟṟiyalukaran tāṉ ēṟi niṉṟa meyyoṭuṅ keṭum, iraṇṭalaṅkaṭai muṟṟa iṉvarūum - āṇṭu iraṇṭallāta eṇṇup peyarkaḷiṭattu muṭiya iṉcāriyai iṭaivantu puṇarum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	patiṉoṉṟu, patiṉmūṉṟu, patiṉāṉku, patiṉaintu, patiṉāṟu, patiṉēḻu, patiṉeṭṭu eṉa varum.
nilaimoḻi muṟkūṟātataṉāṉ, piṟaveṇṇiṉ muṉṉarp piṟa peyar vantuḻiyum iṉpeṟutal koḷka.
e-ṭu:	oṉpatiṉkūṟu, oṉpatiṉpāl eṉa varum.
‘muṟṟa’ eṉpataṉāṟ, patiṉāṉkeṉpuḻi vanta iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram varumoḻikkaṭ karuviceytu keṭuttu muṭikka.	(28)
avveṇṇuppeyar ‘iraṇṭu’ varuvaḻi muṭiyumāṟu
434.	pattaṉoṟ ṟukkeṭa ṉakāram iraṭṭal
	otta teṉpa iraṇṭuvaru kālai.
itu mēl iṉ peṟāteṉṟu vilakkiya iraṇṭaṟkup piṟitu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) pattaṉ oṟṟuk keṭa ṉakāram iraṭṭal - patteṉṉuñ collil niṉṟa takara oṟṟukkeṭa ṉakara oṟṟu iraṭṭittu varutal, iraṇṭu varukālai ottateṉpa - iraṇṭeṉṉum eṇ varuṅkālattiṟ poruntiṟ ṟeṉpar āciriyar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	paṉṉiraṇṭu eṉa varum.
‘kuṟṟiya lukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭum’ (eḻut. 433) eṉṟa viti itaṟkum mēlaṉavaṟṟiṟkuṅ koḷka.	(29)
‘āyiram’ varuvaḻi, atu muṭiyumāṟu
435.	āyiram variṉum āyiyal tiriyātu.
iḵtu āyirameṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar variṉ varum muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) āyiram variṉum āyiyal tiriyātu - muṟkūṟiya pattaṉ muṉṉar oṉṟu mutaliyaṉavēyaṉṟi āyirameṉṉu meṇ vantālum īṟukeṭṭu iṉpeṟṟu muṭiyum iyalpiṉ tiriyātu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	patiṉāyiram eṉa varum.
ummai iṟantatu taḻīiyiṟṟu.	(30)
niṟaiyum aḷavum varuvaḻi, atu muṭiyumāṟu
436.	niṟaiyu maḷavum varūuṅ kālaiyum
	kuṟaiyā tākum iṉṉeṉ cāriyai.
iḵtu eṇṇuppeyaroṭu niṟaippeyarum aḷavuppeyarum puṇarkiṉṟa puṇarcci kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) niṟaiyum aḷavum varūuṅ kālaiyum - muṟkūṟiya patteṉ pataṉ muṉṉar niṟaippeyarum aḷavuppeyarum varuṅkālattum, iṉṉeṉ cāriyai kuṟaiyātākum - avviṉṉeṉṉuñ cāriyai kuṟaiyātu vantu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	patiṉkaḻañcu, toṭi, palam eṉavum; patiṉkalam, cāṭi, tūtai, pāṉai, nāḻi, maṇṭai, vaṭṭi eṉavum; patiṟṟakal, patiṟṟuḻakku eṉavum varum.
‘kuṟaiyātākum’ eṉpataṉāṟ, poruṭpeyarum eṇṇup peyarum niṟaippeyarum vantuḻiyum iṉ koṭuttu vēṇṭuñ ceykai ceytu muṭikka.
e-ṭu:	patiṟṟu vēli, yāṇṭu, aṭukku, muḻam eṉavum, patiṉ tiṅkaḷ eṉavum, patiṟṟuttoṭi eṉavum varum. patiṟ ṟoṉṟu eṉpatupōla iraṇṭumutaṟ pattaḷavum oṭṭuka.
ivvīṟṟiṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramātal ‘aḷavākumoḻimutal’ (eḻut. 121) eṉpataṉuḷ ‘nilaiiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ muṭikka, ivaṟṟiṟku oṟṟiraṭṭutalum ukaram varutalum valleḻuttup peṟutalum ‘oṉṟu mutalāka’ (eḻut. 433) eṉpataṉuḷ ‘muṟṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷka.	(31)
oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉ eṇṇup peyar,
‘pattu’ varuvaḻi muṭiyumāṟu
437.	oṉṟumuta loṉpāṉ iṟuti muṉṉar
	niṉṟa patta ṉoṟṟukkeṭa āytam
	vantiṭai nilaiyum iyaṟkait teṉpa
	kūṟiya iyaṟkai kuṟṟiya lukaram
	āṟa ṉiṟuti alvaḻi yāṉa.
iḵtu oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉ eṇṇuppeyaroṭu patteṉṉum eṇṇup peyarkku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉ iṟutimuṉṉar - oṉṟu mutal oṉpatu īṟākak kūṟukiṉṟa eṇṇup peyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉar, niṉṟa pattaṉ oṟṟukkeṭa - varumoḻiyāka vantuniṉṟa patteṉṉuñ colliṉatu takara oṟṟukkeṭa, āytam vantu iṭai nilaiyum iyaṟkait teṉpa - āyta māṉatu vantu iṭaiyē nilaipeṟum iyalpai yuṭaitteṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar, āṟaṉ iṟuti alvaḻiyāṉa - avaṟṟuḷ āṟeṉṉum īṟallāta viṭattu, kuṟṟiyalukaram kūṟiya iyaṟkai - kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟkūṟiya iyaṟkaiyāy meyyoṭuṅkeṭṭu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
iṅṅaṉam varumāṟu mēṟcūttiraṅkaḷuṭ kāṭṭutum.
‘vantu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, āytamākat tiriyātu takara oṟṟuk keṭṭu orupatu eṉṟu niṟṟaluṅ koḷka.	(32)
oṉṟu iraṇṭu, oru iru - eṉat tirital
438.	mutalī reṇṇiṉoṟṟu rakara mākum
	ukaram varutal āvayi ṉāṉa.
itu mēṟkūṟiyavaṟṟiṉ cilavaṟṟiṟku nilaimoḻicceykai kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) mutalī reṇṇiṉ oṟṟu rakaram ākum - avaṟṟiṉ mutaṟkaṇ niṉṟa iraṇṭeṇṇiṉuṭaiya ṉakara oṟṟum ṇakara oṟṟum rakara oṟṟākat tirintu niṟkum, āvayiṉāṉa ukaram varutal - avviṭattu ukaram varuka e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	orupaḵtu irupaḵtu eṉa varum.
oṉṟeṉpataṉ īṟṟuk kuṟṟukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭuttu, ṉakara oṟṟiṉai rakara oṟṟākki, ukaramēṟṟi, oruveṉa niṟutti, niṉṟa patteṉpataṉ takara oṟṟuk keṭuttu, āytamākkip, paḵteṉa varuvittu, orupaḵtu eṉa muṭikka. irupaḵtaiyum ivvāṟē muṭikka. mēl varuvaṉavaṟṟiṟkuñ cūttiraṅkaḷāṟ kūṟuñ ciṟappuviti oḻintavaṟṟiṟku ituvē muṭipākak koḷka.	(33)
439.	iṭainilai rakaram iraṇṭeṉ eṇṇiṟku
	naṭaimaruṅ kiṉṟē poruḷvayi ṉāṉa.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) iraṇṭeṉ eṇṇiṟku iṭainilai rakaram - iraṇ ṭeṉṉu meṇṇiṟku iṭainiṉṟa rakaram, poruḷvayiṉāṉa - ammoḻi poruḷpeṟum iṭattu, naṭaimaruṅkiṉṟu - naṭakkum iṭamiṉṟik keṭum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	irupaḵtu eṉa varum.
itaṟku rakara uyirmey itaṉāṟkeṭuttu ēṉaiya kūṟiyavāṟē kūṭṭi muṭikka. ‘poruḷ’ eṉavē, eṇṇallāp peyaruṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	irukaṭal, iruviṉai, irupiṟappu eṉa varum.	(34)
mūṉṟum āṟum, muṉ aṟu - eṉat tirital
440.	mūṉṟum āṟum neṭumutal kuṟukum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) mūṉṟum āṟum neṭumutal kuṟukum - mūṉṟeṉṉum eṇṇum āṟeṉṉum eṇṇum neṭumutal kuṟuki muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aṟu eṉak kuṟukkip paḵtu eṉa varuvittu, aṟupaḵtu eṉa muṭikka.	(35)
mūṉṟaṉ ṉakāram pakāram ātal
441.	mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē pakāra mākum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) mūṉṟaṉ oṟṟē pakāramākum - mūṉṟeṉṉum eṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa ṉakāra oṟṟup pakara oṟṟāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	muppaḵtu eṉa varum.	(36)
nāṉkaṉ ṉakāram ṟakāram ātal
442.	nāṉka ṉoṟṟē ṟakāra mākum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) nāṉkaṉ oṟṟē ṟakāramākum - nāṉkeṉṉum eṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu ṟakara oṟṟāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nāṟpaḵtu eṉa varum.	(37)
aintaṉ nakāram makāram ātal
443.	ainta ṉoṟṟē makāra mākum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) aintaṉoṟṟē makāramākum - ainteṉṉum eṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa nakara oṟṟu makara oṟṟāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aimpaḵtu eṉa varum.
ēḻu kuṟṟiyalukara īṟaṉmai urupiyaluṭ kāṇka.	(38)
eṭṭaṉ ṭakāram ṇakāram ātal
444.	eṭṭa ṉoṟṟē ṇakāra mākum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) eṭṭa ṉoṟṟē ṇakāramākum - eṭṭeṉṉum eṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa ṭakara oṟṟu ṇakara oṟṟāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	eṇpaḵtu eṉa varum.	(39)
oṉpaḵtu ‘pattu’ varuvaḻi muṭiyumāṟu
445.	oṉpāṉ okaramicait takaram oṟṟum
	muntai yoṟṟē ṇakāram iraṭṭum
	paḵteṉ kiḷavi āytapaka raṅkeṭa
	niṟṟal vēṇṭum ūkārak kiḷavi
	oṟṟiya takaram ṟakara mākum.
iḵtu eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) oṉpāṉ okaramicait takaram oṟṟum - oṉpatu eṉa niṟuttip paḵtu eṉa varuvittu muṭikkuṅkāl, nilaimoḻiyākiya oṉpateṉṉum eṇṇiṉatu okarattiṟku muṉṉāka oru takara oṟṟut tōṉṟi niṟkum, muntai oṟṟē ṇakāram iraṭṭum - muṉ coṉṉa okarattiṟku muṉṉar niṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu ṇakara oṟṟāy iraṭṭittu niṟkum, paḵteṉ kiḷavi āyta pakaraṅkeṭa - varumoḻiyākiya paḵteṉṉuñ col taṉkaṇ āytamum pakaramuṅ keṭṭuppōka, ūkārak kiḷavi niṟṟal vēṇṭum - nilai moḻiyiṉ iraṭṭiya ṇakarattiṉ piṉṉar ūkāramākiya eḻuttu vantu niṟṟalai āciriyaṉ virumpum, oṟṟiya takaram ṟakara mākum - varumoḻiyākiya patteṉpataṉ īṟṟiṉmēlēṟiya ukaraṅ keṭātu pirintuniṟpa oṟṟāy niṉṟa takaram ṟakara oṟṟāy niṟkum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	toṇṇūṟu eṉa varum.
itaṉai oṟṟāy vantu niṉṟa takara oṟṟiṉmēl nilaimoḻi okarattai ēṟṟit tovvākki, ṇakara oṟṟu iraṭṭi ataṉmēl varumoḻikkaṭ pakaramum āytamuṅkeṭa vanta ūkāramēṟṟit toṇṇū ṟākkip, ‘pakaravāytam’ eṉṉāta muṟaiyaṉṟik kūṟṟiṉāṉ, nilaimoḻik kaṭ pakaramum āytamuṅ keṭuttuk, kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭuttu, varumoḻi iṟutit takara oṟṟut tirintu niṉṟa ṟakara oṟṟiṉmēl niṉṟa ukaramēṟṟit toṇṇūṟeṉa muṭikka.	(40)
oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉ eṇkaḷōṭu aḷavuniṟaippeyarkaḷ muṭiyumāṟu
446.	aḷantaṟi kiḷaviyum niṟaiyiṉ kiḷaviyum
	kiḷanta iyala tōṉṟuṅ kālai.
itu, mēṟkūṟiya oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉeṇkaḷōṭu aḷavup peyarum niṟaippeyarum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) aḷantaṟi kiḷaviyum niṟaiyiṉ kiḷaviyun tōṉṟuṅ kālai - muṟkūṟiya oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷiṉ muṉṉar aḷantaṟiyappaṭum aḷavup peyarum niṟaippeyarum vantu tōṉṟuṅ kālattu, kiḷanta iyala-āṟaṉ īṟu alvaḻik kuṟṟukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭṭu, mutalīreṇṇiṉoṟṟu rakāramāy ukaram vantu, iṭainilai rakara miraṇṭiṟkuk keṭṭu mūṉṟum āṟum neṭumutal kuṟuki, nāṉkaṉoṟṟu vaṉkaṇattu ṟakaramāy, eṭṭaṉoṟṟu ṇakaramāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	orukalam, irukalam; cāṭi, tūtai, pāṉai, nāḻi, maṇṭai, vaṭṭi eṉavum; orukaḻañcu, irukaḻañcu, kaḵcu, toṭi, palam eṉavum varum. akal uḻakku eṉpaṉa muṉṉar muṭittum. ivai mutalīreṇṇiṉ ceykai.
‘tōṉṟuṅ kālai’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ivveṇṇiṉ muṉṉar eṭuttōttāṉum ilēcāṉum muṭiyātu niṉṟa eṇṇup peyarka ḷellām ivvitiyum piṟavitiyum eytuvittu muṭittuk koḷka. oru mūṉṟu orunāṉku, irumūṉṟu irunāṉku, orukāl irukāl, oru muntirikai irumuntirikai, orumukkāl iru mukkāl eṉpaṉa piṟavuṅ koṇarntu oṭṭuka.
iṉip piṟaviti eytuvaṉa: ōroṉṟu, ōriraṇṭu, ōraintu, ōrāṟu, ōrēḻu, ōreṭṭu, ōroṉpatu eṉavum; īroṉṟu, īriraṇṭu, īraintu, īrāṟu, īrēḻu, īreṭṭu, īroṉpatu eṉavum; mūvoṉṟu, mūviraṇṭu, mūvaintu, mūvāṟu, mūvēḻu, mūveṭṭu, mūvoṉpatu eṉavum; ‘mutalī reṇṇiṉmuṉ uyir’ (eḻut. 455) eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṉ uyirkku eytiya piṟavitiyum, ‘mūṉṟaṉ mutaṉilai nīṭalu murittu’ (eḻut. 457) eṉṟa piṟavitiyum peṟṟup, piṟaceykaikaḷum peṟṟu muṭintaṉa.
nāloṉṟu, nāliraṇṭu, nālaintu, nālāṟu, nālēḻu, nāleṭṭu, nāloṉpatu eṉpaṉa ‘nāṉka ṉoṟṟē lakāra mākum’ (eḻut. 453) eṉṟa vitipeṟṟu muṭintaṉa. piṟavum ivvāṟēyaṉṟi aḷavum niṟaiyumaṉṟi varuvaṉavellām ivvilēcāṉ muṭikka.	(41)
mūṉṟaṉ ṉakāram vanta valloṟ ṟātal
447.	mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē vanta tokkum.
itu mēl māṭṭēṟṟōṭu ovvātataṟku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) mūṉṟaṉoṟṟē vantatokkum - mūṉṟāmeṇṇiṉka ṇiṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu varumoḻiyāy vanta aḷavup peyar niṟaippeyar kaḷiṉ muṉṉar vanta valloṟṟōṭu otta oṟṟāyt tirintu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	mukkalam, cāṭi, tūtai, pāṉai eṉavum; muk kaḻañcu, kaḵcu, toṭi, palam eṉavum varum.
‘nāṉka ṉoṟṟē ṟakāra mākum’ (eḻut. 442) eṉṟa muṉṉai māṭṭēṟu niṟṟaliṉ, nāṟkalam, cāṭi, tūtai, pāṉai eṉavum; nāṟ kaḻañcu, toṭi, palam, eṉavum varum.	(42)
aintaṉ nakāram, vanta valloṟṟiṟkēṟṟa meliyākat tirital
448.	ainta ṉoṟṟē melleḻut tākum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) aintaṉoṟṟē melleḻuttākum - aintāvataṉkaṇ niṉṟa nakara oṟṟu varumoḻi valleḻuttukku ēṟṟa melleḻuttākat tirintu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aiṅkalam, cāṭi, tūtai, pāṉai eṉavum; aiṅkaḻañcu, toṭi, palam eṉavum varum. ēkāram īṟṟacai.	(43)
ittiripukaḷ vaṉkaṇam varuvaḻiyē eṉṟal
449.	kacatapa mutaṉmoḻi varūuṅ kālai.
itu muṟkūṟiya mūṉṟaṟkum aintaṟkum varumoḻi varaiyaṟuk kiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) kacatapa mutaṉmoḻi varūuṅ kālai - mūṉṟaṉoṟṟu vantatoppatūum aintaṉoṟṟu melleḻuttāvatūum avvaḷavup peyar niṟaippeyar oṉpatiṉum vaṉkaṇamākiya kacatapakkaḷ mutaṉmoḻi yāy vanta iṭattu e-ṟu.
atu muṉṉark kāṭṭiṉām.
‘āṟu neṭumutal kuṟukum’ (eḻut. 440) eṉṟa māṭṭēṟṟāṉē āṟu neṭumutal kuṟuki niṉṟatu.
e-ṭu:	aṟukalam, cāṭi, tūtai, pāṉai, nāḻi, maṇṭai, vaṭṭi eṉavum; aṟukaḻañcu, toṭi, palam eṉavum varum. akal uḻakku eṉpaṉa mēṟ kāṭṭutum.
ēḻ kuṟṟukara īṟaṉmaiyiṉ māṭṭēṟu ēlātāyiṟṟu.	(44)
meṉkaṇamum iṭaikkaṇamum varumvaḻiyum,
‘eṭṭu’ eṇ - eṉavē niṟṟal
450.	namava eṉṉum mūṉṟoṭu civaṇi
	akaram variṉum eṭṭaṉmu ṉiyalpē.
itu vēṇṭātu kūṟi vēṇṭiyatu muṭikkiṉṟatu, ‘ñanamayava’ (eḻut. 144) mutaliya cūttirattuṭ kūṟiyavaṟṟaik kūṟutaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) eṭṭaṉmuṉ - eṭṭeṉpataṉ muṉṉar, namava eṉṉum mūṉṟoṭu civaṇi akaram variṉum - aḷavuppeyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉar meṉkaṇattu iraṇṭum iṭaikkaṇattu oṉṟumākiya namava eṉṉum mūṉṟaṉoṭu porunti uyirkkaṇattu akaram variṉum, ummaiyāṉ uyirkkaṇattu ukaram variṉum kūṟāta valleḻuttukkaḷ variṉum, iyalpu - muṟkūṟiyavāṟē ṭakāram ṇakāramāy vēṟoru vikāramiṉṟi iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
namavaveṉṉum mūṉṟum vantāṟpōla, akaram variṉum eṉpatu poruḷ.
e-ṭu:	eṇṇāḻi, maṇṭai vaṭṭi eṉavum; eṇṇakal, eṇ ṇuḻakku eṉavum, eṇkalam, cāṭi tūtai pāṉai eṉavum varum.
‘oṉṟeṉa muṭittalāṉ’ vaṉkaṇattu niṟaippeyaruṅ koḷka. eṇkaḻañcu; toṭi, palam eṉa varum.
ivvēṇṭā kūṟalāṉ, eṇṇakal eṉak kuṟṟukara īṟāyk kēṭuntirivum peṟṟu uyirvarumoḻiyāṉa toṭarmoḻikkaṇ oṟṟiraṭṭutal koḷka.	(45)
nama - varuvaḻi, aintu mūṉṟu ivaṟṟiṉ oṟṟu, vanta oṟṟātal
451.	aintum mūṉṟum namavaru kālai
	vanta tokkum oṟṟiyal nilaiyē.
ituvum mēl māṭṭēṟṟōṭu ovvā muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) aintum mūṉṟum nama varukālai - ainteṉṉum eṇṇum mūṉṟeṉṉum eṇṇum nakara mutaṉmoḻiyum makara mutaṉmoḻiyum varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, oṟṟiyal nilai - nilaimoḻikkaṇ niṉṟa oṟṟu naṭakkum nilaimai kūṟiṉ, vantatu okkum - mēṟkūṟiyavāṟē makaramum pakaramum ākātu varumoḻi mutal vanta oṟṟōṭu otta oṟṟāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ainnāḻi, aimmaṇṭai, munnāḻi, mummaṇṭai eṉa varum.
‘mūṉṟum aintum’ eṉṉāta muṟaiyaṉṟik kūṟṟiṉāṉ, nāṉāḻi nāṉmaṇṭai eṉpuḻi nilaimoḻi ṉakaram ṟakaramākātu niṉṟavāṟē niṉṟu muṭitalum, varumoḻi mutaṉiṉṟa nakaram ṉakaramāyt tiriya nilaimoḻi ṉakaraṅ keṭutaluṅ koḷka.	(46)
vakaram varuvaḻi, mūṉṟaṉ ṉakāram vakāramātal
452.	mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē vakaram varuvaḻit
	tōṉṟiya vakarat turuvā kummē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) mūṉṟaṉoṟṟu - mūṉṟām eṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu, vakaram varuvaḻi - vakaramutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu, tōṉṟiya vakarattu uruvākum - avvarumoḻiyāyt tōṉṟiya vakarattiṉ vaṭivāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	muvvaṭṭi eṉa varum.
‘tōṉṟiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉē, mutal nīṇṭu vakara oṟṟiṉṟi mūvaṭṭi eṉṟumām.	(47)
nāṉkaṉ ṉakāram lakāramātal
453.	nāṉka ṉoṟṟē lakāra mākum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) nāṉkaṉoṟṟu - nāṉkām eṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu, lakāramākum - vakara mutaṉmoḻi vantāl lakara oṟṟākat tirintu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nālvaṭṭi eṉa varum.	(48)
aintaṉ nakāram keṭutal
454.	ainta ṉoṟṟē muntaiyatu keṭumē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) aintaṉoṟṟu-aintām eṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa nakara oṟṟu, muntaiyatu keṭum-vakara mutaṉmoḻi vantāl muṉṉiṉṟa vaṭivu keṭṭu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aivaṭṭi eṉa varum.
‘muntai’ yeṉṟataṉāṉ, nakara oṟṟuk keṭātu vakara oṟṟākat tirintu aivvaṭṭiyeṉac ciṟupāṉmai varum.	(49)
uyir varuvaḻi, mutal īreṇṇum ōr, īr - ātal
455.	mutalī reṇṇiṉmuṉ uyirvaru kālait
	tavaleṉa moḻipa ukarak kiḷavi
	mutaṉilai nīṭa lāvayi ṉāṉa.
itu māṭṭēṟṟāṉ eytiya ukarattiṟkuk kēṭu kūṟi mutal nīḷka eṉṟaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) mutalīreṇṇiṉmuṉ uyir varukālai - oru iru eṉa muṭintu niṉṟa iraṇṭeṇṇiṉ muṉṉar uyir mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻi yāy varuṅkālattu, ukarakkiḷavi tavaleṉa moḻipa - nilaimoḻiyukara mākiya eḻuttuk keṭutalāmeṉṟu colluvar pulavar, āvayiṉāṉa mutaṉilai nīṭal - avviraṇṭeṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa mutaleḻuttukkaḷ nīṇṭu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ōrakal, īrakal, ōruḻakku, īruḻakku eṉa varum.	(50)
mūṉṟu nāṉku aintu ivaṟṟiṉ oṟṟu, vakaram varuvaḻik kūṟiya iyalpiṉa ātal
456.	mūṉṟum nāṉkum ainteṉ kiḷaviyum
	tōṉṟiya vakarat tiyaṟkai yākum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) mūṉṟum nāṉkum ainteṉ kiḷaviyum - mūṉṟeṉṉum eṇṇum, nāṉkeṉṉum eṇṇum ainteṉṉum eṇṇum, tōṉṟiya vakarattu iyaṟkaiyākum - muṉṉart tōṉṟi niṉṟa vakaram varumoḻikkuk kūṟiya iyalpāka mūṉṟiṉkaṇ vakara oṟṟāyum, nāṉkiṉkaṇ lakara oṟṟāyum, aintiṉkaṇ oṟṟukkeṭṭum muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	muvvakal, muvvuḻakku eṉa varum. itaṟkut ‘tōṉṟiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, oṟṟiraṭṭutal koḷka. nālakal, nāluḻakku, aiyakal, aiyuḻakku eṉa varum.
‘tōṉṟiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, mēl mūṉṟeṉpatu mutal nīṇṭa iṭattu nilaimoḻi ṉakara oṟṟuk keṭuttukkoḷka. ‘iyaṟkai’ eṉṟata ṉāṉ, toṭarmoḻikkaṇ oṟṟiraṭṭutal koḷka. ‘mūṉṟaṉoṟṟē’ (eḻut. 452) mutaliya mūṉṟu cūttiramum koṇarntu muṭikka.	(51)
‘uḻakku’ varuvaḻi, neṭumutal kuṟukiya ‘mūṉṟu’ nīṇṭu muṭital
457.	mūṉṟaṉ mutaṉilai nīṭalu murittē
	uḻakkeṉ kiḷavi vaḻakkat tāṉa.
itu muṉṉark kuṟukumeṉṟataṉai nīṇṭu muṭika eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mūṉṟaṉ mutaṉnilai nīṭalum urittu - mūṉṟeṉṉum eṇṇiṉ mutaṉiṉṟa eḻuttu nīṇṭu muṭitalum urittu, aḵtiyāṇ ṭeṉiṉ, uḻakku eṉ kiḷavi vaḻakkattāṉa - uḻakkeṉṉuñ col muṭiyum vaḻakkiṭattu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	mūvuḻakku eṉa varum.
‘vaḻakkattāṉa’ eṉpataṉāṉ, akaleṉ kiḷavikku mutaṉilai nīṭaluṅ koḷka. mūvakal eṉa varum. iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, nilaimoḻi ṉakara oṟṟuk keṭukka.
mūḻakku mūḻākku eṉṉum marūumuṭivu ivvōttiṉ puṟaṉaṭai yāṉ muṭikka.	(52)
neṭumutal kuṟukiya ‘āṟu’ nīṇṭu muṭital
458.	āṟeṉ kiḷavi mutalnī ṭummē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) āṟeṉ kiḷavi - āṟeṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar akal uḻakku eṉpaṉa variṉ, mutal nīṭum - muṉṉark kuṟuki niṉṟa mutaleḻuttu nīṇṭu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
‘aṟu’ eṉṉātu ‘āṟu’ eṉṟār, tirintataṉtiripatu eṉṉum nayattāl.
e-ṭu:	āṟakal, āṟuḻakku eṉa varum.	(53)
aḷavu niṟaippeyar varuvaḻi, ‘oṉpatu’ ‘iṉ’ peṟutal
459.	oṉpā ṉiṟuti uruvunilai tiriyā(tu)
	iṉpeṟal vēṇṭuñ cāriyai moḻiyē.
itu kuṟṟukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭātu niṉṟu iṉ peṟuka eṉṟaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakukkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) oṉpāṉ iṟuti uruvunilai tiriyātu - aḷavum niṟaiyum varuvaḻi oṉpateṉṉum eṇṇiṉ iṟutik kuṟṟukaran taṉ vaṭivu nilai tiriyātu niṉṟu, cāriyai moḻi iṉpeṟal vēṇṭum - cāriyaic collākiya iṉpeṟṟu muṭitalai virumpum āciriyaṉ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	oṉpatiṉkalam, cāṭi, tūtai, pāṉai, nāḻi, maṇṭai, vaṭṭi, akal, uḻakku eṉavum; kaḻañcu, toṭi, palam eṉavum varum.
‘cāriyai moḻi’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, iṉṉōṭu ukaramum valleḻut tum koṭuttuc ceykai ceytu muṭikka. oṉpatiṟṟukkalam, cāṭi eṉa ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka.
‘uruvu’ eṉpataṉāṉ, oṉpatiṟṟeṉa oṟṟiraṭṭutal ellāvaṟ ṟiṟkum koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, oṉpatiṉāḻi yeṉpuḻi vanta iṉṉiṉ ṉakarak kēṭuṅ koḷka. ‘aḷavāku moḻi mutal’ (eḻut. 121) eṉpataṉkaṇ, ‘nilaiiya’eṉṉum ilēcāṉ iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramātal koḷka.	(54)
oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉ eṇkaḷoṭu ‘nūṟu’ puṇarumāṟu
460.	nūṟumuṉ variṉuṅ kūṟiya iyalpē.
iḵtu oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷoṭu nūṟeṉpataṉaip puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) muṉ - oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷiṉ muṉṉar, nūṟuvariṉum - nūṟeṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar vantālum, kūṟiya iyalpu - mēṟ patteṉpataṉoṭu puṇarumvaḻik kūṟiya iyalpu eyti muṭiyum e-ṟu.
atu kuṟṟukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭṭu mūṉṟum āṟum neṭumutal kuṟuki, mutalīreṇṇi ṉoṟṟu rakaramāy ukaram peṟṟu, iṭainilai rakaram iraṇṭaṉkaṭ keṭṭu muṭitalām.
e-ṭu:	orunūṟu, irunūṟu, aṟunūṟu, eṇṇūṟu eṉa varum.
ivai māṭṭēṟṟāṉ muṭintaṉa. māṭṭēṟu ovvātaṉa mēṟkūṟi muṭippa.	(55)
mūṉṟaṉ ṉakāram nakāramātal
461.	mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē nakāra mākum.
itu māṭṭēṟṟōṭu ovvātataṟku vēṟumuṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē nakāramākum - mūṉṟām eṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa ṉakaravoṟṟu nakaravoṟṟākum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	munnūṟu eṉa varum.	(56)
nāṉku aintu - ivaṟṟiṉ oṟṟut tiriyāmai
462.	nāṉkum aintum oṟṟumey tiriyā.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) nāṉkum aintum oṟṟu mey tiriyā-nāṉkeṉṉum eṇṇum ainteṉṉum eṇṇum tammoṟṟukkaḷ nilai tiriyātu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nāṉūṟu, ainnūṟu eṉa varum.
‘mey’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, nāṉūṟu eṉpuḻi varumoḻi nakarattuḷ ūkāram pirittu, ‘laṉa veṉa varūum’ (eḻut. 149) eṉpataṉāṉ ṉakara voṟṟākki, ūkāramēṟṟi, nilaimoḻi ṉakaraṅkeṭuttuk koḷka.	(57)
oṉpatu ‘nūṟu’ varuvaḻi muṭiyumāṟu
463.	oṉpāṉ mutaṉilai muntukiḷan taṟṟē
	muntai yoṟṟē ḷakāram iraṭṭum
	nūṟeṉ kiḷavi nakāra meykeṭa
	ūā vākum iyaṟkait teṉpa
	āyiṭai varutal ikāra rakāram
	īṟumey keṭuttu makara moṟṟum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) oṉpāṉ mutaṉilai muntu kiḷantaṟṟu - oṉpateṉṉu meṇṇiṉ mutaṉiṉṟa okaram mēṟ patteṉpataṉoṭu puṇarumvaḻik kūṟiyavāṟu pōla oru takaram oṟṟi ataṉmēl ēṟimuṭiyum, muntai oṟṟē ḷakāram iraṭṭum - avvokarattiṉ muṉṉiṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu ḷakara oṟṟāy iraṭṭittu niṟkum, nūṟeṉkiḷavi nakāra meykeṭa ū āvākum iyaṟkait teṉpa - varumoḻiyākiya nūṟeṉṉum eṇṇup peyar nakāramākiya meykeṭa ataṉmēl ēṟiya ūkāram ākāramām iyalpaiyuṭaitteṉpar pulavar, āyiṭai ikāra rakāram varutal - ammoḻiyiṭai ōr ikaramum rakaramum varuka, īṟu mey keṭuttu makaram oṟṟum - īṟākiya kuṟṟukarattiṉaiyum aḵtu ēṟiniṉṟa ṟakara oṟṟiṉaiyum keṭuttu oru makara oṟṟu vantu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
‘mey’ eṉpataṉāṉ, nilaimoḻikkaṭ pakaraṅ keṭukka.
e-ṭu:	toḷḷāyiram eṉa varum.
itaṉai oṉpateṉṉum okarattiṉ muṉṉar vanta takara oṟṟiṉ mēlē okarattaiyēṟṟip, pakaraṅ keṭuttuk kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭuttu, niṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟiṉai iraṇṭu ḷakara oṟṟākki, nūṟeṉpataṉ nakaraṅkeṭuttu, ūkāram ākāramākki ḷakarattiṉ mēlēṟṟi, ikaramum rakaramum varuvittu, vikārappaṭṭa uyirākiya ākārattiṉmuṉ uṭampaṭu mey yakāram varuvittu, ṟakara ukaraṅ keṭuttu, makara oṟṟu varuvittu muṭikka.	(58)
oṉṟu, iraṇṭu - eṇkaḷoṭu ‘āyiram’ muṭiyumāṟu
464.	āyirak kiḷavi varūuṅ kālai
	mutalī reṇṇiṉ ukaram keṭumē.
iḵtu avvoṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu āyiram muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) āyirakkiḷavi varūuṅ kālai - āyirameṉṉuñ col oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷ muṉ varuṅkālattu, mutal īreṇṇiṉ ukaram keṭum - oru iru eṉṉum iraṇṭeṇṇiṉkaṭ peṟṟu niṉṟa ukaraṅ keṭṭu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
‘ukaraṅ keṭum’ eṉavē, ēṉaiyaṉa muṉṉark kūṟiyavāṟē niṟṟal peṟṟām.
e-ṭu:	orāyiram, irāyiram eṉa varum.	(59)
avai or, īr - eṉa uyir nīṭalum ātal
465.	mutaṉilai nīṭiṉum māṉa millai.
iḵtu eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) mutaṉilai nīṭiṉum māṉamillai - ammutalīreṇṇiṉ mutaṟkaṇiṉṟa okāra ikāraṅkaḷ nīṇṭumuṭiyiṉuṅ kuṟṟamillai e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ōrāyiram, īrāyiram eṉa varum.	(60)
mūṉṟaṉ ṉakāram vakāramātal
466.	mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē vakāra mākum.
itu mūṉṟeṉṉum eṇ āyirattoṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) mūṉṟaṉoṟṟē vakāramākum - mūṉṟeṉṉum eṇṇiṉ kaṇiṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu vakara oṟṟākat tirintu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	muvvāyiram eṉa varum.
muṉṉarc cūttirattu ‘nilai’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, itaṉai mutaṉilai nīṭṭi vakara oṟṟuk keṭuttu mūvāyiram eṉavum muṭikka.	(61)
nāṉkaṉ ṉakāram lakāramātal
467.	nāṉka ṉoṟṟē lakāra mākum.
itu nāṉkeṉṉum eṇ ataṉoṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) nāṉkaṉoṟṟē lakāramākum - nāṉkeṉṉum eṇṇiṉ kaṇiṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu lakara oṟṟākat tirintu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nālāyiram eṉa varum.	(62)
aintaṉ nakāram yakāramātal
468.	ainta ṉoṟṟē yakāra mākum.
iḵtu ainteṉṉum eṇ ataṉoṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
i-ḷ: aintaṉoṟṟē yakāramākum - ainteṉṉum eṇṇiṉ kaṇiṉṟa nakara oṟṟu yakara oṟṟākat tirintu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aiyāyiram eṉa varum.	(63)
mutal kuṟuki niṉṟa ‘āṟu’ īṟṟu muṟṟukaram keṭṭu muṭital
469.	āṟaṉ maruṅkiṟ kuṟṟiya lukaram
	īṟumey oḻiyak keṭutal vēṇṭum.
iḵtu āṟeṉṉum eṇ ataṉoṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) āṟaṉ maruṅkiṟ kuṟṟiyalukaram - āṟeṉṉum eṇṇiṉ kaṇiṉṟa kuṟṟiyalukaram neṭumutal kuṟuki aṟuveṉa muṟṟukaramāy niṟṟaliṉ, mey oḻiya īṟukeṭutal vēṇṭum - atu tāṉēṟiya meyyākiya ṟakara oṟṟukkeṭātuniṟpa muṟṟukaramākiya īṟu tāṉ keṭṭup puṇartalai virumpum āciriyaṉ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aṟāyiram eṉa varum.
muṉṉar ‘neṭumutal kuṟukum’ (eḻut. 440) eṉṟavaḻi aṟuveṉa niṉṟa muṟṟukarattiṟkē īṇṭuk kēṭu kūṟiṉāreṉpatu peṟṟām. eṉṉai? kuṟṟiyalukaramāyiṉ ēṟimuṭitaliṉ. itu kuṟṟukaran tirintu muṟṟukaramāy niṟṟaliṉ īṇṭu muṭipu kūṟiṉār.
muṟṟiyalukaram ‘īṟumeyyoḻiyak keṭum’ eṉavē, kuṟṟukaraṅ keṭātu ēṟimuṭiyu meṉpatu aruttāpattiyāṟ peṟutum.
e-ṭu:	āṟāyiram eṉa varum.
‘maruṅku’ eṉṟataṉāṟ piṟa poruṭpeyarkkaṇṇum neṭumutal kuṟukātu niṉṟu muṭital koḷka.
e-ṭu:	‘āṟākuvatē’ (col. 80) eṉa varum.	(64)
‘oṉpatu’ iṉcāriyai peṟutal
470.	oṉpā ṉiṟuti uruvunilai tiriyā(tu)
	iṉpeṟal vēṇṭuñ cāriyai marapē.
iḵtu oṉpateṉṉum eṇ ataṉoṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) oṉpāṉ iṟuti - oṉpateṉṉum eṇṇiṉ iṟutik kuṟṟukaram, uruvunilai tiriyātu - taṉ vaṭivunilai tirintu keṭātē, cāriyai marapu iṉ peṟal vēṇṭum - cāriyaiyākiya marapiṉaiyuṭaiya iṉpeṟṟu muṭitalai virumpum āciriyaṉ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	oṉpatiṉāyiram eṉa varum.
‘uruvu’ eṉṟum ‘nilai’ eṉṟum ‘cāriyaimarapu’ eṉṟum kūṟiya mikaiyāṉ, āyiramallāta piṟaveṇṇiṉkaṇṇum poruṭ peyariṭat tum iṉṉum ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟu muṭiyum muṭipu koḷka.
e-ṭu:	oṉpatiṟṟuk kōṭi, oṉpatiṟṟoṉpatu, oṉpatiṟṟut taṭakkai (pari. 3 : 39), oṉpatiṟṟeḻuttu eṉa varum.
iṉṉum ivvilēcāṉē, vēṟoru muṭipiṉmaiyiṟ kūṟā toḻinta eṇṇāyiram eṉṟavaḻi oṟṟiraṭṭutalum, īṇṭuk kūṟiya vaṟṟiṟku oṟṟiraṭṭutaluṅ koḷka. ‘aḷavāku moḻi mutal’ (eḻut. 121) eṉpataṉuḷ, ‘nilaiiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramātal koḷka.	(65)
oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉ eṇkaḷoṭu ‘nūṟāyiram’ muṭiyumāṟu
471.	nūṟā yiramuṉ varūuṅ kālai
	nūṟa ṉiyaṟkai mutaṉilaik kiḷavi.
iḵtu oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷoṭu nūṟeṉṉum eṇ aṭaiyaṭutta āyiram muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) nūṟāyiram muṉ varūuṅ kālai - nūṟāyiram eṉṉum aṭaiyaṭuttamoḻi oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷ muṉ varumoḻiyāy varuṅ kālattu, mutaṉilaik kiḷavi nūṟaṉ iyaṟkai - oṉṟeṉṉum mutaṉilaikkiḷavi oṉṟumuṉ nūṟeṉṉu meṇṇoṭu muṭintāṟ pōla vikārameyti muṭiyum e-ṟu.
eṉavē vaḻinilaik kiḷaviyākiya iraṇṭu mutaliya eṇkaḷ vikārameytiyum, eytātu iyalpāyum muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	oru nūṟāyiram eṉa varum. ēṉaiyaṉa irunūṟāyiram iraṇṭunūṟāyiram, munnūṟāyiram mūṉṟunūṟāyiram, nāṉūṟāyiram nāṉkunūṟāyiram, ainnūṟāyiram aintu nūṟāyiram aṟunūṟāyiram āṟunūṟāyiram, eṇṇūṟā yiram eṭṭunūṟāyiram, oṉpatu nūṟāyiram eṉa varum.
ivvikārappaṭṭaṉavaṟṟiṟkuk kuṟṟukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭuttu, mutalīreṇṇi ṉoṟṟu rakaramākki ukaram varuvittu, mūṉṟum āṟum neṭumutal kuṟukki, mūṉṟaṉoṟṟu nakāramākki, nāṉkum aintum oṟṟu mey tiriyā vākki, eṭṭaṉoṟṟu ṇakāramākki, ilēcukaḷāṟ koṇṭa ceykaikaḷil vēṇṭuvaṉavuṅ koṇarntu muṭikka.
‘ēṟpuḻikkōṭal’ eṉpataṉāṉ, toḷḷāyirameṉṟa muṭipi ṉoṭu māṭṭēṟu ceṉṟatēṉum avvāṟu muṭiyāteṉṟu koḷka. ‘muṉ’ eṉpataṉāṉ, iṉcāriyai peṟṟu oṉpatiṉūṟāyiram eṉṟumām. ‘nilai’ eṉpataṉāṉ, mūṉṟum āṟum iyalpāka muṭivuḻi neṭumutal kuṟukāmai koḷka.	(66)
oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉ eṇṇuppeyar varuvaḻi, ‘nūṟu’ muṭiyumāṟu
472.	nūṟeṉ kiḷavi oṉṟumutal oṉpāṟku
	īṟuciṉai yoḻiya iṉavoṟṟu mikumē.
itu nūṟeṉpataṉōṭu oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷaip puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) nūṟu eṉ kiḷavi - nūṟeṉṉum eṇṇup peyar, oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṟku - oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷoṭu puṇarumiṭattu, īṟu ciṉaiyoḻiya - īṟākiya kuṟṟukaran taṉṉāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭa meyyoṭuṅ keṭātuniṟpa, iṉa oṟṟu mikum - acciṉaikku iṉamākiya ṟakara oṟṟu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nūṟṟoṉṟu eṉa varum. iraṇṭu mutal oṉpatu aḷavuñ ceykai yaṟintu oṭṭuka.
‘īṟuciṉai’ eṉṟu ōtiya mikaiyāṉ, nūṟeṉpataṉoṭu piṟa eṇṇum, piṟaporuṭpeyarum, ivvitiyum, piṟavitiyum eyti muṭital koḷka. nūṟṟuppattu, nūṟṟukkōṭi, nūṟṟut toṇṇūṟu eṉavum; nūṟṟukkuṟai, ‘nūṟṟitaḻttāmarai’ (aiṅkuṟu. 20), nūṟṟukkāṇam, nūṟṟukkāṉmaṇṭapam eṉavum iṉa oṟṟu mikkaṉa koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, irunūṟṟoṉṟu iraṇṭunūṟṟoṉṟu eṉa nūṟu aṭai yaṭutta vaḻiyuṅkoḷka.	(67)
orupaḵtu mutaliyaṉa varuvaḻiyum, ‘nūṟu’ aṟṟātal
473.	avaiyūr pattiṉum attoḻiṟ ṟākum.
iḵtu annūṟu eṉpataṉōṭu oṉṟumutal eṭṭu eṇkaḷ aṭaiyaṭuttavaḻip puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) avai ūrpattiṉum - annūṟu eṉpatu niṉṟu muṟkūṟiya oṉṟumutal eṭṭu eṇkaḷai ūrntu vanta patteṉpataṉoṭu puṇaru miṭattum, attoḻiṟṟākum - īṟu ciṉaiyoḻiya iṉa oṟṟu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nūṟṟorupaḵtu; irupaḵtu, muppaḵtu, nāṟpaḵtu, aimpaḵtu, aṟupaḵtu, eḻupaḵtu, eṇpaḵtu eṉa varum.
maṟṟu, nūṟṟoṉpatu avai ūrappaṭṭu vantatu aṉmai uṇarka. ‘ākum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, orunūṟṟorupaḵtu irunūṟṟorupaḵtu eṉa nilaimoḻi aṭaiyaṭuttu muṭiyum muṭipuṅ koḷka.	(68)
aḷavu niṟaippeyar varuvaḻiyum aṟṟātal
474.	aḷavum niṟaiyum āyiyal tiriyā
	kuṟṟiya lukaramum valleḻut tiyaṟkaiyum
	muṟkiḷan taṉṉa eṉmaṉār pulavar.
itu nūṟu eṉpataṉōṭu aḷavuppeyarum niṟaippeyarum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) aḷavum niṟaiyum āyiyal tiriyātu - nūṟeṉpataṉōṭu aḷavuppeyarum niṟaippeyarum puṇarumiṭattu muṟkūṟiya iyalpiṉ tiriyātu iṉa oṟṟu mikku muṭiyum, kuṟṟiyalukaramum valleḻut tiyaṟkaiyum - avviṭattuk kuṟṟiyalukaraṅ keṭāmaiyum iṉa oṟṟu mikku vaṉṟoṭarmoḻiyāy niṟṟaliṉ varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikum iyalpum, muṟkiḷantaṉṉa eṉmaṉār pulavar - ‘valloṟṟut toṭar moḻi valleḻuttu mikumē’ (eḻut. 426) eṉa vaṉṟoṭar moḻikkuk kūṟiya taṉmaiyavāy muṭiyumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nūṟṟukkalam, cāṭi, tūtai, pāṉai, nāḻi, maṇṭai, vaṭṭi, akal, uḻakku eṉavum; kaḻañcu, toṭi, palam eṉavum varum.
‘tiriyā’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, nūṟeṉpatu aṭaiyaṭutta vaḻiyum ivviti koḷka. aḵtu orunūṟṟukkalam irunūṟṟukkalam eṉa varum.	(69)
orupaḵtu mutaliya eṇkaḷ,
oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉoṭu puṇartal
475.	oṉṟumuta lākiya pattūr kiḷavi
	oṉṟumuta loṉpāṟku oṟṟiṭai mikumē
	niṉṟa āytaṅ keṭutal vēṇṭum.
iḵtu oṉṟu mutal eṭṭu īṟākiya eṇkaḷ aṭaiyaṭutta pattaṉōṭu oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷaip puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) oṉṟu mutalākiya pattu ūr kiḷavi - oṉṟu mutal eṭṭu īṟākap patteṉṉum eṇ ēṟi orucollāki niṉṟa orupaḵtu mutaliya eṇkaḷ, oṉṟumutal oṉpāṟku - oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷ varu moḻiyāy vantu puṇarum iṭattu, niṉṟa āytaṅ keṭutal vēṇṭum-paḵteṉpataṉkaṇ niṉṟa āytaṅ keṭṭu muṭitalai virumpum āciriyaṉ, oṟṟu iṭaimikum - āṇṭu iṉa oṟṟākiya oru takara oṟṟu iṭaimikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	orupattoṉṟu, irupattoṉṟu, orupattiraṇṭu, irupat tiraṇṭu eṉa ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka.
ivaṟṟuḷ orupattoṉṟu orupattiraṇṭu eṉṉum eṇkaḷ atikārattāl niṉṟa nūṟeṉpataṉōṭu aṭuttu varumeṉṟu uṇarka.	(70)
‘āyiram’ varuvaḻi, avveṇkaḷ ‘iṉ’ peṟutal
476.	āyiram variṉē iṉṉeṉ cāriyai
	āvayi ṉoṟṟiṭai mikuta lillai.
iḵtu orupaḵtu mutaliyavaṟṟōṭu āyirattaip puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) āyiram variṉ iṉṉeṉ cāriyai - avvoṉṟu mutalākiya pattu ūr kiḷavi āyirattoṭu puṇarum iṭattu iṉ cāriyai peṟum, āvayiṉ oṟṟu iṭai mikutal illai - avviṭattut takara oṟṟu iṭai vantu mikātu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	orupatiṉāyiram irupatiṉāyiram eṉa eṇpatiṉkāṟum oṭṭuka. ivai nūṟṟorupatiṉāyiram eṉavum varum.
‘āvayiṉ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, nūṟāyirattorupattīrāyiram eṉṟāṟ pōla attup peṟutalum piṟavuṅ koḷka.	(71)
aḷavum niṟaiyum varuvaḻiyum ‘iṉ’ tōṉṟutal
477.	aḷavum niṟaiyum āyiyal tiriyā.
iḵtu oṉṟu mutalākiya pattu ūr kiḷavi muṉṉar aḷavup peyarum niṟaippeyarum puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) aḷavum niṟaiyum āyiyal tiriyā - orupaḵtu mutaliya eṇkaḷiṉ muṉṉar aḷavuppeyarum niṟaippeyarum vantāl oṟṟu iṭaimikātu iṉcāriyai peṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	orupatiṉkalam, cāṭi, tūtai, pāṉai, nāḻi, maṇṭai, vaṭṭi, akal, uḻakku eṉavum; orupatiṉkaḻañcu, toṭi, palam eṉavum varum. ivaṟṟiṟku nūṟu aṭaiyaṭuttu oṭṭuka.
‘tiriyā’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, orupatiṟṟukkalam irupatiṟṟukkalam eṉṉun toṭakkattaṉavaṟṟiṉkaṇ iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramākat tirintu iraṭṭu talum, ukaramum valleḻuttup peṟutaluṅ koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, orupatiṉāḻi eṉṟavaḻi varumoḻi nakaran tirintuḻi nilaimoḻi ṉakarakkēṭuṅ koḷka.
‘aḷavum niṟaiyum ataṉō raṉṉa’ eṉṟu pāṭam ōtuvār, muṉṉarc cūttirattu ‘āvayiṉ’ eṉṟataṉāṉum, ataṉ muṉṉarc cūttirattu ‘niṉṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉum ivaṟṟai muṭippār.	(72)
vaṉkaṇam meṉkaṇam iṭaikkaṇam varuvaḻi,
oṉṟu ‘oru’ ātal
478.	mutaṉilai eṇṇiṉmuṉ valleḻuttu variṉum
	ñanamat tōṉṟiṉum yavavan tiyaiyiṉum
	mutaṉilai yiyaṟkai eṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷoṭu poruṭ peyaraip puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) mutaṉilai eṇṇiṉmuṉ valleḻuttu variṉum - oṉṟeṉṉum eṇṇiṉmuṉ valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi variṉum, ñanama tōṉṟiṉum - ñanamakkaḷākiya melleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻivariṉum, yava vantu iyaiyiṉum - yavakkaḷākiya iṭaiyeḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi variṉum, mutaṉilai iyaṟkai eṉmaṉār pulavar - avvoṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷ muṉṉeytiya muṭipu nilaimai eyti muṭiyumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar e-ṟu.
eṉavē, vaḻinilaiyeṇṇākiya iraṇṭu mutalākiya eṇkaḷ ammūṉṟu kaṇamum mutaṉmoḻiyāyvariṉ, mutaṉilai muṭipāki vikāram eytiyum, eytātu iyalpāyum muṭiyum.
e-ṭu:	orukal cuṉai tuṭi paṟai ñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu eṉavum; irukal, iraṇṭukal cuṉai tuṭi paṟai ñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu eṉavum oṭṭuka.
ivveṇkaḷiṟ kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭṭu, mutalīreṇṇiṉ oṟṟu rakāramāka ukaram vantatu. irukal mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku iṭainilai rakāraṅ keṭukka. mukkal mūṉṟukal; cuṉai tuṭi paṟai ñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu eṉa oṭṭuka. itaṟku neṭu mutal kuṟukki ‘mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē vanta tokkum’ (eḻut. 447) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭikka.
muṉṉar, eṇṇuppeyarum aḷavuppeyarum niṟaippeyarum varuvaḻikkūṟiya vikāraṅkaḷiṟ, poruṭpeyarkkum ēṟpaṉa koṇarntu muṭittu, ellāvaṟṟiṟkum ‘nilai’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, oṟṟuttirittu muṭikka. avai, mūṉṟaṟkum aintaṟkum ñakaram varuvaḻi ñakara oṟṟātalum, mūṉṟaṟku yakaram varuvaḻi vakara oṟṟātalumām.
e-ṭu:	nāṟkal nāṉkukal cuṉai tuṭi paṟai; nāṉñāṇ nāṉkuñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu; aiṅkal aintukal cuṉai tuṭi paṟai; aiññāṇ aintuñāṇ nūl maṇi; aiyāḻ aintuyāḻ aivaṭṭu aintuvaṭṭu; aṟukal āṟukal cuṉai tuṭi paṟai ñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu; eṇkal eṭṭukkal cuṉai tuṭi paṟai; eṇñāṇ eṭṭuñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu; oṉpatukal cuṉai tuṭi paṟai ñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu eṉa oṭṭuka. oṉpatiṉkal eṉac ceṉṟatēṉum vaḻakkiṉmaiyiṉ oḻikka.
iṉṉum māṭṭēṟiṉṟi varuvaṉavaṟṟiṟ kellām muṭipu ‘nilai’ yeṉṟataṉāṉ muṭikka.	(73)
uyirkkaṇamum yāvum varuvaḻi, aḵtu ‘ōr’ ātal
479.	ataṉilai uyirkkum yāvaru kālai
	mutaṉilai okaram ōvā kummē
	rakarat tukaran tuvarak keṭumē.
iḵtu oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu, poruṭpeyaruḷ uyir mutalmoḻi muṭiyumāṟum, mēṟkūṟiya yakāram vēṟupaṭa muṭiyumāṟum kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) mutaṉilaikku - oṉṟeṉṉum eṇṇiṉ tiripākiya oru eṉṉum eṇṇiṟku, uyirum yāvum varukālai - uyir mutaṉmoḻiyum yāmutaṉmoḻiyum varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, ataṉ nilai - ammutaṉilaiyiṉ taṉmai ivvāṟām; okaram ōvākum - okaram ōkāramāy nīḷum; rakarattu ukaran tuvarak keṭum - rakarattu mēṉiṉṟa ukaram muṟṟak keṭṭu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
nāṉkāvataṉai mutaṉilaiyōṭu kūṭṭi, ataṉkaṇ niṉṟa ummaiyai uyiroṭum yāvoṭuṅ kūṭṭuka. eṉavē vaḻinilaiyeṇkaḷ uyir mutaṉmoḻi vanta iṭattu muṟkūṟiyavāṟē iruvāṟṟāṉum muṭiyum.
e-ṭu:	ōraṭai, ōrākam, ōrilai, ōrīṭṭam, ōrulai, ōrūcal, ōreḻu, ōrēṭu, ōraiyam, ōroḻuṅku, ōrōlai ōrauviyam eṉa varum.
kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭuttu, mutaleṇṇiṉoṟṟu rakaramākkuka. ōryāḻ, ōryāṉai eṉa varum.
‘tuvara’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, iraṇṭeṉṉum eṇṇiṉ ikarattai nīṭṭi, rakarattuḷ ukarattaik keṭuttu, īracai īryāṉai eṉavum, mūṉṟeṉṉum eṇṇiṉ ṉakaravoṟṟuk keṭuttu, mūvacai mūyāṉai eṉavum muṭikka. ivai ceyyuṇ muṭipu. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, iṅṅaṉam varuvaṉa piṟavum aṟintu muṭittuk koḷka.	(74)
‘mā’ varuvaḻi, iraṇṭu mutal oṉpāṉ eṇkaḷ tiriyumāṟu
480.	iraṇṭumuta loṉpāṉ iṟuti muṉṉar
	vaḻaṅkiyal māveṉ kiḷavi tōṉṟiṉ
	makara aḷavoṭu nikaralu murittē.
iḵtu iraṇṭu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷiṉ muṉṉar aḷavu mutaliya mūṉṟaṟkum uriya māveṉpatu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) iraṇṭu mutal oṉpāṉ  iṟuti muṉṉar - iraṇṭeṉṉu meṇ mutalāka oṉpateṉṉumeṇ īṟāka niṉṟa eṇṇup peyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉar, vaḻaṅku iyal mā eṉ kiḷavi tōṉṟiṉ - vaḻakkiṉkaṇṇē naṭanta aḷavu mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku uriya mā veṉṉuñ col varumoḻiyāy variṉ, makara aḷavoṭu nikaralum urittu - avveṇṇup peyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉart tantu puṇarkkappaṭum maṇṭaiyeṉṉum aḷavup peyarōṭu ottu vikārappaṭṭu muṭitalum urittu; ummaiyāṉ vikārappaṭātu iyalpāy muṭitalum urittu e-ṟu.
‘vaḻakkiyal’ vaḻaṅkiyaleṉa vikāram. ‘makaraaḷavu’ makara mutaṉ moḻiyākiya aḷavup peyareṉap paṇputtokai. aḵtu ‘aḷantaṟi kiḷaviyum’ (eḻut. 446) eṉpataṉuḷ, oru maṇṭai eṉa muṭittatām.
e-ṭu:	irumā, mummā, nāṉmā, aimmā, aṟumā, eṇmā, oṉpatiṉmā eṉa muṉṉark kūṟiya cūttiraṅkaḷāṉ vikārap paṭutti muṭikka.
iṉi, ummaiyāṉ vikārappaṭuttātu iraṇṭumā mūṉṟumā nāṉkumā aintumā āṟumā eṭṭumā oṉpatumā eṉavum muṭikka.
puḷḷimayaṅkiyaluḷ ‘aḷavu niṟaiyum’ (eḻut. 389) eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṉ, ēḻneṭumutal kuṟuki ukaram vantu puṇarumāṟu kūṟiṉār. ataṉāṉ, īṇṭu eḻumā eṉa muṭikka. ēḻmāveṉa muṭital vaḻakkiṉṟu.
‘iraṇṭumutal oṉpāṉ’ eṉṟu eṭuttōtiṉamaiyiṉ, oṉṟaṟku orumāveṉṉum muṭipēyaṉṟi, oṉṟumā veṉṉum muṭipu illai yāyiṟṟu.
‘vaḻaṅkiyaṉmā’ eṉṟār, vilaṅku māvai nīkkutaṟku.	(75)
puḷḷimayaṅkiyaluḷ eñci niṉṟa ceyyuḷmuṭipu
481.	laṉaveṉa varūum puḷḷi yiṟutimuṉ
	ummuṅ keḻuvum uḷappaṭap piṟavum
	aṉṉa marapiṉ moḻiyiṭait tōṉṟic
	ceyyuḷ toṭarvayiṉ meypeṟa nilaiyum
	vēṟṟumai kuṟitta poruḷvayi ṉāṉa.
itu puḷḷimayaṅkiyaluḷ oḻintu niṉṟa ceyyuṇmuṭipu kūṟu kiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) laṉa eṉa varūum puḷḷiyiṟuti muṉ - lakāra ṉakāra meṉṟu kūṟa varukiṉṟa puḷḷi īṟṟuc coṟkaḷiṉ muṉṉar, ummuṅ keḻuvum uḷappaṭa - ummeṉṉuñ cāriyaiyum keḻuveṉṉum cāriyaiyum uṭpaṭa, piṟavum aṉṉa marapiṉ moḻiyiṭait tōṉṟi - piṟacāriyaiyum appeṟṟippaṭṭa marapiṉaiyuṭaiya moḻiyiṭattē tōṉṟi, ceyyuḷ toṭarvayiṉ mey peṟa nilaiyum - ceyyuṭ coṟkaḷait toṭarpu paṭuttik kūṟumiṭattup poruḷpaṭa niṟkum, vēṟṟumai kuṟitta poruḷ vayiṉāṉa - vēṟṟumaiyaik kuṟitta poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	‘vāṉavari villun tiṅkaḷum pōlum’. itaṟku ummeṉṉuñ cāriyaiyiṉ makarattai ‘ammiṉiṟuti’ (eḻut. 129) eṉṉuñ cūttirattuḷ ‘taṉmey eṉṟataṉāṟ piṟa cāriyaiyun tiriyumeṉa nakara oṟṟākki nilaimoḻi lakara oṟṟiṉ mēl uyirēṟṟi muṭikka. villun tiṅkaḷum pōlumeṉpataṟku, villiṭaittiṅkaḷ pōlumeṉa ēḻaṉurupu virittup poruḷuraikka. ‘kalkeḻu kāṉavar nalkuṟu makaḷē’ (kuṟun. 71); itaṟkuk kallaik keḻīiyiṉa eṉa uraikka. ‘mānitikkiḻavaṉum pōṉm’ (akam. 66); itaṟkuk kiḻavaṉaip pōṉmeṉa uraikka. ivvummai ciṟap paṉṟu.‘kāṉkeḻunāṭaṉ’ (akam. 98) itaṟkuk kāṉaikkeḻīiya eṉa uraikka.
iṉi ‘aṉṉamarapiṉ moḻiyiṭai’ eṉṟataṉāṟ, ‘keḻu’ eṉṟatu piṟa colliṭattē ‘paṇaikeḻu peruntiṟaṟ palvēṉ maṉṉar’ (maturaik. 234), ‘tuṟaikeḻu māntai’ (naṟ. 35) eṉa iyalpāka varuvaṉavum; ‘vaḷaṅkeḻu tirunakar’ (akam. 17) ‘payaṅkeḻu māmaḻai’ (puṟam. 266) eṉa nilaimoḻi yīṟṟeḻuttut tiriya varuvaṉavuṅ koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, iccāriyaiyatu ukarakkēṭum ekara nīṭciyuṅ koḷka. ‘pūkkēḻ toṭalai nuṭaṅka veḻuntu’ (akam. 28) ‘tuṟai kēḻūraṉ koṭumai nāṇi’ (aiṅkuṟu. 11) ivaṟṟiṟku, iraṇṭāvatum ēṟpuḻi mūṉṟā vatum virikka. ‘ceṅkēḻ meṉkoṭi’ (akam. 80) eṉpuḻik keḻu veṉṉum uriccol ‘eḻuttup pirinticaittal’ (col. 395) eṉpataṉāṉ nīṇṭateṉṟu uṇarka. ‘mey’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ‘pūkkēḻeṉ’ (naṟ. 10) puḻi valloṟṟu mikutal koḷka. iṉṉuñ cāṉṟōr cey yuṭkaṭ piṟacāriyai peṟṟu vikāraṅkaḷ eyti muṭivaṉavaṟṟiṟkellām iccūttiramē vitiyāka muṭittuk koḷka.	(76)
ivvatikārattup puṇarkkappaṭāta coṟkaḷ ivai eṉal
482.	uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟuti yākik
	kuṟippiṉum paṇpiṉum icaiyiṉum tōṉṟi
	neṟippaṭa vārāk kuṟaiccoṟ kiḷaviyum
	uyartiṇai aḵṟiṇai āyiru maruṅkiṉ
	aimpā laṟiyum paṇputoku moḻiyum
	ceyyuñ ceyta eṉṉuṅ kiḷaviyiṉ
	meyyoruṅ kiyalun toḻiltoku moḻiyum
	tammiyal kiḷappiṉ tammuṉ tāmvarūum
	eṇṇiṉ tokuti uḷappaṭap piṟavum
	aṉṉavai yellām maruviṉ pāttiya
	puṇariyal nilaiyiṭai uṇarat tōṉṟā.
iḵtu ivvatikārattāṟ puṇarkkappaṭāta coṟkaḷ ivaiyeṉa avaṟṟai eṭuttu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟutiyāki - kūṟuṅkāl uyirum puḷḷiyum īṟāka niṟkum collāki, kuṟippiṉum paṇpiṉum icaiyiṉum tōṉṟi - kuṟippiṉāṉum paṇpiṉāṉum icaiyiṉāṉum piṟantu, neṟip paṭa vārāk kuṟaic coṟkiḷaviyum - oruvaḻippaṭa vārāta coṟṟaṉmai kuṟainta coṟkaḷākiya uriccoṟkaḷum, uyartiṇai aḵṟiṇai āyiru maruṅkiṉ - uyartiṇai aḵṟiṇaiyeṉṉum avviraṇṭiṭattum uḷavākiya, aimpālaṟiyum paṇputoku moḻiyum - oruvaṉ orutti palar oṉṟu pala eṉṉum aintu pāliṉaiyum aṟitaṟkuk kāraṇamākiya paṇpu koḷpeyar tokun  tokaiccollum, ceyyuñ ceyta eṉṉum kiḷaviyiṉ - ceyyum ceyta eṉṉum peyareccac coṟkaḷiṉuṭaiya, mey oruṅku iyalun toḻil tokumoḻiyum - kālaṅkāṭṭum ummum akaramum oru coṟkaṇṇē cēra naṭakkum puṭaipeyarcci tokku niṟkuñ coṟkaḷum, tam iyal kiḷappiṉ-tamatu taṉmai kūṟumiṭattu, tammuṉ tāmvarūum eṇṇiṉ tokuti uḷappaṭa - niṟutta colluṅ kuṟittuvarukiḷaviyumāy vārātu tammuṉṉart tāmē vantu niṟkum eṇṇup peyariṉatu tokutiyum uḷappaṭa, aṉṉa piṟavum ellām - attaṉmaiyākiya piṟavumellām, maruviṉ pāttiya -  ulakattu maruvi naṭanta vaḻakkiṉatu pakutiyait tam ilakkaṇamākavuṭaiya, puṇariyal nilai iṭai yuṇarat tōṉṟā - oṉṟaṉōṭoṉṟu puṇarutal naṭanta taṉmai iṭam viḷaṅkat tōṉṟā e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kaṇ viṇṇa viṇaittatu, viṇviṇaittatu ivai kuṟippuriccol; āṭai veḷḷaviḷarttatu, veḷviḷart tatu ivai paṇpuriccol; kaṭal olla volittatu, ollolittatu ivai icaiyuriccol. ‘ollolinīr pāyvatē pōlun tuṟaivaṉ’ eṉṟār ceyyuṭkaṇṇum.
ivai uyirīṟāyum puḷḷiyīṟāyum niṟṟaliṉ oṉṟaṉkaṇ aṭakka lākāmaiyiṉ ‘neṟippaṭavārā’ eṉṟār. viṇṇaviṇaittatu teṟipputtōṉṟat teṟittateṉṟum, viṇviṇaittatu teṟipput teṟittateṉṟum ām. ēṉaiyavaṟṟiṟkum ivvāṟē uṇarka. iṅṅaṉam niṟṟaliṉ taṉmai kuṟainta collāyiṟṟu. ‘viṉaiyē kuṟippē’ (col. 258) eṉṉuñ cūttirattiṟ kūṟiya ‘eṉa’ eṉpataṉai ivaṟṟoṭu kūṭṭiyavaḻi iṭaiccollātaliṉ, viṇṇeṉa viṇaittatu eṉap puṇarkkappaṭu māṟu uṇarka.
iṉik karumpārppāṉ, karumpārppaṉi, karumpārppār, karuṅ kutirai, karuṅkutiraikaḷ eṉa varum. ivaṟṟuṭ kariyaṉākiya pārppāṉ, kariyaḷākiya pārppaṉi, kariyarākiya pārppār, kariyatākiya kutirai, kariyaṉavākiya kutiraikaḷ eṉa aimpāliṉaiyum uṇarttum paṇpu koḷpeyar tokkavāṟu kāṇka. ivaṟṟuṭ karumai eṉṉum paṇpup peyar tokkatēṟ karumaiyākiya pārppāṉeṉa virittal vēṇṭum; aṅṅaṉam viriyāmaiyiṟ paṇpukoḷpeyar tokkateṉṟu uṇarka.
veṟṟilai, veṟṟuppili, veṟṟaṭi, veṟṟeṉattoṭuttal eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉavaṟṟuḷ, veṟuvitākiyailai yeṉpatu, pākkuṅ kōṭṭunūṟuṅ kūṭātatāya paṇpuṇarttiya īṟu tokutaliṉ maruviṉ pāttiyatāy niṉṟu oṟṟaṭuttatu. veṟuvitākiya uppili yeṉṟatu, ciṟitum uppiliyeṉa niṉṟatu. ēṉaiyavum aṉṉa. iṅṅaṉam aimpālun tokuttaṟku uriya mutaṉilaiyātaliṟ puṇarttalākāmai kūṟiṉār.
iṉi, āṭaraṅku, ceykuṉṟu, puṇarpoḻutu, arivāḷ, kol yāṉai, celcelavu eṉa, nilam mutalākiya peyareccan tokka viṉait tokaikaḷai virikkuṅkāl, āṭiṉavaraṅku eṉac ‘ceyta’ eṉṉum peyareccattu īṟu virinta akara īṟu iṟappu uṇarttiyum, āṭā niṉṟavaraṅku, āṭumaraṅku eṉac ‘ceyyum’ eṉṉum peyareccattu īṟu virinta ummīṟu nikaḻvum etirvum uṇarttiyum, avaṟṟāṉāya puṭai peyarcciyait tōṟṟuvittu iraṇṭu peyareccamum oru coṟkaṇ oruṅku tokku niṟṟaliṉ, ataṉai oru peyareccattiṉkaṇ aṭakkip puṇarkkalākāmaiyiṟ, puṇarkkalākāteṉṟār.
ummiṟuti nikaḻvum etirvum uṇarttumāṟu, ‘viṉaiyiṉ tokuti’ (col. 415) eṉṉum eccaviyaṟ cūttirattuṭ kūṟutum. iv um īṟu iraṇṭu kālamum oruṅkuṇarttutaṟ ciṟappu nōkkic ‘ceyta’ eṉpataṉai āciriyar muṟkūṟārāyiṉar.
iṉip pattu eṉa niṟuttip, patteṉat tantu puṇarkkappaṭātu, pappatteṉavum, paḵpatteṉavum vaḻaṅkumāṟu uṇarka. orōvoṉ ṟeṉpatum atu. atutāṉē ōroṉṟōroṉṟākak koṭu eṉṟāṟ puṇarkkappaṭum.
iṉi ‘aṉṉapiṟavum’ eṉṟataṉāṉē, uṇṭāṉ eṉpuḻi uṇ eṉṉum mutaṉilaiyuṅ, kālaṅkāṭṭum ṭakaramum, iṭaṉum pālum uṇarttum āṉum oṉṟaṉōṭoṉṟu puṇarkkappaṭā, avai niṟuttacolluṅ kuṟittuvarukiḷaviyum aṉmaiyiṉ. kariyaṉ eṉpuḻik, karu eṉa niṟutti aṉ eṉat tantu puṇarkkappaṭātu, aḵtu iṉṉa ṉeṉṉum poruḷ tarutaliṉ. ēṉai viṉaiccoṟkaḷum ivvāṟē pirittup puṇarkkalākāmai uṇarka. iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, koḷḷeṉak koṇṭāṉ eṉpuḻik koḷ eṉpataṉai eṉa eṉpataṉoṭu puṇarkkap paṭāmaiyum, ūraṉ veṟpaṉ mutaliya viṉaippeyarkaḷum, piṟavum puṇarkkappaṭāmaiyum koḷka.
ivvāciriyar puṇarkkappaṭā veṉṟa ic coṟkaḷaiyum, vaṭanūṟkaṇ muṭitta aṉakaṉ aṉapāyaṉ akaḷaṅkaṉ mutaliya vaṭacoṟ kaḷaiyum piṉṉuḷḷōr muṭittal mutaṉūloṭu ‘māṟukoḷak kūṟalām’ eṉṟu uṇarka.	(77)
atikārap puṟaṉaṭai
483.	kiḷanta alla ceyyuḷuḷ tirinavum
	vaḻaṅkiyal maruṅkiṉ maruvoṭu tirinavum
	viḷampiya iyaṟkaiyiṉ vēṟupaṭat tōṉṟiṉ
	vaḻaṅkiyal maruṅki ṉuṇarntaṉar oḻukkal
	naṉmati nāṭṭat teṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu ivvatikārattu eṭuttōttāṉum ilēcāṉum muṭiyātu niṉṟavaṟṟai yellām itaṉāṉē muṭikka eṉa atikārap puṟaṉaṭai kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) kiḷanta alla ceyyuḷuḷ tirinavum - muṉṉar eṭuttōtap paṭṭaṉa allāta coṟkaḷ ceyyuḷiṭattut tirintu muṭivaṉavum, vaḻaṅkiyal maruṅkiṉ maruvoṭu tirinavum-nālvakai vaḻakkum naṭakku miṭattu maruvutaloṭu tirintu muṭivaṉavum, viḷampiya iyaṟkaiyiṉ vēṟupaṭat tōṉṟiṉ-muṉṉark kūṟiya ilakkaṇa muṟaimaiyiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭat tōṉṟumāyiṉ avaṟṟai, naṉmati nāṭṭattu-nalla aṟi viṉatu ārāycciyāṉē, vaḻaṅkiyaṉ maruṅkiṉ-vaḻakku muṭintu naṭakku miṭattē, uṇarntaṉar oḻukkal eṉmaṉār pulavar-muṭipu vēṟu pāṭukaḷai aṟintu naṭāttuka eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	‘taṭavuttirai’ eṉa ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟum ‘taṭavunilai’ (puṟam. 140) eṉa ukaram peṟṟum akara īṟṟu uriccol vantatu. atavattaṅkaṉi eṉa vēṟṟumaikkaṇ akara īṟu attup peṟṟatu. ‘kacatapat tōṉṟiṉ’ eṉa akara īṟṟiṉ muṉṉart takaraṅ koṭukka.
‘naṟavaṅ kaṇṇi naṟpōrc cempiyaṉ
kurava nīṭiya koṉṟaiyaṅ kāṉal’
eṉa ākāra iṟuti kuṟiyataṉiṟutic ciṉaikeṭṭu iruvaḻiyum ammup peṟṟaṉa.
‘muḷavumāt tolaicciya painniṇap piḷavaip
piṇavunāy muṭukkiya taṭiyoṭu viraii’	(malaipaṭu. 176)
eṉa avvīṟu alvaḻikkaṇ ammup peṟāta muṭipupeṟṟaṉa. ‘tiṇvār vicitta muḻavo ṭākuḷi’ (malaipaṭu. 3), ‘cuṟaveṟi mīṉ’, ‘iravaḻaṅku ciṟuneṟi’ (akam. 318) ivai ukaram peṟāmal vantaṉa. ‘kaḷḷiyaṅ kāṭṭa puḷḷiyam poṟikkalai’ (akam. 97) eṉa ikaraīṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ ammup peṟṟaṉa. ‘tīyiṉaṉṉa voṇceṅ kāntaḷ’ (malaipaṭu. 145) eṉa īkāra īṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ iṉ peṟṟatu. ‘nalloḻukkaṅ kākkun tiruvattavar’ (nālaṭi. 457) eṉa ukara īṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ attuppeṟṟatu. ‘ēppeṟṟa māṉpiṇai pōl’ (cīvaka. 2945) eṉa ēkāra īṟu vēṟ ṟumaikkaṇ ekaram peṟātu vantatu. ‘kaittuṇṭām pōḻtē’ (nālaṭi. 19), ‘kaittillār nallar’ (nāṉmaṇik. 69) eṉavum, ‘puṉṉaiyaṅkāṉal’ (akam. 80), ‘mullaiyan toṭaiyal’ eṉavum, aikāra īṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ attum ammum peṟṟaṉa. ‘aṇṇal kōyil vaṇṇamē’ (cīvaka. 126) eṉa ōkāra īṟu yakara uṭampaṭumey peṟṟatu.
iṉi ‘añcevi niṟaiya vāliṉa’ (mullaip. 89) eṉa alvaḻikkaṭ kakaramum akaramuṅkeṭṭaṉa. ‘maravam pāvai vayiṟārap paruki’ ‘maravanākam vaṇaṅki māṟkaṇam’ eṉa iruvaḻiyum makaram vikārappaṭṭu ammup peṟṟaṉa. ‘kāretir kāṉam pāṭiṉēmāka’ (puṟam. 144), ‘poṉṉan tikiri muṉcamat turuṭṭi’ (puṟam. 368) ‘poṉṉaṅ kuvaṭṭiṟ poliveyti’ eṉa ṉakara īṟu iruvaḻiyum ammuppeṟṟaṉa. ‘vērpiṇi vetirattuk kālporu naralicai’ (naṟ. 62) eṉa rakara īṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ attup peṟṟatu. ‘nāvalan taṇpoḻil’ (perumpāṇ. 465), ‘kāṉalam peruntuṟai’ (aiṅkuṟu. 158) eṉa lakara īṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ ammup peṟṟaṉa. ‘neytalañ ciṟupaṟai’ iḵtu alvaḻikkaṇ ammup peṟṟatu. ‘āyiṭai yirupē rāṇmai ceyta pūcal’ (kuṟun. 43) eṉpuḻi āyiṭai yeṉpatu urupātaliṉ ‘nīṭavarutal’ (eḻut. 208) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭiyātu nīṇṭu vakara oṟṟu vēṟupaṭa muṭintatu. temmuṉai eṉat tevveṉpuḻi vakaraṅ keṭṭu makara oṟṟup peṟṟu muṭintatu. ‘a’ eṉṉuñ cuṭṭu ‘aṉṟi yaṉaittum’ eṉat tirintatu. ‘mutirkōṅkiṉ mukai’ (kuṟiñcikkali. 20) eṉavum, ‘kāymāṇṭa teṅkiṉ paḻam’ (cīvaka. 31) eṉavum, kuṟṟukara īṟu iṉ peṟutaluṅ koḷka. ‘toṇṭutalaiyiṭṭa pattukkuṟai’ (tol. poruḷ. 413) eṉa ṇakaram iraṭṭātu takara oṟṟu ṭakara oṟṟāyk kuṟṟiyalukaram ēṟi muṭintatu.
iṅṅaṉañ ceyyuḷuṭ piṟavum tirivaṉa uḷavēṉum ip puṟaṉaṭaiyāṉ muṭikka.
arumaruntāṉeṉpatu rakaravukaraṅ keṭṭu arumantāṉeṉa muṭintatu. cōḻaṉāṭu cōṇāṭu eṉa aṉ keṭṭu muṭintatu. pāṇṭi nāṭum atu. toṇṭaimāṉāṭu toṇṭai nāṭu eṉa īṟṟeḻuttuc cila keṭṭu muṭintatu. malaiyamāṉāṭu malāṭu eṉa mutaleḻuttoḻintaṉa palavuṅ keṭṭu muṭintatu. potuvil eṉpatu potiyileṉa ukaran tirintu ikaramāy yakara uṭampaṭumey peṟṟu muṭintatu. piṟavum ivvāṟē tirintu maruvi vaḻaṅkuvaṉa ellām ippuṟaṉaṭaiyāṉ amaittuk koḷka.	(78)
kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal muṟṟiṟṟu.
eḻuttatikāram nacciṉārkkiṉiyarurai muṟṟiṟṟu.

eḻuttatikāra iyalkaḷiṉ amaippu
‘nūliṉ marapu moḻimarapu nuṇpiṟappu
mēlaip puṇarcci tokaimarapu - pālām
urupiyaliṉ piṉṉuyir puḷḷi mayakkam
terivariya kuṟṟukarañ ceppu.’

eḻuttatikāra nūṟpākkaḷiṉ tokai
‘eḻuttati kārattuc cūttiraṅkaḷ ellām
oḻukkiya oṉpatōt tuḷḷum - vaḻukkiṉṟi
nāṉūṟ ṟirunāṟpāṉ mūṉṟeṉṟu nāvalarkaḷ
mēṉūṟṟu vaittār virittu.’
